Chapter 463: The Peak Crossing Is Too Dangerous

The feather in his hand appeared dull and lifeless because it had lost the spiritual energy that Qiu Feng had infused into it. It neither emitted heat nor possessed the demonic power that had made Ji Yuan’s heart tremble. One could easily believe that it was merely a wild bird feather that had been painted over.

But this seemingly lifeless feather still filled Ji Yuan with lingering unease. He slowly stretched out a hand, then took a deep breath and channeled his spiritual energy and power into the feather.

A faint warmth reappeared, and the color of the feather brightened to a golden-red with a faint luster. Apart from that, it seemed unremarkable. This little bit of warmth was not even enough to keep warm in winter; it was less useful than a charcoal brazier.

But this feather, which appeared to have “no demonic energy” to others, felt extremely “hot” to Ji Yuan at this moment, as if he could barely hold onto it. A terrifying demonic aura emanated from it, and had Ji Yuan had not possessed extraordinary self-control he would have instinctively reacted violently.

After experiencing it for the span of only three breaths, Ji Yuan immediately dissipated the spiritual energy in the feather.

“Hooh…”

With a soft sigh, Ji Yuan tucked the feather into his sleeve. At that moment, he even felt a slight weight in his sleeve from the light feather.

Qiu Feng was looking at the spiritual coins in the stall owner’s hand, while the latter was carefully examining the two spiritual coins with a technique. Ju Yuanzi, on the other hand, had been keeping an eye on Ji Yuan, and he had noticed the change in Ji Yuan’s previously relaxed expression.

Ji Yuan had always given Ju Yuanzi the impression of being calm and collected; it was rare to see such a serious expression on his face.

‘So, there’s a big problem with that feather?’

This was the conclusion Ju Yuanzi had reached, but this wasn’t the place to talk about it. He planned to ask Ji Yuan about it in private, and if it was convenient, Ji Yuan would probably tell him.

Once Ji Yuan had put the feather away, Ju Yuanzi’s attention naturally shifted to the spiritual coins in the stall owner’s hand.

Qiu Feng watched as the stall owner, a cultivator, beamed in uncontrollable joy. Although he knew he could just ask Ji Yuan later, he couldn’t resist asking the stall owner out of curiosity.

“Fellow Taoist, you’ve only just received these two spiritual coins, and Mr. Ji hasn’t explained their uses in detail. Why are you so happy? Could it be that you already understand them?”

This person didn’t seem to have a high level of cultivation. He hadn’t even reached the boundary of the Returning to Origin1 Realm, so he’s not qualified to be called a master. In terms of age, he might not be older than Qiu Fengda either.

“Hahaha, fellow Taoist, it seems you haven’t come into contact with these spiritual coins before either. Hmm, the term ‘spiritual coins’ is quite apt!”

The old cultivator tucked one of the coins in a small pocket on his chest, leaving the other one in his palm as he rubbed it.

“As the name suggests, this spiritual coin is a coin imbued with spiritual energy. Money is a mortal concept, used to exchange for goods, while spiritual energy is the wonder of cultivation, used to display supernatural powers. I can tell from a cursory examination that this coin contains pure spiritual power, which can change according to my thoughts. It has endless wondrous uses. By relying on its spiritual energy, I can turn the tide of battle. Plus, Qi deviation when cultivation goes astray is dangerous enough; even ignoring all its other uses, as long as my mind is still clear, I can use the spiritual coin to exchange for a pure spiritual technique to exorcise evil!”

Upon hearing this, Ji Yuan glanced at the old cultivator. His cultivation level wasn’t particularly outstanding, but his insight was quite good.

“It seems that I don’t need to say more, fellow Taoist already knows the wonders of the spiritual coin. I will just remind you one more thing: the spiritual coin is very effective, and multiple can be used in tandem.”

The old cultivator was slightly taken aback by these words. He then immediately took out the other magic coin, placed it in his palm, closed his eyes to feel it carefully, and then opened his eyes to look at Ji Yuan.

“Tao… no, senior! Could you spare some more of these spiritual coins? You can buy any of the spiritual items on this stall that you like with your spiritual coins.”

Ji Yuan waved his hand. Refining magic coins wasn’t that difficult, but refining them to their current perfect state wasn’t easy either. What was the point of exchanging something useless for some other useless trinket? As for purchasing spirits, Ji Yuan had a whole bunch of them at home.

Ji Yuan and his companions strolled away as the stall owner watched them leave. Because of the feather they had found, Ji Yuan spent much more time browsing the market afterward, his eyes wide open and much more attentive as he wandered. He even went into some buildings set up by various cultivation factions to see if there was anything special inside.

Well, the result was that almost everything was “special” for Ji Yuan. After all, he had little experience in these settings, and all sorts of strange and unusual things dazzled him.

Putting aside everything else, he had seen quite a few different kinds of spirits and monsters, some of which he indeed recognized as valuable. For example, there were spirits that could help take care of spiritual herbs and medicines and prevent them from dying, as well as spirits and monsters that could help people regulate their mind.

The spirits that guarded the spiritual herbs in particular were extremely rare. Spiritual herbs and medicines had very strict environmental requirements. Compared to other spiritual objects, these creatures were actually very fragile, easily attracted birds and beasts to peck at them or evil spirits to covet them.

Some natural spiritual herbs could only grow by either being looked after by special spirits or by being constantly watched over by demonic beasts. The kind of luck that would lead a mortal to fall off a cliff and encounter a Vermilion Fruit was truly unbelievable.

Although these things dazzled Ji Yuan, they were just things that existed in the normal cultivation world, and could not compare to that strange feather.

After leaving a building called “Lingbao Pavilion”,2 Ji Yuan and his group ran into the six people they had brought up the mountain earlier, as well as the young cultivator beside them.

The other party had obviously also seen Ji Yuan and Ju Yuanzi, and called out with delight.

“Immortal Master Ji, Immortal Master Ju, you’re here too?”

“It really is the two immortal masters, haha! Hiccup-“

The group greeted each other excitedly, and one of them even let out a long hiccup.3

Seeing Ji Yuan, Ju Yuanzi, and Qiu Feng looking over, the young cultivator quickly bowed slightly and clasped his hands in greeting.

“Greetings, esteemed seniors!”

The cultivation levels of these three individuals were unfathomable. They had previously brought six people with them up the mountain, and they exuded an aura of peace and tranquility, suggesting they were all true masters of the immortal path.

“Hmm, no need for formalities. You all, too.”

Ji Yuan said to the young cultivator, then turned to the group of six, who were all about to bow. Judging from the oily sheen lingering on their lips, they seemed to be doing well.

Perhaps influenced by Ji Yuan’s easygoing and refreshing aura, the girl who had asked the young cultivator questions during the meal suddenly had a flash of inspiration and spoke up again.

“Immortal Master Ji, Immortal Master Ju, there was a strange man with a peach blossom branch who kept staring at us and smiling. He didn’t seem like a good person; he seemed like he wanted to harm us…”

The young cultivator was speechless upon hearing this. He had only said that the other party was somewhat sinister, but whether he was harmful or not was still uncertain. Moreover, asking an immortal master he had only met once for help might be considered normal in the mortal world, where a cultivator might intervene if they encountered a demon pestering a mortal, but here, it was unheard of.

However, Ji Yuan’s reaction surprised the young cultivator. His lowered eyes widened slightly as he scanned the six people and the young cultivator, before nodding.

“I’ve seen the person you’re talking about. He is indeed somewhat sinister. His peach blossoms glow a blood-red hue, and his deathly aura seems to laugh at the living. I don’t know how such a malevolent creature got into the Peak Crossing.”

As Ji Yuan spoke, a bluish-white light appeared around him, and the Green Vine Sword faintly materialized, startling Qiu Feng.

“Mr. Ji, you mustn’t! The Peak Crossing forbids combat, even True Immortals cannot act rashly!”

Ju Yuanzi showed no surprise, instead stroking his beard and asking with a smile.

“Is that so, Master Qiu? What if a True Immortal master were to make a move?”

“This…”

Qiu Feng subconsciously glanced at Ji Yuan and the immortal sword that had appeared. True Immortals had never made a move at the Peak Crossing, but if they really did, would Yuelu Mountain dare to utter a sound?

The most likely explanation they would give in that instance would be that “the truth had been uncovered,” and the true immortal would exorcise the evil spirit. Regardless of whether the immortal’s actions were right or wrong, someone worthy of the title of “True Immortal” must possess a pure heart and clear mind, or at least they would be more insightful than the cultivators of Yuelu Mountain at the Peak Crossing.

However, this was merely the Green Vine Sword acting on its own, which meant that the immortal sword also disliked that evil aura. After all, the immortal sword was wrapped in emerald green vines, imbued with the vitality and energy of life, which naturally clashed with the peach blossom branch-wielding man.

But that didn’t mean Ji Yuan would make a move here. He had only glanced at the being; he wouldn’t necessarily annihilate them at the Peak Crossing.

Therefore, Ji Yuan gently stroked the Green Vine Sword, and its tender vines unfurled.

“Don’t be impatient.”

As soon as he finished speaking, the sharpness that only Ji Yuan and Ju Yuanzi could feel from the immortal sword dissipated. Ji Yuan grabbed the immortal sword with his left hand and held it horizontally. With a flick of his right hand, a stream of pure energy swept over the six people in front of him, causing two of them to tremble.

“It’s nothing serious.”

After speaking, Ji Yuan looked towards the outside of the Peak Crossing anchor point and gazed at the enormous Sky-Devouring Beast in the distance. He hadn’t sensed the aura of the person carrying the peach blossom branch while he was strolling around earlier, so he figured that they had either left the Peak Ferry Crossing or had already boarded the “ship”.

A moment later, the six learned that Ji Yuan and the others would also be traveling on the same ferry to the Heng Region in the Northern Territory. Naturally, they were overjoyed and left with smiles on their faces.

At this very moment, atop the Sky-Devouring Beast, near its tail, a person wrapped in a large animal skin huddled in a ball, shivering violently.

‘I didn’t do anything, I didn’t do anything! Oh my, what was that chill I felt just now…?’

The young man, who looked like a teenager, gripped the peach blossom branch tightly. When he looked down, he saw that one of the petals on a peach blossom had split open. The tear was extremely smooth, as if it had been cut by a sword.

‘My goodness… what is this… this Peak Crossing is too dangerous…’

…

Two days later, the roar of the Sky-Devouring Beast echoed throughout the Peak Ferry Crossing. For this enormous beast, two days was merely a nap. As it roamed the sky, it once again stirred up a fierce wind around the entire Peak Ferry Crossing.

Less than half a day after the Sky-Devouring Beast left, a huge flying boat with golden sails also sailed towards the summit. Of course, compared to the Sky-Devouring Beast, this flying boat was just like a toy in a child’s hand.

Chapter 464: The Senses of the Four-Eared Beast

Although the Xuanxin Mansion’s realm-crossing flying ship was like a toy compared to the Weimei Sect’s Sky-Devouring Beast, it was still an incredible behemoth to ordinary people.

Having seen everything they wanted to see in the past two days, Ji Yuan and the group from Yuhuai Mountain had nothing left to do at the Peak Crossing, so decided to head straight to the flying ship.

The flying ship hovered at the outer peak anchor point of the Peak Ferry Crossing, just like a large ship moored in the river, except that the water had been replaced by thick clouds and mist, as if it were supporting the flying ship.

Firstly, this was because the clouds and mist were part of the Peak Ferry Crossing’s shielding array. Secondly, it also prevented people with a fear of heights from being frightened when they saw the abyss below when boarding the ship. In Ji Yuan’s opinion, this was quite a humane design.1

As the group approached the outer peak harbor, they saw that many passengers, including immortal cultivators, spirits, mortals, and even demons, were preparing to board the flying boat. A huge gangplank was placed between the flying boat and the outer harbor cliffside.

Beside those who wanted to board the ship, many others disembarked as well. Burly monsters carried cargo boxes several times their own size, each step feeling extremely heavy.

At the entrance of the flying ship stood several Xuanxin Mansion cultivators dressed in golden robes, alongisde a gray dog-like immortal beast that seemed to have four ears and was more than a meter tall. It sat at the entrance, its gaze wandering among the passengers coming and going, with one eye open and one eye closed.2

“Heh, so even immortal abodes turn a blind eye.”3

Ju Yuanzi spoke. Wei Yuansheng, Shang Yiyi and other juniors were a little confused, but Ji Yuan, Qiu Feng, Yang Ming and other cultivators understood what he meant. Yang Ming thought for a moment and decided that he should explain some aspects to Ju Yuanzi, this old master of the mountain sect who had not left his home for a long time.

“Master Ju, you may not know this, but immortal crossings like the Peak Ferry Crossing have developed extremely rapidly. The Peak Ferry Crossing of today is completely different from the Peak Ferry Crossing of one or two hundred years ago. Cultivators, including as immortals, demons, monsters, Buddhas, humans, ghosts, spirits, and so on, all need a place to exchange goods and services.”

Yang Ming continued as he watched the pedestrians passing by.

“Therefore, in this situation, even if the aura of some demons or monsters is slightly off, it won’t be investigated too closely unless it’s very serious. After all, even immortal cultivators and Buddhist monks can be burdened with karmic obstacles at times.”

Ji Yuan was quite pleased to see this development. A ferry crossing led by cultivators would certainly be orderly, and it might even influence more demons and monsters, which might not be a bad thing.

The realm-crossing ferry was essentially a special kind of public transportation. In that case, it must bring returns to the immortal forces that support it. Regardless of what cultivators think, it’s ultimately about making a profit, which inevitably makes it carry a bit of a commercial air.

Therefore, to make it seem more pleasing, there was basically no “fee” at the boarding and exit points. Most of the time, the “fare” was already paid in the market at the Peak Ferry Crossing. The money was then naturally handed over to the people of Yuelu Mountain, who then settled accounts with the immortal cultivators who ferried people around various realms. This was a much more pleasing arrangement and was more convenient.

Ji Yuan and the others already possessed tokens, which served as a kind of ticket. The cultivators of Xuanxin Mansion were less concerned with identifying whether there were any heinous demons or evil spirits, and more concerned with checking the status of the tickets. As for mortals boarding and disembarking, they would turn a blind eye as long as they weren’t too outrageous or rude.

The group moved slowly. As they approached the gangplank, several cultivators from Xuanxin Mansion recognized the characteristics of the immortal cultivators from Yuhuai Mountain. Although Yuhuai Mountain was just a sacred land in the southern part of the Eastern Cloud Continent, it was still a legitimate immortal cultivation force with a name and reputation. In addition, there were obviously some experts among the people in the group. Therefore, the cultivators from Xuanxin Mansion took the lead in bowing to the people from Yuhuai Mountain at an appropriate distance.

“It’s been many years since I last met Fellow Taoists from Yuhuai Mountain. Welcome aboard, fellow Taoists!”

“Fellow Taoists of Xuanxin Mansion, you are too kind!”

Ji Yuan returned the greeting along with the others from Yuhuai Mountain. There was no need for him to jump out and say that he wasn’t from Yuhuai Mountain. His gaze then fell on the immortal beast beside him. He thought to himself that Hu Yun would really hate a beast like this.

The moment Ji Yuan looked over, the four-eared immortal beast’s two eyes, which had been alternating open and closed, suddenly opened wide. Even its droopy ears stood up and twitched slightly. Its gaze flickered as it looked at Ju Yuanzi and Ji Yuan, then focused on Ji Yuan, but made no sound.

Once Ji Yuan and his group had boarded the ship and disappeared from sight, a cultivator from Xuanxin Mansion with a high bun and a small hairpin crown immediately looked at the immortal beast beside him.

“Fellow Taoist Si Ting,4 is there something wrong?”

The immortal beast looked in the direction where Ji Yuan and the others had left, and spoke in human language.

“During our trip to Yuhuai Mountain, the immortal without a jade pendant was surrounded by a lot of noise. I seemed to hear many voices arguing and exclaiming, probably because he is keeping quite a few little spirits. Behind him, there was a celestial sword that had no hint of sword intent. Apart from the noisy arguments, the sounds around this immortal were like the tinkling of a mountain spring or the gentle breeze, almost pure and flawless. I had never felt anything like it before…”

“Si Ting” was not a name, but a title, which also referred to this rare demon beast. The Four-Eared Beasts in Xuanxin Mansion all used “Si Ting” as their surname and took a single character as their given name, such as the “Si Ting Xiu”5 before them now. They were also called “Fellow Taoist Si Ting”, which was similar to the title of “Lady Immortal Crane” in Yuhuai Mountain.6

“Pure and flawless?”

The cultivator from Xuanxin Mansion looked back at the direction of the flying ship, seemingly not understanding the deeper meaning. Si Ting beside him nodded and continued.

“I have seen many people with profound cultivation, although I cannot see through many of them. But this immortal has a particularly unique feeling about him. He is a true taoist cultivator.”

The difference between “cultivating immortality” and “cultivating the Tao” was only one word, and they were often used interchangeably even in the cultivation world. However, when Si Ting said it, the meaning was naturally different.

Immortal artifacts were treasures even in various immortal sects. Immortal artifacts possessed their own thoughts and preferences, and their spirituality often rivaled that of ordinary immortal cultivators. Some even had artifact spirits that could take human form. Immortal artifacts rarely recognized another owner aside from their first. However, they may protect the sect they belonged to if they felt nostalgic.

There were only a few possibilities for someone to be able to carry an immortal artifact. The word “extraordinary” alone could not encompass them all.

Hearing Si Ting’s words, the cultivators of Xuanxin Mansion began to wonder.

…

The airship was bustling with activity, featuring various shops and small markets, each corner with its own exotic flair. Some of the mortals doing business on the ship would occasionally call out in heavily accented dialects, inviting the newly arrived immortals or mortals to come and take a look at their shops.

Although the world was interconnected due to the teachings of cultivators, and people could communicate in various official languages across different regions, the differences in accents could be significant over larger geographical areas. It was not surprising for people to have their own dialects. However, the traces of the official languages were still quite strong, so Ji Yuan could mostly understand them.

The dialects spoken by some people on the flying ship were so different that Ji Yuan found them incomprehensible, causing him to frown frequently. He used to think that language habits were similar everywhere, aside from the fleet he had encountered in the Eastern Sea, which had been searching for Xianxia Island. Although they had strong accents, they could still communicate.

Even Wei Yuansheng and Cultivator Yiyi couldn’t understand them, and the younger disciples looked at each other in confusion. Then Wei Yuansheng directly asked Qiu Feng, who was standing next to him.

“Master, what are they saying?”

“Yes, we can’t understand a word they’re saying.”

Qiu Feng wasn’t really familiar with this either, as he had never left the Eastern Cloud Continent. He could only look to his senior brother, who was about the same level of knowledge as him. Ju Yuanzi offered his explanation.

“Long ago, the Eastern Cloud Continent was ruled by an enormous dynasty that also governed the divine path. During the gradual development of the continent, internal strife arose due to long-standing conflicts within the dynasty. However, even after the dynasty disintegrated, the people of the Eastern Cloud Continent were all originally of the same country, coupled with thousands of years of unified governance and the deep-rooted language memory of their ancestors. Even if they did not know ancient history, their speech was still largely the same, despite some differences.”

“If you cross the Eastern Cloud Continent, the differences in dialects can sometimes be exaggerated. Of course, the standard language is still largely the same.”

These words not only enlightened Wei Yuansheng and others, but also Ji Yuan, who was hearing them for the first time. None of the information he had encountered before had covered these points.

On their way to their inn on the flying ship, almost everyone in the group except Ju Yuanzi was filled with curiosity, including Ji Yuan, though he didn’t show it on his face.

It is almost universally acknowledged that flying ships, as enormous spiritual artifacts, contained hidden wonders within. The one belonging to Xuanxin Mansion was no exception. Its interior was far larger than its exterior, with multiple cabins, each serving a unique purpose. The living quarters were mostly located around the perimeter of the ship.

From this perspective, even if the actual usable area of ​​this flying ship was smaller than the back of the Sky-Devouring Beast, it wouldn’t be as exaggerated a discrepancy as the size comparison suggested.

After looking around for a while, they finally found their residence. Ji Yuan then understood why it was called an inn instead of a cabin: it was a small courtyard with walls, even larger than Ju’an Pavilion.

A somewhat unfamiliar immortal from Yuhuai Mountain flicked a token into the courtyard. A hazy spiritual light flashed through the courtyard, and the gate opened automatically, revealing flowers, trees, a table, and a scenic view inside.

“Not bad. Everyone, go inside and find your own rooms to rest. You can enter and exit using the tokens you carry.”

“Yes!”

“Yes!”

The disciples responded and excitedly ran inside. The martial brothers and sisters searched for rooms together, but they all wisely chose the outermost rooms, leaving the inner rooms for the elders.

A day later, Ji Yuan, who was sitting in his room cultivating on his bed, felt a slight vibration around him. Although it was barely audible to ordinary people, it was quite noticeable to him.

This commotion could only mean one thing: the Xuanxin Mansion’s realm-crossing flying ship had taken off and was leaving the harbor.

Ji Yuan immediately got up from his bed, opened the door, and saw Ju Yuanzi and and several other Yuhuai Mountain cultivators, who could also be considered masters, emerge from their own rooms at that same moment.

Qiu Feng even called out towards Wei Yuansheng’s room.

“Yuansheng, the flying ship has set sail. Aren’t you going to come out and take a look?”

“What? Now? I’m coming over!”

“Me too! Me too!”

The disciples came out one after another, and together with their elders, they quickly reached the deck of the flying ship using a technique similar to shrinking the earth.

On the mountainside of the outer peak, they could see the bustling market at the Peak Crossing in the distance. The flying ship slowly rose up, with the clouds below crashing against its hull like waves before they parted ways.

The mountains and mist of Yuelu Mountain appeared vast and ethereal, as the flying ship rose little by little, as if moving away from the picturesque scene under a slight sense of pressure.

Chapter 465: A Falling Galaxy

At this moment, besides Ji Yuan and his group, there were many other passengers on the deck watching the flying ship leave the harbor. Many of those who were riding the realm-crossing ferry for the first time couldn’t help but exclaim in amazement, marveling at the wonder of the immortal sect’s creation.

When the Xuanxin Mansion’s flying ship was forged, the sails were crafted using the same techniques used to forge the Yin-Yang Banner, another treasure. Therefore, during the daytime, the Sun’s power shone down and gathered in the sails, creating a golden halo. Although there was little wind, the sails began to billow.

A series of rustling sounds emanated from within the ship as it gained altitude and began to move forward. Considering the uneven cultivation levels of the immortal cultivators on board, as well as the presence of mortals, acceleration was a slow process.

Even so, with the array in place, the speed was still worthy of the name “flying,” providing a slight sense of propulsion. This in turn gave those on deck an even more unique sense of novelty. Many of the younger mortals shouted excitedly, including some near Ji Yuan and his group.

“Ah…ah…we’re flying…we’re flying…”

A child shouted with all his might towards the increasingly distant Peak Ferry Crossing, his face flushed with excitement, drawing the attention of Ji Yuan and his companions.

However, upon hearing his child call out like that, the elder, presumably his father, quickly grabbed the child and covered his mouth, scolding him in a heavily accented, official-sounding tone.

“Stop shouting, little one! There are immortals all around, don’t bother them…”

Ji Yuan found the accent very interesting and could roughly understand it. Seeing the man looking at him nervously, Ji Yuan and most of the other cultivators from Yuhuai Mountain gave him a friendly smile.

The child’s father’s worries were obviously unnecessary. It wasn’t just them, even some spirits with shallower cultivation were excited at his moment. Most of them were just “country bumpkins,” so no one would laugh at anyone else.

As the flying ship gradually accelerated, the feeling of the gentle breeze grew stronger, but the protective array prevented strong gusts of wind from sweeping people away.

The feel of the ship when docked versus when it was moving was completely different. It is as if it was only complete when it was sailing. And it seemed that this feeling wasn’t unique to mortals. The entire flying ship began to fully “unfurl” at this moment, with everything coming alive.

Ji Yuan even saw some cultivators take flight, following the Xuanxin Mansion’s flying ship, their long sleeves fluttering, looking very elegant.

“Master, why are they flying? We’re already on a flying ship, following along would be too… draining on their spiritual power.”

The speaker was Guan He, a disciple of Taoist Yang Ming. He originally wanted to say, “would be too stupid,” but after thinking about it, he changed it to a more tactful wording.

Master Yang Ming looked up at the cultivators who were flying alongside him and spoke calmly.

“You think they’re just doing this for fun? You’re wrong. The Yin-Yang sails of the Xuanxin Mansion’s Yin-Yang Flying Ship are incredibly powerful, drawing in the power of the Sun. Those cultivators had all coordinated with the Xuanxin Mansion beforehand, either because they were already acquainted or because they paid a certain price. They are now taking flight to use these enormous ‘Yin-Yang Banners’ to collect the power of the Sun. At night, you’ll see people collecting the power of the Moon in a similar way.”

Upon hearing this, Ji Yuan’s heart stirred. Every now and then, he would use the effects of Ambergris wine and his own wondrous techniques to draw upon the power of the stars to wear down the last bit of residual damage from the lightning tribulation.

As the saying went, “it takes a hundred days to recover from a broken bone.” Although his injury had lasted for several years and had not yet healed, Ji Yuan was already incredibly grateful that the healing had reached this point. He was not originally someone who was supposed to undergo tribulation. Even if he survived this tribulation lightning, the tribulation would not be considered to have ended, and he couldn’t escape it entirely. In addition, the lightning was special, making this kind of injury the most troublesome.

Ji Yuan alone could create the sensation of a falling galaxy using his wondrous Transformation of Heaven and Earth. However, his power ultimately had its limits. He had felt invincible up until now, but he could never break through the last trace of the lightning’s residual power. As a result, Ji Yuan had almost made it a habit to suppress the injuries from the lightning tribulation.

Now, if he were to use Xuanxin Mansion’s flying ship, a product of complex and exquisite formations, wouldn’t it be more effective?

‘It’s worth a try!’

Having reached this conclusion, Ji Yuan did not hesitate and directly turned to Qiu Feng, who was standing to the side. It was obviously futile to ask the old hermit Ju Yuanzi about such matters.

“Fellow Taoist Qiu, if I also wish to use the Yin-Yang Sail to absorb the power of the Moon1 tonight, should I speak directly with the Fellow Taoists of Xuanxin Mansion now, or do I need to make an appointment a few days in advance? What would the cost be?”

“Mr. Ji also wants to collect the power of the Moon?”

“That’s right.”

Hearing this, Qiu Feng glanced towards the bow of the ship, then spoke to Ji Yuan.

“Logically speaking, we should inform them in advance and wait for permission from Xuanxin Mansion before going up, as these positions are quite sought after. However, if Mr. Ji is interested, I will go with you to inquire.”

“That would be perfect!”

Ji Yuan responded with a smile.

Those standing nearby, such as Ju Yuanzi, naturally assumed that this was a matter of cultivation, and it was inappropriate to ask too many questions, so they didn’t say anything. They just watched Ji Yuan and Qiu Feng leave together towards the bow of the deck, secretly speculating about what might be going on.

Generally, Xuanxin Mansion cultivators would sit cross-legged and watch over the masts at the front of the deck, and passersby would avoid these areas.

When Qiu Feng and Ji Yuan arrived, the two Xuanxin Mansion cultivators there actually recognized them, or rather, recognized Ji Yuan, and had already stood up and greeted them with clasped hands.

“Fellow Taoists of Yuhuai Mountain, is there something you need?”

Qiu Feng quickly stepped forward, cupped his hands and greeted them in return, and said.

“Fellow Taoists, I wish to inquire about the price to pay for drawing upon the power of the Moon using the flying ship, and when we might have a turn?”

The two Xuanxin Mansion cultivators glanced at Ji Yuan before asking Qiu Feng another question.

“Do all the fellow Taoists of Yuhuai Mountain need this?”

Ji Yuan bowed slightly and spoke.

“It’s not that the fellow Taoists of Yuhuai Mountain need it, but rather that I simply wish to borrow the convenience of the Yin-Yang Sail.”

Seeing that it was indeed Ji Yuan who wanted to use their power, one of them immediately answered.

“Oh, so it’s just you alone, senior. Then it’s no big deal. Adding one more person won’t hurt. You can fly and follow tonight. As for the price, it depends on the time you collect the Yin energy. If you use the technique all night every day, you’ll need to pay one pound of spirit stones of any of the Five Elements every seven days.”

Ji Yuan felt a little relieved upon hearing this. So it was calculated by time. He was worried that it would be calculated by quantity.2 He felt that he was probably the kind of person who would collect a lot of Yin energy, and his intuition was quite accurate. It would be a bit embarrassing if he couldn’t pay the bill.

“Very well then. I plan to use the technique tonight. Uh, do I need any credentials?”

The Xuanxin Mansion cultivator nodded.

“Senior, do you have your token with you?”

Upon hearing this, Ji Yuan took out a token from his sleeve. The cultivator took it, touched it with his finger, chanted a few words, and then returned the token to Ji Yuan.

“Alright, then you can ascend freely.”

“Thank you! By the way, it’s alright if I make a bit of a commotion, right?”

“What kind of commotion? Will it damage the ship?”

The cultivators from Xuanxin Mansion asked cautiously, knowing that this person might be a person of great supernatural power.

Ji Yuan shook his head.

“The ship itself would be undamaged. It’s just that the noise might be a bit loud, but the damage would be minimal.”

“Oh, then it’s alright. It’s normal for us immortal cultivators to produce some unusual phenomena when perfroming techniques, so please rest assured, senior!”

“Alright, that’s good!”

Having received assurance, Ji Yuan’s mood improved. After thanking them, he left with Qiu Feng. The Xuanxin Mansion cultivators soon informed the ship’s administrator of the matter via mental transmission not long after.

…

While the speed of the realm-crossing ship might not have been the fastest, it was definitely the most stable and comfortable among flying magical artifacts. Often, those on board didn’t even feel like they were flying. Its stability far surpassed that of ships on water, with very little swaying and no chance of seasickness.

The second deck of the ship’s was a unique space: a small plaza, with an enormous flat glass mirror in the center. This was no ordinary floor; it projected the scenery of the mountains and rivers below as the flying ship passed over them.

During the day, mortals, cultivators, spirits… many came here to witness this spectacle.

At night, due to the unique nature of the yin-yang sails, the starlight and especially the moonlight overhead on the deck were exceptionally dazzling, creating a breathtaking scene. Passengers would often go to the deck to enjoy the beautiful view before going to sleep.

Tonight was no different; after the sun set, the sails gradually tinged with starlight and moonlight, creating a striking contrast to their golden hue in the daytime.

One by one, cultivators and even a few spirits rose into the air, some riding on magical artifacts, others riding on the wind and clouds, following behind the sails, all bathed in the moonlight.

Everyone from Yuhuai Mountain was on the deck. However, aside from enjoying the night view, they were mainly there to see what Ji Yuan was up to.

Ji Yuan glanced at the group of curious Yuhuai Mountain immortals around him. Even Ju Yuanzi was distracted, feigning interest in the scenery. He couldn’t help but shake his head with a smile, then lightly pushed off the ground and took flight on a gentle breeze.

Once he reached over five zhang in the air, the protective effect of the flying ship’s array seemed to weaken considerably. The surrounding area filled with howling winds, and Ji Yuan’s long hair was whipped behind him by the wind, his clothes fluttering loudly.

Instead of using his own wind control to erase this influence, he kept a suitable distance from the other cultivators, slowly calmed his mind, and focused his spirit. When the time was right, Ji Yuan looked around and spoke.

“Fellow Daoists, please prepare the necessary immortal storage items.”

After this baffling remark, before anyone could ask any questions, Ji Yuan flicked his sleeve and channeled his spiritual power.

‘Heaven and Earth give rise to the world, the river of stars shines on untouched.’3

Within his mental realm, the giant avatar projected by Ji Yuan’s mind stood between heaven and earth uttered these words. The celestial stars composed of chess pieces shone brightly in an instant, manifesting outside of Ji Yuan’s body alongside with the river of stars, the galaxy in his mental realm.

In the instant between illusion and reality, the giant Yin-Yang sail sensed the continuous rise of Taoist energy, and suddenly brightened up as if in response, inheriting the mental conception of Ji Yuan’s manfestation.

In the next instant, endless celestial power and moonlight descended, and the power of the Moon became increasingly dense, even viscous…

Whoosh…

Continuous, illusory flames of Yin energy materialized around the large ship.

In a daze, Xuanxin Mansion’s flying ship was suddenly sailing through a sea of stars, surrounded by dazzling starlight, and the entire ship seemed to have transformed into a bright moon, radiating a moonlight fire.

This change surprised even Ji Yuan, let alone the others on board the ship. The Xuanxin Mansion cultivators who were sitting cross-legged on the sail had already stood up, and more cultivators were rushing out of the cabins. The tops of the various buildings on the deck were also already full of people.

“A falling galaxy?”

One of the two administrators of the flying ship stared wide-eyed as he spoke, while the other muttered to himself in the same manner.

“You call this some kind of commotion!?”

Interesting note: I say “collect the power of the moon,” but the word they use is 太阴 (tàiyīn). The common word for moon is 太阴 (yuèliàng), whereas 太阴 (tàiyīn) roughly means “greater Yin” and roughly stands for the Yin power of the night, as exemplified by the moon, similar to how the sun stands for the power of Yang. I’m still going to translate them as “Moon” and “Sun” but I’ll do my best to capitalize them if they refer to greater concepts (though I might forget sometimes, please forgive me)

4/4 couplet. Note the second phrase could be translated a whole host of different ways, so if anyone is particularly attached to a different translation (i.e. “brilliantly”, “regardless,” etc.) let me know

Chapter 466: A Sky Full of Stars Returning to Ji Yuan

Even the administrator of Xuanxin Mansion’s flying ship was stunned, let alone the other Xuanxin Mansion cultivators and the passengers on the flying ship.

For mortals, the surrounding scenery was breathtakingly beautiful, giving the feeling of traveling through a galaxy. It was a dreamlike sensation as if they were in close proximity to a dazzling sea of ​​stars, leaving everyone mesmerized.

Some people couldn’t resist leaning against the ship’s railing, reaching out to touch the flowing starlight around them, and actually felt as if they were gliding through water.

“How fortunate I am to be able to ride on this immortal’s ferry! How fortunate I am!”

An elderly man trembled slightly with excitement, his eyes wide open, trying to etch this scene into his mind.

For the cultivators, the shock of the changes before their eyes was even greater than that of ordinary people. Compared to ordinary people who knew nothing of what they were seeing, they understood the deeper meaning behind the scene they were witnessing.

“This is absolutely not something that the Yin-Yang Sail of the Xuanxin Mansion’s flying boat could do! Absolutely not!”

An old cultivator darted out of the stern hatch and stood on the deck, gazing at the surrounding river of stars. Stars seemed to float in the hazy light, not stationary but rather exhibiting their own subtle patterns. This was no longer a simple manifestation of starlight, but the true Tao of the Starry River.

“Someone is using the Yin-Yang Sail to cast a technique!”

Another voice rang out beside the old man. It belonged to a cultivator with a full head of black hair but an aged face. Although his voice sounded calmer, his expression revealed that he was also deeply shaken.

“Who? Could it be the Sect Master or the Ancestor of Xuanxin Mansion who has come in person?”

The old man subconsciously looked up at the sky above the sails of the flying ship, where a group of cultivators were still flying alongside the boat. Streams of spiritual light continuously flowed out of a certain point, but the light was so dense that it was impossible to see who was inside.

The two were silent for a while, then the second individual suddenly changed the subject.

“Such abundant Yin energy and celestial power, what a waste it would be not to take it!”

“That’s right. We’ll meet again at the Xuanxin Mansion later and present them with a token of our sincerity!”

The two no longer held back, and with a flick of their sleeves, they summoned their own immortal storage artifacts, channeling their power into the starry river outside the ship and using the celestial power and moonlight to collect the main force of Yin energy.

Many cultivators did the same. Some, wary of the Xuanxin Mansion’s attitude, initially just observed and tried to understand the possible mysteries. But as more and more people took action, they couldn’t help but join in.

Cultivation was about seeking freedom, since the Xuanxin Mansion hadn’t explicitly forbade action, why hold back?

Some spirits and monsters were the last to move. It wasn’t that they were more polite than the immortal cultivators, but rather that this flying ship was, after all, the territory of immortals. These beings wouldn’t dare to be too presumptuous. But now that all the immortals had made their move, these creatures, who relied more on Yin energy, naturally couldn’t wait to start absorbing moonlight as well.

The cultivators of Xuanxin Mansion, of course, had no energy to care about others at the moment, focused entirely on maintaining the formation of the flying ship. The sudden appearance of the celestial river was indeed shocking, and the dazzling stars were indeed beautiful, but it was as turbulent as a real celestial river.

The Xuanxin Mansion cultivators were terrified that the flying ship would capsize in the starry river. If something went wrong with the formation, it was hard to predict what would happen. Even though they knew that a master was casting the technique and could definitely control it, the Xuanxin Mansion cultivators dared not gamble.

However, in addition to maintaining the formation together, the two Xuanxin Mansion administrators were also observing the Yin-Yang Sail. In truth, the biggest beneficiary so far was not the cultivators flying with the ship, nor was it the other cultivators on the ship who were currently using techniques to collect the power of the starlight, but rather the flying ship itself.

Each time the flying ship used its spiritual power to draw upon the power of the Sun and Moon during the day and night, it was also a refining of the flying ship’s resilience.

The administrators in charge of the flying ships were just like the grandmasters of Yuhuai Mountain who rotated administrative duties; none of them had low cultivation. The two were well aware that the vast sea of stars was actually following the flying ship.

They did their best to maintain the formation, not only to prevent accidents, but also to consider whether they could refine the river of stars to a certain extent. Even if it seemed a bit like a snake trying to swallow an elephant,1 it didn’t mean they had to swallow it all. If they could refine a piece of it, this flying ship, which was already a treasure of the Xuanxin Mansion, would be even more remarkable!

“If this galaxy could be incorporated into the flying ship, then…”

“Don’t speak, concentrate on casting the technique!”

The two had already returned to a special quiet room inside the cabin, where they sat cross-legged on futons and continuously cast techniques, placing radiant spiritual stones onto the array diagrams hanging on the walls of the room.

In an instant, the light from the flying boat became even brighter, and the ethereal Moon’s Yin fire that emerged around it grew more and more intense. This fire was not activated by Ji Yuan, but rather was generated by the flying ship’s own array in response, having been refining the power of the stars from the very beginning.

Somewhere on the ship’s deck, all the cultivators from Yuhuai Mountain sat cross-legged, jade pendants floating above their heads like tiny stars, drawing in the surrounding Yin energy.

Like everyone else, after being deeply shocked, they did not waste this rare opportunity for spiritual practice.

While appropriately absorbing the power of Moon, Ju Yuanzi spoke.

“The people of Xuanxin Mansion reacted quickly, but they are overestimating themselves. How could Mr. Ji’s technique that brought down the river of stars be used to benefit someone else?”

Just as Ju Yuanzi suspected, Ji Yuan was constantly casting techniques as he hung suspended in the air, pushing the Wondrous Method of Heaven and Earth to its limit, creating magnificent spectacle of a radiant galaxy. Ultimately, it was for his own injuries; perhaps, seeing how effective it was, he also had the idea of ​​taking a little more from it.

Ji Yuan was happy to share some of the benefits with the other cultivators, since he couldn’t control so much celestial power. However, it was impossible for him to work hard to maintain the descending galaxy solely for Xuanxin Mansion to refine it below. He was paying for it!

After maintaining it for about a quarter of an hour, Ji Yuan made his move. Although it seemed as if the formation of the galaxy was instantaneous, it was because Ji Yuan’s vision of the creation of heaven and earth was too realistic. The true celestial power still had to be accumulated little by little, and it was only at this moment that it was at its most concentrated.

At this moment, Ji Yuan was enveloped in dense starlight, his entire figure was surrounded by rings of light, as if countless overlapping silhouettes of Ji Yuan were performing actions.

In his inner realm, Ji Yuan, who embodied the laws of heaven and earth, opened his eyes, carrying the hints of an Edict, and spoke in a majestic voice.

‘The entire starry sky returns to my body.’

The simplest way of guiding Qi into the body, performed by the Wondrous Method of Heaven and Earth, had now become the path to channel the power of the Moon into his body.

Swish… swish…

Although it was physically inaudible, a wave-like resonance echoed within everyone’s hearts, mortals included.

With a single thought, the starry river began to sway.

At this moment, Ji Yuan waved his hand and used the Yin-Yang Sail and his own Universe in the Sleeve, then combined them with his control of the Transformation of Heaven and Earth and his own intent. A new bright moon was created within this starry river, with Ji Yuan at its center. Endless starlight surged like a river, and the moon’s halo resembled a spinning vortex.

Ji Yuan was multitasking. A small part of his consciousness was used to maintain the absorption of the Yin energy, while the rest was completely immersed in guiding it. The terrifying starry river seemed to have entered Ji Yuan’s inner world, flowing through the sky in arcs. Then, guided by Ji Yuan’s will, it rushed out of the inner world in an instant and surged fiercely into his left arm.

The extreme yang energy of the heavenly lightning entangled his body and soul, while the boundless yin energy, like a raging flood exuding cold air, crashed down from the heavens.

‘This is truly a sight to behold, like a true galaxy falling from the heavens!’

Ji Yuan actually had the leisure to think about it for a moment, before witnessing the Yin energy and the lightning energy collide, creating endless spiritual fluctuations.

Buzz… Boom… Buzz… Boom…

Swish swish swish…

Above the flying ship, in the outside world, blurry ripples spread outwards, centered on Ji Yuan. Those who were touched by them, whether mortals, cultivators, spirits, or monsters, felt their bodies go numb, and a terrifying sense of oppressive power swept over them.

Among the spirits and monsters, there were quite a few who were so frightened that they huddled together, not even bothering to absorb the Yin energy. However, things were not as calm as before. The surging starry river had already made many cultivators hesitate, and even those who were still capable stopped to observe the changes.

The realm of the inner world was formed from the power of the mind and the body. Even if Ji Yuan’s inner world were a vast and boundless world, physical constitution still had a significant impact. It was impossible for him to absorb so much Yin power.

Let alone Ji Yuan in his current state, even a genuine immortal master with years of experience couldn’t possibly absorb all the celestial power directly. However, Ji Yuan was absorbing it while simultaneously using it to cleanse his left arm, so the consumption was greater than he had anticipated.

Ji Yuan never expected that the tribulation lightning, which was clearly on its last breath, could exhibit such exaggerated tenacity, even showing a tendency to stubbornly resist dissipating. How could Ji Yuan allow such a thing to happen? Otherwise, it would truly become a chronic ailment.

Since he currently had access to an enormous amount of celestial power at the moment,Ji Yuan simply risked bringing the “battlefield” to his entire body to dilute the remaining tribulation lightning. Because he could mobilize a vast amount of Yin star energy, every part of his body remained in a state of saturated, scouring cleansing.

Boom…

A thunderous aura emanated from Ji Yuan, and the faint rumble of thunder, though nearly inaudible, caused all the cultivators to tremble slightly.

“Boom…”

This time, thunder really did rumble, and many people with weak cultivation and poor willpower subconsciously crouched down.

About ten breaths later, after the first and only real thunderclap…

Whoosh…

The whole starry river vanished in an instant, leaving only the fading starlight surrounding the flying ship, fading into the distance.

In reality, Ji Yuan had withdrawn his mental river of stars and dispersed the Wondrous Method of Heaven and Earth. The speed at which the starlight flowed was incredibly fast. Without Ji Yuan’s restraint, the Yin-Yang Sail’s power alone was insufficient to maintain its convergence, and it immediately scattered from the earth.

However, because the commotion was so astonishing, to everyone’s eyes, it seemed as though the vast starry river had been entirely absorbed by Ji Yuan.

The Yin-Yang sails of the flying boat still shone faintly with starlight, much like before Ji Yuan cast the technique. However, comparisons were inevitable; compared to moments before, the Yin-Yang sails now appeared dull and lifeless.

Ji Yuan’s body was surrounded by many “double images” of starlight, keeping his form still indistinct. The cultivators flying around him subconsciously kept a certain distance from Ji Yuan as a sign of respect, all wanting to get a clear look at this extraordinary figure.

The cultivators and mortals below did the same; apart from Yuhuai Mountian and Xuanxin Mansion, who already knew some information, everyone wanted to see the expert hidden within the layers of light.

Ji Yuan was relieved that he was now free of the tribulation lightning. However, he didn’t want to be used as a free show, and needed to consolidate his strength, so he didn’t have time to make his presence known.

All that could be seen was a flash of light in the sky, and the mysterious master transformed into a beam of light and shot into the front cabin at the bow of the flying boat, disappearing from sight.

Only the people in Yuhuai Mountain heard Ji Yuan’s mental transmission.

“I have gained something tonight, so I will return to my room and remain in seclusion for a few days.”

Chapter 467: The Master Merely Stayed for One Day

The master vanished in a flash of light, but some of the spirits and monsters on board the ship hesitated to get up. The thunder they had just heard was terrifying, like a calamity descending upon them, a truly frightening event. They didn’t recover from the shock for quite some time.

With Ji Yuan gone, the starry river around Xuanxin Mansion’s flying boat also dissipated, thus announcing the end of the night’s condensation of Yin power. Although the Yin-Yang Sail was still drawing upon the power of the Moon, having witnessed that grand spectacle, this now seemed quite ordinary.

The two old cultivators on the stern of the deck had long since stopped casting their techniques. They watched the starlight in the sky dissipate, and then looked at the bottle-shaped magic artifact in their hands, which was rattling and shaking as if it were half-filled with water.

“In less than an hour, we have obtained so much True Yin Water.”

“Our luck is indeed good, but I wonder who exactly that person who just cast the technique is, and what their relationship is with Xuanxin Mansion?”

“That remains to be seen.”

As they spoke, their gazes fell on the bottle-shaped magical artifact in their hands. They paused for a moment, then looked at each other, both seeing a hint of surprise in each other’s eyes.

“That master absorbed the entire starry river?”

“That should be the case…”

Even knowing that the starry river they just saw wasn’t a true galaxy, the Yin power, rich moonlight, and celestial power it was comprised of were real. How could it be contained in an instant?

…

On Yuhuai Mountain’s side, after receiving Ji Yuan’s message, everyone looked at each other. They all realized that everyone within their small group must have received Ji Yuan’s message.

“Mr. Ji has gone back into seclusion. You may continue your cultivation here for a while, or enjoy the night view. I will go back first to protect Mr. Ji.”

As Ju Yuanzi stood up with a flick of his clothes, he vanished like a phantom.

After Ju Yuanzi left, the younger generation of disciples of Yuhuai Mountain were noticeably more relaxed. Wei Yuansheng in particular looked at his master Qiu Feng with barely concealed excitement.

“Master, was Mr. Ji responsible for creating that river of stars just now?”

Qiu Feng looked at him with amusement, then glanced at the group of young cultivators around him who were acting like curious children. He exchanged smiles with his senior brother Yang Ming and the other two masters.

“If it wasn’t Mr. Ji’s doing, who do you think could have done it? The Fellow Taoists from Xuanxin Mansion? Even if the Yin-Yang Sail could withstand such supernatural powers, the Xuanxin Mansion experts on this ship don’t have that kind of cultivation. Even the two administrators are far from that level.”

Seeing that the young disciples were unable to contain their excitement as they discussed the scene they had just witnessed and their cultivation gains, Qiu Feng gave them a word of advice.

“Don’t reveal this to outsiders. Not many people know that Mr. Ji was behind it. Just keep it to yourselves.”

“This disciple understands!”

“Yes!”

“Understood, Master Uncle!”

As the disciples of Yuhuai Mountain agreed, Yang Ming and the other masters of Yuhuai Mountain saw several cultivators in golden robes hurrying towards them; they were cultivators from Xuanxin Mansion.

At this moment, an administrator from Xuanxin Mansion was leading two disciples to the deck area where the group from Yuhuai Mountain was seated. They bowed before they even arrived.

“Greetings, fellow Taoists of Yuhuai Mountain!”

Even though he knew that Ji Yuan was definitely not present, the flying ship’s administrator still couldn’t help but glance around the Yuhuai Mountain contingent.

Yang Ming, Qiu Feng, and the others stood up together and led their disciples in returning the greeting.

“Greetings, fellow Taoists of Xuanxin Mansion. May we ask what brings you here to see us?”

The administrator of Xuanxin Mansion straightened his robes, smiled, and shook his head slightly.

“Fellow Taoist Yang, please don’t play dumb or speak in riddles. May I ask if you masters could speak to me in private?”

A Taoist from Yuhuai Mountain replied.

“Senior Brother Qiu, Senior Brother Yang, I’ll take the younger generation back to the cabin first. You can talk to the fellow Taoists of Xuanxin Mansion.”

“Alright, thank you for your trouble, Junior Brother Wang.”

The group from Xuanxin Mansion quickly bowed slightly to Master Wang, and after he returned the bow, they watched him and his group leave.

Then the administrator from Xuanxin Mansion turned his gaze back and asked solemnly.

“Fellow Taoists of Yuhuai Mountain, has Mr. Ji returned to his cabin yet?”

Yang Ming and his fellow disciples exchanged glances and then spoke.

“Mr. Ji was tired and had already returned to his rooms to rest.”

He didn’t say that Ji Yuan had gone into seclusion, only that he was tired. The cultivators of Xuanxin Mansion didn’t doubt this statement. Then the magistrate looked back at the direction of the Yin-Yang Sail.

“Mr. Ji’s supernatural power is profound. He is unlike any cultivator from Xuanxin Mansion, yet he was able to manipulate the Yin-Yang Sail to perform such astonishing and extraordinary techniques. The image of the falling stars is still captivating…”

At this point, the administrator glanced at Qiu Feng and the others, and lowered his voice.

“Fellow Taoists, is Mr. Ji a highly accomplished master from Yuhuai Mountain?”

Since they had asked so directly, Yang Ming, Qiu Feng, and the others had no choice but to answer truthfully.

“Mr. Ji and our Yuhuai Mountain have a very close friendship, but he is not a cultivator of Yuhuai Mountain.”

“Do you know where Mr. Ji’s sect is?”

Qiu Feng shook his head in reply.

“The master has never mentioned having a sect, and even if he did, it would not be an immortal abode we are familiar with today.”

“Oh…”

The administrator from Xuanxin mansion hesitated for a moment before carefully asking.

“Do any of you fellow Taoists have any understanding of the cultivation method Mr. Ji practices? Uh, I mean, Mr. Ji will probably be using the power of the stars to cultivate again tomorrow, or rather some other day, right? If so, could you please tell us a rough date? It would be best if you could inform us in advance so we can set up the array diagram and maximize the flying ship’s array, which would also be of great assistance to Mr. Ji!”

“Hahaha…”

The masters from Yuhuai Mountain all laughed.

“We understand your thoughts, fellow Taoist. However, Mr. Ji has always been free and unrestrained, acting as he pleases. We, having shallower understanding, have no ideas as to his profound and mysterious methods, let alone whether he will once again activate the power of the stars. Fellow Taoist, such a magnificent celestial phenomenon is something that can only be encountered once in a lifetime.”

“Alas, how could we be unaware of this? That’s why we came to you fellow Taoists of Yuhuai Mountain to subtly inquire. By the way, since you are going to the Heng Region in the Northern Territories, you must be going to the Cultivator’s Grand Assembly, right?”

“Of course!”

“Mr. Ji is going too?”

“Mr. Ji is going too!”

Upon hearing this, Administrator Zhou from Xuanxin Mansion bowed again.

“Then I won’t disturb you fellow Taoists any longer. Please rest well on the flying ship. We will visit you again another day. I also hope our Xuanxin Mansion might have the opportunity to exchange more with you at the Cultivator’s Grand Assembly!”

Yang Ming, Qiu Feng, and the others’ eyes lit up slightly, and they quickly returned the bow.

“Definitely! Our Yuhuai Mountain has not participated in the Cultivator’s Grand Assembly for many years, and we still need the guidance of the Fellow Taoists from Xuanxin Mansion!”

“Oh, not at all. Not to mention Mr. Ji, who is traveling with you all, even Elder Ju is an extraordinary figure. We can simply exchange ideas with each other!”

“Haha, yes, let’s exchange ideas!”

“Please be seated, fellow Taoists. Farewell!”

“No need to see us off!”

The cultivators from Xuanxin Mansion arrived and left quickly. It wasn’t that they didn’t want to say more, but rather that some questions were inconvenient to ask too deeply. The person was right on the ship, which was far too close for such a highly skilled expert. They were so close that he could probably sense their presence. Therefore, they could only formally get to know the experts before delving into such topics, otherwise it might be easy to offend them.

For Yuhuai Mountain, as they had not attended the Cultivator’s Grand Assembly for many years, it was naturally beneficial to be able to communicate with Xuanxin Mansion in advance. Although they had inadvertently used Mr. Ji’s name, the people of Yuhuai Mountain already knew Ji Yuan’s character quite well, and he wouldn’t mind such things.

Inside the courtyard the inn, Ju Yuanzi sat at the wooden table, slowly sipping a pot of hot tea.

Ji Yuan had already lay down on his side in the room, drifting off to sleep in stillness, feeling the changes within his body.

This tribulation lightning had troubled him for several years. Now, his physical condition was like that of a person who had been running with heavy weights for a long time who had suddenly had their restraints removed, becoming incredibly light. His spiritual power flowed more smoothly, and he was finally able to restore a complete cycle of energy.

However, because Ji Yuan managed to guide the remaining power of the tribulation lightning throughout his body at the last moment, diluting and purifying it piece by piece, his body still felt somewhat tingly and numb. But compared to the pain he had experienced before, this tingling sensation was quite comfortable.

In the landscape of his inner world, Ji Yuan, in the manifestation of his consciousness, closed his eyes to perceive the scene. The stars in the sky disappeared, a dark cloud obscured the landscape. Thunder roared within his mind.

This was a manifestation of his thoughts and intentions, foreshadowing an advancement in his art of lightning.

As the saying went, no one was perfect. Even though Ji Yuan felt that he had always been quite successful in cultivation, he was not outstanding in everything.

For example, his lightning techniques had always relied entirely on the power of the Lightning Talisman Edict. Now, after a long “struggle” against the tribulation lightning, Ji Yuan had gained a “painful understanding” of lightning techniques, and reached a new level.

Putting aside everything else, Ji Yuan already had some ideas on how to refine and restore the Lightning Talisman Edict, which had not yet recovered. This Lightning Talisman was formidable. If it were truly restored, it would definitely be a trump card. Once used, it would unleash a devastating lightning tribulation upon the world.

At this moment, Ji Yuan’s only problem was the excessive erosion of the Yin energy. Even though Ji Yuan’s Five Elements were in perfect balance, he was still somewhat imbalanced in Yin and Yang. Fortunately, Ji Yuan had a way to quickly adjust himself. He carefully controlled the True Fire of Samadhi to circulate throughout his body, which also served as an opportunity to temper his physical body. However, the extremes of ice and fire he experienced during this time were not much better than being struck by lightning.

Although he had initially claimed it would take a few days, Ji Yuan slept for a full half month.

During these past two weeks, every night, whether it was the cultivators of Xuanxin Mansion, the cultivators on the flying ship, or even ordinary people, everyone seemed to be anticipating something.

Even though those spirits were terrified by the thunder that night, they still went out to wait every night afterward. Being frightened wouldn’t cost them anything. Besides, cultivation was the most important thing. What did physical wounds matter?

Unfortunately, the magnificent sight of the flying ship traveling through the galaxy never appeared again. According to rumors circulating on the flying ship, the master had already absorbed enough Yin energy after casting his technique on the first night, so there was no need for him to make a big fuss.

With that perspective, it did make sense. Many people received a considerable amount of Yin energy in less than an hour. Every cultivator had witnessed the master absorbing the entire river of stars that night.

They couldn’t even imagine how much power that master who had single-handedly absorbed the entire galaxy had collected. Either his own cultivation was unimaginably high, or he possessed an unimaginably wondrous magical treasure, or, most likely, both.

The people of Xuanxin Mansion were no longer in such a hurry. As long as they could talk to Ji Yuan, that was enough. They were in a much better position than the other cultivators outside, who didn’t even know anything about him. There was plenty of time in the future.

On the very day Ji Yuan emerged from seclusion, the view below the flying ship was already a vast ocean.

Chapter 468: A Cry for Help in the Heavenly Storm

One morning, about two weeks after entering the sea…

The sun rose above the horizon, and morning light spilled across the ocean surface, making the distant sea appear golden.

Ji Yuan preferred standing on the deck to admire the scenery over staying inside the cabin. Even though with his poor eyesight, everything was blurry aside from some supernatural items, he was already used to it. In addition, with the presence of his inner world’s landscape, when he brought himself to a certain mystical state, he could visualize the landscape with great accuracy by focusing his mind on the outside world.

The wind tousled Ji Yuan’s hair back, and as he watched the sun rise over the sea, he once again took out the golden-red feather, examining it closely in the sunlight.

The morning light shone on the feather, making its colors even more vibrant and brilliant. For a moment, it seemed as though the sunlight were flowing like water on the feather’s surface, but upon closer inspection, it was merely an illusion.

Ju Yuanzi stood beside Ji Yuan, also staring at the feather. Now that no one was around and the opportunity was right, he finally asked Ji Yuan the question that had been bothering him.

“Mr. Ji, when you bought this bird feathers from the market at the Peak Ferry Crossing, your expression was unusual. What is so special about this bird feather?”

Ji Yuan infused a tiny bit of spiritual energy and power into the feather, causing it to briefly emit a demonic aura. After a few breaths, Ji Yuan immediately stopped.

Then Ji Yuan turned to look at Ju Yuanzi and asked.

“Fellow Taoist Ju, did you sense anything?”

Ju Yuanzi knew, of course, that Ji Yuan had infused spiritual energy into the feather, since heat had just been emitted. But he couldn’t sense anything else special about it, so he shook his head truthfully.

“Aside from the heat emanating from it, I couldn’t sense anything else. What did you notice, Mr. Ji?”

Ji Yuan nodded with a solemn expression.

“Demonic aura, an extremely dense and terrifying demonic aura…”

After thinking for a moment, Ji Yuan added:

“Between reality and illusion.”

“Demonic aura?”

Ju Yuanzi was slightly taken aback, then stared intently at the bird feather again. Was there a demonic aura just now? Between reality and illusion?

Ji Yuan had expected Ju Yuanzi’s reaction. He had been too shocked previously, which had affected his judgment. Now he understood that the demonic aura was not real, but rather existed at the level of “intent”.

Because of his special supernatural eyes and unique state of mind, Ji Yuan was able to trigger that terrifying demonic aura when he had activated the demonic feather, but this was definitely no hallucination.

Ju Yuanzi knew that Ji Yuan was definitely not lying to him, but despite his best efforts, trying every means and even borrowing the feather from Ji Yuan to infuse it with spiritual energy and power, observing it closely, he still found nothing. After trying for a long time, he had no choice but to give up.

After returning the feather to Ji Yuan, Ju Yuanzi asked curiously.

“Sir, do you know what kind of demonic beast this feather is from?”

Ji Yuan actually did not know the answer to this question; although he had a bold guess in his mind, it was only a guess.

“That remains to be seen. Perhaps it’s just a strange beast with unique innate abilities, or perhaps…”

Ji Yuan’s gaze returned to the slowly rising sun, and Ju Yuanzi followed his gaze. However, the latter did not think much of it, assuming that Ji Yuan was simply subconsciously looking at the sun.1

“Perhaps I’m overthinking it. In any case, this feather is not as simple as it seems on the surface. I also need to study it carefully.”

Ji Yuan really wanted to try burning the feather with the True Fire of Samadhi, but he only had the one feather. If the feather couldn’t withstand the fire and was burned to ashes, where would he find a second one?

However, one of the components of the True Fire of Samadhi was Yang fire. Taken alone, it certainly could not compare to the legendary True Fire of the Sun. However, if he used the power of the complete True Fire of Samadhi and transformed it into extreme Yang, it might be possible to simulate it.

‘Should I try it…? But it’ll be gone if it’s burned…’

Until a more suitable method were to be found, reason forced Ji Yuan to continue hesitating.

“Roar—”

A loud sound came from afar, and all the cultivators on the ship, including Ji Yuan, looked in the direction of the sound. In the distance, a long serpentine shadow was churning in the clouds.

“A dragon’s roar? A flood dragon?”

The flying boat made no attempt to change direction, sailing straight through the clouds. The flood dragon off to the side continued to churn in the clouds, emitting roars. At its closest, the two were less than a hundred feet apart.

The dragon was about ten zhang long and as thick as a large water vat.2 Its four claws gripped the clouds as if gripping the soft ground rather than fog.

“Roar—”

This dragon seemed very small compared to the Xuanxin Mansion’s flying ship. The passengers on the boat were all looking at the dragon, some with curiosity, some with amazement, and some with fear.

The flood dragon was also watching the immortal flying boat soar past with its golden sails. It knew that this was not something to be trifled with.

“Master, what is that flood dragon doing? Why is it constantly rolling around in the clouds and roaring?”

One cultivator asked their elder.

“It’s probably a courtship. There’s a saying that dragons are inherently lustful. Although this doesn’t apply to all dragons, dragons are indeed the most mate-obsessed and the most prone to seeking pleasure among monsters. They also love to reproduce. In fact, this is directly tied to the prosperity of the dragon race.”

“Oh…”

The voices of an unfamiliar master and disciple drifted over from not too far away. Ji Yuan couldn’t help but smile, thinking to himself that they had truly never meet the Old Bull. He might not be the most prone of all monsters to seek pleasure, but he was definitely in the top three.

“Attention fellow Taoists, the flying ship is about to enter the region of ​​the Heavenly Storm up ahead. Please do not fly out of the protection range of the flying ship’s array.”

The cultivators of Xuanxin Mansion used mental transmission throughout every corner of the flying ship, warning of potential dangers.

The current concept of the Four Seas on the map did not originate from the immortals’ definitions of territories, but rather from the dragon race.

In earlier times, apart from some locations with celestial storms and desolate ocean currents, the boundaries of other regions were rather vague, and sometimes dragons would fight each other over the control of the territory.

Thus, about a thousand years ago, some True Dragons gathered in the vast ocean and defined the boundaries of the four seas. The Xuanxin Mansion’s flying ship was now flying over the Eastern Sea.

The Eastern Cloud Continent was also known as the Eastern Continent, and was entirely surrounded by the vast Eastern Sea. To this day, the coastal countries of Cloud Continent simply referred to the ocean as either “the Great Sea” or as the “Eastern Sea.” Some countries that were not on the eastern coast also used this name due to ancient traditions and memories passed down.

At this moment, the flying ship was about to pass through a region of turbulent sea winds. Looking ahead, the people on board could see nothing but a hazy gray expanse, with waves higher than normal ocean areas, and the seawater appeared murky.

The flying ship was gradually descending, and soon its hull touched the sea surface, transitioning from flying in the air to sailing on the sea.

“Whoosh…whoosh…whoosh…”

The surrounding wind grew increasingly turbulent, its sound carrying a sharp edge. Bright lights illuminated the flying ship, resembling a hazy, luminous egg, standing out starkly against the gray, desolate ocean currents.

“Crash… crash…”

Huge waves surged around the ship, causing it to pitch back and forth. However, the rocking was very regular, and while it rose and fell, it didn’t sway in all directions. While one couldn’t claim to feel nothing on the ship, it remained quite stable, and even the soup in the restaurant didn’t spill.

Many mortals had retreated back into their cabins, but some cultivators were still watching from the deck, including Ji Yuan.

He was actually quite concerned about his own safety, so even though his current cultivation level could withstand the winds, he rarely ventured out into the outer layer of gales. There wasn’t much scenery there, and unlike some people, he didn’t need to use the celestial storm to cultivate.

However, watching from the ship at this moment was rather safe. The power of the Realm-Crossing Ferry was indeed incredible. As the ship moved forward, backward, left, and right, the fierce winds were broken apart by an invisible force and stabilized. The ocean surface rose and fell, but there was always a wave touching the bottom of the ship.

By the second night in the storm, the surrounding sea had turned completely dark.

“Crash… crash… whoosh…”

“Boom…”

Besides the turbulent winds and towering waves, a torrential downpour was also underway, with lightning occasionally illuminating the dark sea.

The starlight in the sky was completely obscured, and the flying ship bobbed alone in the storm.

Ji Yuan stood alone at the stern of the ship, watching the thunderstorm amidst the chaotic gale. This thunderstorm was also unusual, which was why the thunderclouds had not been shattered by the winds, although the aura of the lightning itself was not particularly special.

“Roar…”

Just then, a dragon’s roar suddenly sounded from the distance behind the ship. With Ji Yuan’s exceptional hearing, he immediately recognized it as the same dragon who had been courting before.

“Roo…Roo…”

“Boom…”

“Whoosh…”

A chaotic mix of sounds mingled in the gales, accompanied by thunderous booms and strange howls.

“Roar…”

As the dragon’s roar drew nearer, many cultivators on the flying ship gathered on the stern deck, gazing into the distance in the storm that obscured their vision and senses.

“Was that the dragon from before?”

“It seems so, but I wonder what happened?”

“I hear sounds of fighting!”

“Could it be that the courtship attracted other male dragons to fight?”

Such discussions were endless. Ji Yuan looked solemnly behind him, as did Ju Yuanzi. Two elderly men, one with black hair and the other with white hair, stood not far from Ju Yuanzi, their expressions equally grave. The old man with black hair spoke to the crowd around him.

“That dragon seems to be in big trouble.”

Ji Yuan could naturally sense this as well, because he had dealt with the dragons many times. He could even distinguish that the dragon’s roar, which sounded like a cow’s mooing, almost became a wail on several occasions.

As the cultivators on the flying ship looked back, the dragon in the distance, who had been in despair, suddenly spotted a glimmer of immortal light in the chaos. He immediately thought of the realm-crossing ferry that had passed by not too long ago.

“I am the Crimson Dragon under the Dragon Lord’s command! Immortals ahead, save me! Immortals, save me! Roar… Roo…“

The dragon’s roar echoed, startling the cultivators on the flying ship. They hadn’t expected the dragon to ask them for help.

While others were still discussing, Ji Yuan didn’t hesitate. There were more Dragon Lords than just the Old Dragon, so what if there truly was another one? He waved his hand and gently pointed forward. The Green Vine Sword faintly appeared before flashing away, shooting into the distance behind the ship.

This series of sudden changes startled the two old cultivators not far away; they could see the astonishment in each other’s eyes as they looked at each other.

‘An Immortal Sword?’

Swish–

A bright sword light overwhelmed the lightning, and the gale and sea behind the flying boat split in two in the next instant, forming two magnificent walls of wind and sea, as if endless air currents and seawater were rolled back to either side.

“Clang…”

The sound of a sword clashing echoed back from afar in the next instant.

I am NOT good with horror but for some reason I decided to scar myself by looking into that game. Good game. Definitely not for me.
