Chapter 479: The Country of Daxiu

Seeing that the dust had settled, the old beggar offered a suggestion.

“Mr. Ji, they’ve stopped fighting over there. They were just venting their anger with a fight, and with people from Nine Peaks Mountain around, they definitely won’t be able to start arguing anymore. Let’s go too; there’s no need to run into them.”

Even as he said these, the two factions and the cultivators from Nine Peaks Mountain had already noticed Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

Originally, their two auras were hardly noticeable. The fierce battle with its raging winds and lightning made them difficult to spot. Now that the clouds had dispersed and the sky had cleared, a small white cloud that hadn’t dispersed with them became more conspicuous, and it was hard not to notice the two people standing atop it.

“Grandmaster Uncle! Grandmaster Uncle!”

Before the old beggar could even ride away on his cloud, shouts came from afar, and two groups of people approached on spiritual artifacts.

Led by the elder, the cultivators of the Qianyuan Sect approached the old beggar and Ji Yuan, bowing respectfully.

“This junior of the Qianyuan Sect greets Grandmaster Uncle!”

The old beggar turned away, refusing the bow, glanced at them, and waved his hand.

“Oh dear, please don’t bow to an old beggar like me. I am not worthy of the Qianyuan Sect. You must have mistaken me for someone else. Mr. Ji, look at them. They are dressed so well, they are even more handsome than you. What am I, an old beggar? You must have mistaken me for someone else.”

The old man leading the Qianyuan Sect straightened up, looked at the old beggar and then at Ji Yuan, and spoke with a wry smile.

“Grandmaster Uncle, after all these years, you still hold a grudge? Grandmaster…”

“No…no, no, Mr. Ji, let’s go. I should have disappeared earlier.”

The old beggar turned around with a flick of his sleeve, preparing to ride the cloud away, but found that the cloud was not moving. He immediately looked at Ji Yuan and saw that Ji Yuan had just retracted a finger that had just been gesturing in the air. He immediately knew that Ji Yuan had tampered with the cloud.

Ji Yuan smiled slightly, looking at the old beggar, and readily admitted it.

“Indeed, I froze the cloud. So, Elder Lu is from the Qianyuan Sect? I must sincerely bow and ask to speak with them for a few more minutes before leaving.”

In fact, Ji Yuan was completely unfamiliar with the Qianyuan Sect. The fact that the old beggar had never mentioned his sect before seemed to indicate that there was quite the story behind it.

“What method did you use?”

The old beggar was even more surprised by Ji Yuan’s methods; just now, Ji Yuan claimed to have frozen the cloud, but he hadn’t noticed Ji Yuan’s spiritual power penetrating it at all. After struggling for a bit with his spiritual energy, the cloud returned to his control. It seemed that Ji Yuan had only blocked it for a moment and didn’t intend to keep obstructing it.

The old beggar deliberately ignored the people from the Qianyuan Sect and tried to change the subject, to no avail.

“Thank you for persuading my grandmaster, fellow Taoist.”

The cultivators from the Qianyuan Sect first thanked Ji Yuan, and then bowed sincerely to the old beggar again.

“Grandmaster Uncle, although the Grandmaster didn’t explicitly say this, he truly hopes that you can return to the sect. Since you’ve come to the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly, why don’t you come back with us after it’s over? Even just briefly visiting the sect would be good!”

He hadn’t expected a friendly response from the old beggar, but the latter suddenly turned around and looked at the old man.

“Wait, what did you just say?”

The elder of the Qianyuan Sect was slightly taken aback, but then a look of delight appeared on his face.

“I said, our Grandmaster didn’t explicitly say this, but…”

“No, no, the sentence before that!”

“the sentence before?”

The elder of the Qianyuan Sect frowned and thought for a moment, then hesitated before looking at Ji Yuan.

“I thanked this fellow Taoist.”

The old beggar clapped his hands with a loud “smack,” glanced at Ji Yuan, who looked completely bewildered, and then back at the elder of the Qianyuan Sect.

“What did you call me?”

“Grandmaster Uncle.”

“And him?”

“Uh, fellow Taoist?”

“Haha, that’s right!”

The old beggar chuckled, then immediately gave him a stern reprimand.

“You’re being disrespectful, calling him that! Haha, Mr. Ji, I’m sorry, these juniors have lowered your seniority. You won’t hold it against them, will you?”

Ji Yuan paused for a moment, then shook his head with a smile. This old beggar was quite the character; he could even take a title so seriously.

“Oh, do as you please. I don’t have any particular thoughts about my form of address. We are fellow cultivators, so calling each other ‘fellow Taoist’ is perfectly acceptable.”

“Hmm, look at this, this is the awareness of a senior!”

The old beggar felt he had gotten the better deal from Ji Yuan, and his mood improved considerably. Regardless of whether Ji Yuan was genuinely indifferent or pretending, he was happy nonetheless.

When in a good mood, it was easier to say a few kind words — or at least, his words wouldn’t be too harsh.

“Alright, you bunch of little brats, you’ve been making a ruckus here, but you’ve made some progress. Just now, Mr. Ji praised you and the cultivators from Great Wind Valley for your amazing wind-control techniques. That carries significant weight. Go back early, Mr. Ji and I will take our leave first.”

“Oh, Grandmaster Uncle…”

The cultivator of the Qianyuan Sect subconsciously reached out his hands, but the spiritual cloud had already transformed into a streak of light and vanished into the distance, faster than flying on a sword.

Above the white clouds, Ji Yuan looked at the old beggar. Though he spoke coldly to the cultivators of the Qianyuan Sect, in reality he was kind-hearted and still cared for them.

Ji Yuan felt that even if he hadn’t been there, the old beggar would still have come to the beach, ostensibly to watch the spectacle, but in reality he must have also been concerned about the younger generation, and worried that the fight would go too far.

The white cloud drifted for a long time, and neither Ji Yuan nor the old beggar spoke a word as they flew. After a while, the old beggar finally spoke softly.

“Mr. Ji, do you have any questions for me?”

Ji Yuan did not look at the old beggar, his gaze fixed on the landscape below the clouds. Although it was rather blurry to Ji Yuan, he had long developed his own method of distinguishing objects, starting with patterns and then the gathering concentrations of Qi. Even high in the sky, he could make out things with about 80 or 90 percent accuracy.

At this moment, amidst the chaotic scene below, even more fiery vital energy was rising. Combined with some regular and iconic features, he could basically determine that this was a human settlement.

“What country is below?”

The old beggar glanced at Ji Yuan, then looked down. Since Ji Yuan wasn’t going to mention what happened before, he was happy to let it go. He carefully examined the scenery before speaking.

“Although I’m not very familiar with this place, I do know some well-known mortal kingdoms. Judging from the geographical location, the one below should be the country of Daxiu, one of the major dynasties in the southern part of the Heng Region in the Northern Territories.”

The old beggar, fearing that Ji Yuan might not have fully understood, added another sentence.

“It is quite different from Dazhen in the Eastern Cloud Continent. Although the common people are similar, there are true cultivators in charge of the dynasty, and the upper echelons of the country even have some connection with the divine path. This is already quite remarkable among human dynasties.”

Ji Yuan nodded in realization.

“Oh, so this is the country of Daxiu! Does Elder Lu know who the Imperial Preceptor of the Daxiu is now?”1

The old beggar chuckled dismissively.

“I was just saying that Daxiu was quite remarkable, but Mr. Ji, you shouldn’t take it too seriously. With our level of cultivation, what does it matter how great an imperial dynasty’s hegemony is? Of course, I don’t know who the Imperial Preceptor is, nor is there any need for me to know.”

“Indeed. Mr. Lu, you should go back first. I’ll go somewhere on the way. I know the way to Nine Peaks Sacred Cavern, so I won’t get lost.”

Ji Yuan left behind these words, then took a step towards the clouds. His body slowly floated down. After drifting down several dozen feet, a gentle breeze swept past his feet, carrying Ji Yuan towards a town below.

“What are you going to do, Mr. Ji? Is it something secret? Would it be convenient for this old beggar to follow you?”

“Come if you want.”

“You could have just said so earlier, why did you have to ride the wind yourself?”

The old beggar chuckled and quickly followed. He dispersed the white clouds and also rode the wind.

Ji Yuan originally just wanted to go to a nearby large city to ask for some information, but as he descended on the wind, a thought stirred in his mind. He turned towards the wilderness in the distance and changed the wind direction in an instant, carrying himself and the old beggar towards what he sensed.

The old beggar frowned slightly, glancing at the direction of the city and then at the distant horizon ahead, a thoughtful expression on his face, but he said nothing.

Before long, Ji Yuan could hear the sound of horses’ hooves, and saw dust rising from the distant wilderness, clearly indicating that many riders were moving swiftly. The old beggar had also noticed something.

Outside Pozi Mountain, in Pozi Forest, two hundred riders were galloping at full speed on tall horses. These riders were all in high spirits, dressed in fine clothes and riding energetic steeds. They wore leather armor inlaid with rings of iron, and carried bows, arrows, and long spears. Leading them was a military official in gleaming silver chainmail.

“Clip-clop-clip-clop-clip-clop…”

“Clop, clop, clop…”

The thunderous sound of hooves pounded the ground, causing a slight tremor, and the general’s powerful roar echoed throughout the entire army.

“Giddy up…giddy up…hey…everyone keep up, don’t let them get away!

“You, take your men to the right flank! You, go further forward and set up the net formation!”

“Yes, sir!”

“Yes, sir!”

The riders split into two groups, flanking from the left and right.

The military officer looked ahead and sneered. He took his exquisite bow from behind his back and drew an arrow from the quiver hanging on the horse’s side.

“Trying to run? Impossible!”

The military officer maintained his balance by gripping the horse’s flanks with his legs, drew the bow to its fullest extent, and channeled his inner Qi into the arrow. After briefly aiming the bow, the arrow veered slightly to the left before being released immediately.

“Go!”

“Thwang!”

“Whoosh…”

The sounds of the bowstring snapping and the arrow tearing through the air rang out simultaneously. The arrow shot forward like a meteor, carrying a faint glimmer of light.

“Aaah-“

The next moment, a sharp scream rang out from a hundred zhang ahead.

The military officer’s expression remained cold and unchanged. He had already nocked another arrow on the bowstring, and after drawing the bow to its fullest extent, he aimed slightly to the left before releasing the arrow.

“Go!”

“Thwang!”

“Whoosh…”

“Aaaah-“

As expected, another scream rang out from ahead…

For reference, see ch. 336

Chapter 480: Enemies Meet On a Narrow Road

After hitting the target with two arrows, the military officer drew a third arrow, nocked it on the bowstring, and drew the bowstring back to its fullest extent.

“Whoosh!”

The third arrow was even more powerful, shooting towards a white fox on the left-hand side of the fox pack, chasing after it like a shooting star.

“No!”

As the white fox cried out in alarm, its demonic energy surged, and its form blurred as it narrowly dodged the arrow. It embedded itself in the ground, leaving only a small section of the feathered fletching.

The fox pack was already in a state of extreme panic. All they knew was to run and get away from the mounted soldiers behind them.

The leading military officer fired three arrows in quick succession. The first two arrows hit two foxes, and the third arrow was dodged. However, the foxes’ fear intensified; the officer’s arrows were all fired to the left, so the foxes instinctively fled to the right.

The sound of horses’ hooves clattered and shook the earth. After the fox pack had fled, two agile knights from the rear leaned forward, reached down, and snatched the two foxes that had been struck by arrows as they rode past.

“General, what excellent archery skills! Both of them were killed with a single arrow, giving the monsters no chance to use their magic.”

“Hmph, they’re already exhausted. Even if they use demonic techniques, our military formation can handle them. Today, we’ll take them all down! Giddy up!”

Ahead, pursued by the cavalry, were a group of around thirty to forty foxes, mainly red and gray, thought there was one rare white fox as well.

The cavalry behind them pressed closer, firing arrows every now and then. Their aim was not to kill the foxes, but to drive them in a suitable direction. The military officer, however, did not fire another arrow after his third shot missed.

“Hahaha…”

“Sister, what should we do?”

“Hmm… I don’t know either, I don’t know…”

“Just run! Escape to Pozi Mountain, deep into the mountains. The horses can’t catch us there. We can go through ravines and caves!”

“Ah!”

Another fox was shot by a soldier’s arrow. It cried out in pain and tried to continue running with its limp, but its speed was greatly reduced. It could only watch helplessly as other foxes passed by and disappeared into the distance.

“No! Save me, save me, please save me! Don’t leave me behind!”

In this situation, the fox pack was barely able to survive, so who would stop? And even if they did stop, who could save it?

The foxes gritted their teeth and refused to look at their lagging companion. Everyone knew what would happen if they were caught. Soon, the fox pack crossed a small hill as they escaped. However, in the next moment, another squadron of the cavalry had already surrounded them, suddenly attacked.

“Die!”

“Slay these demons!”

“You demons, prepare to die!”

“Haah!”

“Ah!”

“They’re here too!”

Forty to fifty riders charged in, catching the fox pack off guard. Many foxes were struck by the sweeping blades, and some were even pinned to the ground by spears.

The entrance to the mountain was right in front of them. All the foxes that were fleeing forward tried their best to escape in that direction, with all their attention focused on the pursuers behind them and on their right flank. However, as they charged forward, unexpected changes occurred.

“Ding ding… Ding ding…”

As a series of bells rang, sturdy nets rose from the ground at the crossroads ahead, and descended from all directions, catching almost all the foxes in one fell swoop.

…

The thunderous sound of hooves grew louder and louder in Ji Yuan’s ears. He and the old beggar rode the wind through the sky and soon approached the place where the stampede of the cavalry had kicked up dust. A strong military aura rose in his vision, while a demonic aura rose ahead of the cavalry.

“Are they chasing demons?”

The old beggar could naturally see the demonic aura ahead, and he could also tell that there were more than just a few demons. It seemed to be a whole group of them. He stroked his short, disheveled white beard and spoke.

“It’s not too strange for something like this to appear in a great dynasty like Daxiu. Mr. Ji, these soldiers all have a strong aura of bloodlust, clearly seasoned veterans. They are all full of vigor and extraordinarily skilled. No wonder they can chase and kill the demonic beasts.”

Ji Yuan frowned and remained silent, simply using the wind to lower his altitude. The cavalry below had gradually calmed down from their charge, and the two hundred or so riders slowed down and converged on the location where they had captured the fox pack.

Everywhere he looked, he could hear the heavy breathing of horses. Riders dismounted one after another, and everyone was in high spirits.

“Hahahaha… We’ve been chasing you for so long, and you’ve finally been caught! You dared to use your sorcery to wreak havoc in Bianrong, and even committed murder. Even if you are demons, you still can’t escape!”

The leading military officer, carrying a bow and arrows and wielding a long spear, walked step by step to the group of foxes caught in the net. Small copper bells hung from the knots in the sturdy net, making soft ringing sounds as the net swayed.

The military officer scanned the surroundings. The foxes that were not trapped in the net had already been caught or killed. It was unlikely that any foxes had escaped, or if any had, they could not have been among the pack they had been pursuing, but rather were those that had escaped at the very beginning.

The military officer beckoned to the side, and immediately a personal guard presented him with a scroll document. Holding the document, the officer walked up to the foxes, who were too terrified to utter a sound, and slowly unfurled the scroll.

“A fox demon has been using its power to harm people in Bianrong Prefecture, resulting in the deaths of three officials and six civilians. It was apprehended by the Heavenly Masters and, after divination, was identified as Luo Xiaoqiu, the fox demon of Kumu Grave.1 Such acts of violence are unforgiveable. By order of the prefect of Bianrong Prefecture and the Heavenly Masters, the fox demon must be eradicated and Kumu Grave leveled.”

The officer slowly put away the document in his hand and looked again at the group of trapped fox demons, his gaze lingering on the white fox among them.

“Hmph, your pelts are quite nice. Executing you all and then offering up the furs would certainly be a worth a small achievement.”

Even though the foxes were terrified, they knew that this was their last chance, and begged for mercy.

“We’re innocent! General, we’re innocent!”

“General, we don’t know any Luo Xiaoqiu at all. There’s no such fox in Kumu Grave!”

“General, please spare us! We have never done anything immoral!”

“We would never dare to harm anyone! We beg the general to investigate!”

“Don’t kill us! Don’t kill us!”

The foxes kept begging for mercy, struggling to break free. Some even scratched at the ropes of the net with their claws and gnawed at them with their teeth, but they couldn’t budge. The copper bells on them swayed slightly, making their teeth and claws ache, but even so they dared not stop.

The surrounding soldiers were all armed and ready for battle.

“Choose the right spot to strike, but don’t damage the pelts.”

“Yes, sir!”

“Understood!”

The military officer gave the order to his subordinates, then sneered at the group of foxes again.

“Stop crying about injustice! What kind of demons are good? Even commoners in Daxiu who commit serious crimes are punished along with their relatives, let alone you fox demons! Blame Luo Xiaoqiu if you must!”

The old beggar and Ji Yuan happened to land nearby at that moment and spotted the foxes that had been killed in the chase earlier, as well as the remaining twenty-odd foxes trapped in the net.

The two had already concealed their presence and hidden their forms. In addition, Ji Yuan used the energy from the Great Void Jade Talisman2 in his hand, making it impossible for the fox demon and all the soldiers to sense their presence.

“What do you think, Mr. Ji?”

After all, one shouldn’t harm one’s family. Even though the old beggar didn’t have much affection for demons, he wouldn’t mind doing something if these foxes were truly innocent. But to say that these foxes were completely pure wasn’t entirely true.

At least, these foxes weren’t telling the whole truth. They said they had never done anything immoral, which was obviously a lie. After all, the aura of demonic energy couldn’t fool anyone, at least not the old beggar or Ji Yuan. But the intensity of the aura wasn’t that serious, either; at most, they had merely bewitched mortal men to have sex with them and stolen some of their vital energy.

Ji Yuan squinted and looked for a while, then suddenly shifted his gaze and spoke.

“It’s not our place to interfere.”

Upon hearing this, the old beggar felt something amiss, andturned to look towards the direction of the Pozi Mountain forest. At that moment, a woman in white appeared, strolling toward them while carrying a gray fox in her arms. She lowered her head and gently stroked the fox’s fur as she approached the mountain path entrance, her movements seemingly slow but actually rather swift.

“It seems enemies meet on a narrow road.”

Ji Yuan remarked casually, glancing down at his right sleeve. It seemed the Great Void Jade Talisman was indeed wondrous; even the newcomer couldn’t see through it.

The old beggar looked at the newcomer with interest, but instead of asking any questions, he waited quietly to see how things would unfold.

On a desolate mountainside, a woman in white holding a fox appeared. Something seemed amiss, and the soldiers, of course, were not fools.

“Halt! Who goes there?”

At the soldier’s shout, the woman stood abruptly still, then slowly raised her head and smiled slightly at the crowd.

This was the kind of smile that could topple a city; all the soldiers, including the military officer, were stunned at that moment.

‘Is this Tu Siyan’s true form? Or is it yet another transformation?’

As Ji Yuan was pondering this, the woman seemed to ignore the distance and instantly walked to the edge of the large net trapping the foxes. With a gentle swipe of her hand, the entire net was torn apart, and the foxes were freed.

“Thank you, Immortal Master! Thank you, Immortal Master!”

“Thank you, Immortal Master!”

“Thank you, Immortal Master, for saving our lives!”

All the foxes gathered around and bowed to the woman, which amused her.

“Ah ha…calling me an immortal master? An immortal master wouldn’t do this…”

As the woman spoke, she reached out and grasped a handful of glowing silk threads from thin air, each thread connected to one of the soldiers.

“Whoosh!”

“Whoosh!”

“Whoosh!”

…

Black smoke billowed from all the soldiers’ bodies; their protective amulets had burned away.

“Anyone who dares to hunt down my fox clan shall die!”

Just as the woman was about to tug at the silk threads, she suddenly heard a series of snapping sounds. Then her hand loosened, and all the silk threads broke. The old beggar had made his move.

枯木 (kūmù): dead tree, 冢 (zhǒng): burial mound

Reminder that Qiu Feng gave him this item back in ch. 248

Chapter 481: Eight-Tailed Demonic Fox Tu Siyan

The moment all the threads snapped, the woman in white realized something was terribly wrong.

‘This is a trap!?’

Countless thoughts flashed through the white-clad woman’s mind. Perhaps the soldiers’ attempt to capture the foxes of Kumu Grave was a ruse, their real intention being to lure her out.

The woman glanced at the gray fox in her arms out of the corner of her eye, thinking that it was unlikely that the fox had betrayed her, or that she herself might not even know that something had been done to her.

Although her mind was racing, the woman in white showed no sign of panic on the surface. Instead, she revealed a charming smile and looked at the old beggar, who had revealed himself through his attack.

“Oh dear, does the Heavenly Master position in Daxiu not provide clothes? Why is the Immortal Master dressed so shabby? Hahaha…”

As she spoke, the woman in white carefully observed the man who looked like an old beggar, finding his aura to be obscure and unfathomable.

‘Does the department of Heavenly Masters have such a person? The Imperial Preceptor doesn’t resemble him either, or perhaps…’

Now that the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly was about to be held, many eminent immortals would be coming to Nine Peaks Mountain. Could this be one of them? It was a possibility that could not be ruled out.

It wasn’t until the old beggar waved his hand and cut all the silk threads that the group of soldiers in the grand show suddenly woke up as if from a dream, their bodies trembling as they came to their senses.

The leading military officer immediately dismissed his soldiers.

“Everyone, retreat! Retreat! Don’t look at this demon! Don’t look at her!”

The military officer not only commanded but also led by example. He slowly stepped back a distance, not daring to look at the woman in white, while also bowing to the old beggar to express his gratitude.

“Thank you so much, Immortal Master, for saving us. Otherwise, my brothers and I would have surely fallen victim to this demon’s tricks!”

The old beggar simply waved his hand at them, focusing his attention on the woman in white. From Ji Yuan’s earlier murmured words, it wasn’t hard to guess that he knew this demon.

Hearing the woman speak in a seductive tone, the old beggar simply smiled.

“Haha, you don’t need to guess anymore. I have nothing to do with this country of Daxiu, and I’m certainly not a so-called immortal master from the department of Heavenly Masters. I thought I was just here to watch the show, but I never expected to meet such a powerful demon as you.”

The woman squinted at the old beggar.

“Oh? Immortal Master, you’ve come to watch the show? You think watching human soldiers kill my poor fox clan juniors is a show?”

“Heh, you’re sharp-tongued! You don’t have to be so confrontational. These foxes aren’t exactly clean themselves, and how do you know I wouldn’t make a move? And look at you, you’re ready to kill all these soldiers with just one sentence: ‘Anyone who dares to hunt down my fox clan shall die,’ how do you explain that? According to your logic, although I am a celestial cultivator, I am still a human. Can I also claim ‘anyone who dares to hunt down my fellow humans shall die’?”

The old beggar maintained his smile throughout, neither frowning nor speaking sarcastically, but his words never allowed the woman in front of him to gain the slightest advantage.

‘This old beggar might not be able to out-argue Ji Yuan, but can’t I out-argue you, you demon?’

The woman in white swayed slightly and said helplessly.

“Then what can I do? I can’t argue with you, Immortal Master. I can only leave it to you to decide whether to kill me or torture me, or… anything else is fine too. I, Siyan, am willing to do anything…”

The soldiers around her stared blankly, their faces filled with infatuation. Even the military officer was no exception, and they all subconsciously moved closer to the woman, with wisps of smoke rising from their bodies.

“You monstrous fiend!”

Enraged that someone dared to make a move right under his nose, the old beggar immediately swung his hand towards the woman, using a mountain-suppressing technique to gather immense pressure around her, making even the air around the woman in white thick and difficult for her to move through.

“Smack…”

The old beggar grabbed the woman’s throat and hissed.

“Quit the act!”

“Ah…”

The woman screamed, her limbs trembling, as if she could not withstand the immense pressure. First, she knelt down, then she braced her palms on the ground, and then she prostrated herself, gradually curling up.

A few breaths later, the woman transformed into a white fox, pinned to the ground by the old beggar. The old beggar didn’t even need to lift a finger to deal with all the surrounding foxes; they were already suffocating under the pressure of the Mountain-Suppressing Technique and lay dead on the ground, as if a mountain was pressing down on their heads.

Just as the old beggar was about to laugh out loud, he suddenly frowned.

“Something’s not right! Something feels off!”

A glance swept across the fox pack, and sure enough, the white fox that had been there was gone. Looking to the side, Ji Yuan was nowhere to be seen either.

“Hmph!”

The old beggar snorted coldly, patted his waist, and a tattered cloth bag flew into his hand, which he then tossed aside.

“Whoosh…whoosh…”

A gray, hazy wind blew by, and all the foxes on the ground, aside from the dead ones, were collected into the bag and disappeared. The old beggar then tossed the white fox in his hand into the sack, and the next moment he himself turned into a gust of light and disappeared.

“Ugh…”

“What’s wrong?”

“What happened?”

“Where are the foxes?”

The two hundred-plus soldiers finally came to their senses again, looking at each other in bewilderment as they stared at the dead foxes that remained on the ground.

“General?”

The military officer waved his hand, his expression grave as he scanned the surroundings.

“We shouldn’t stay here any longer. The monster we just encountered was extremely powerful. the Immortal Master said that we as battle-hardened soldiers possess a strong, violent aura, and with the addition of protective talismans, we can restrain ordinary demons and evil spirits. However, we were powerless against that female demon. It would be best to leave now!”

“But what about the immortal masters who appeared before?”

“They’re gods, we’re just mortals. Let’s take care of ourselves first, so as not to hold them back. Let’s take the dead foxes with us and go!”

“Yes, sir!”

Even though the demon seemed to have left, a faint unease lingered in everyone’s hearts. This was not because they were cowardly, but rather a lingering effect of their earlier disturbances; their very spirits had been frightened.

Carrying the fox corpses, the soldiers rushed to the area where the horses had been tied, only to find that all the horses had fallen to the ground. A soldier checked their breathing and pulse and confirmed that they were dead.

“General, all our horses are dead!”

“Let’s go! Run! Get out of the mountains quickly!”

Feeling that the surrounding situation was becoming increasingly strange, and with all their protective talismans burned, the general lost much of his confidence and became somewhat frightened. With a wave of his hand, he led his men to run.

…

Tu Siyan’s decisive abandonment of the fox clan, escaping with only the gray fox Jin Chan in her arms, was for good reason. The old beggar’s skill was already unfathomable, but in addition, she felt a faint sense of crisis lingering in her heart. Her mind was clear, and she was much more sensitive than ordinary demons. As soon as she sensed something was wrong, she immediately began to think of a plan to escape.

Even though it seemed as though she had escaped, the unease in her heart hadn’t dissipated.

At this moment, Ji Yuan was already high in the sky, his full divine vision locked on Tu Siyan’s aura. He held the Green Vine Sword in his hand, hesitating several times whether to strike. He didn’t want this fox to escape. The immobilization spell would definitely not be very effective against such a slippery demon; on the contrary, it would reduce the psychological pressure that Ji Yuan exerted on the fox demon. Although the True Fire of Samadhi was domineering, it might not be able to hit her. The Immortal Sword was invincible, but he didn’t want to kill her with a single strike.

It wasn’t that Ji Yuan felt sorry for her, but rather that he wanted to know what this mischievous fox was up to, and whether it was related to the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern or her own affairs. On the other hand, since the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern did have nine-tailed foxes, and they might even be a male and a female pair, Ji Yuan still had some reservations.

Perhaps he could use a move from the Heavenly Inclined Sword Stance he had comprehended to deliver a Heart-Severing sword strike!

“You demon, where do you think you’re going!”

The old beggar’s voice boomed like thunder, shaking the mountains and fields. A series of crystal-like lights appeared around him, which then turned into ripples. The forests of Pozi Mountain looked as though a great river were flowing through them, except that this river was composed of light and air.

“Light flows at my will, original essence within my grasp, now show your power!”1

The old beggar was clearly furious. He had let the fox escape unnoticed, which was a loss of face in front of Ji Yuan. As the old beggar always said, he usually didn’t care about face at all and would even beg from ordinary mortals with a broken bowl. But with Ji Yuan, whom he knew to a certain extent, this was unacceptanble.

The flowing light, like a river, washed over the entire landscape, penetrating every crevice like water, while eerie lights rose on the other side of the mountain.

“Old beggar, you really think I’m afraid of you? Roar—”

The sharp fox cry swelled into a roar like a giant beast, and a sharp claw swept towards a mountain peak.

“Boom…”

The mountain exploded and the old beggar soared into the air, and several more enormous tails swept past.

“Good heavens! An eight-tailed fox demon!?”

The old beggar exclaimed in surprise, sensing a faint sharpness. He knew that Ji Yuan was nearby and that the immortal sword might already be in his hand. He immediately transmitted his voice through his aura.

“Mr. Ji, stay your hand! Let me deal with her!”

With that, the old beggar waved his arms in the air, creating a large gust of wind that lifted him into the air, dodging the sweeping fox tails. He then stretched out the five fingers of one hand, forming an aura that merged with the surrounding mountains, and slammed it down hard.

Whoosh…

The surrounding light parted, revealing Tu Siyan, her body still humanoid and clad in white, with eight enormous tails extending from her back.

Just as Tu Siyan recovered from the dazzling light, the old beggar’s palm strike came crashing down like a mountain, aiming for one of her tails.

“Boom…”

“Ugh…”

Tu Siyan felt a tingling numbness in half her body, and one of her tails drooped to the ground, unable to be lifted. Only then did she realize that the old beggar in front of her was far more powerful than she had imagined.

“Old beggar, I’ve already let those humans go. What more do you want? Do you really want to fight me to the death?”

Two rosy spots of blush rose on Tu Siyan’s fair face, her eyes gleamed with a mysterious light, and her eyes and thin eyebrows appeared even longer. Her fingernails grew sharp.

A flash of pinkish-white light appeared on her abdomen, and Tu Siyan, who originally had weak demonic energy, suddenly unleashed a surge of demonic power.

“Roar–“

With a stomp of her foot, her remaining giant tail thrashed wildly, and her sharp claws lashed out repeatedly.

Rumble… Rumble…

“I’ll shatter your mountain, let’s see how you use its power then!”

The slope of Pozi Mountain crumbled into a landslide, dust billowing everywhere, even obscuring the light from the old beggar’s technique. The earthquake shook the surrounding mountains, causing several peaks to tremble.

“Hmph, it’s nothing more than an eight-tailed fox demon!”

“You’re quite arrogant. This Eight-Tailed Fox could take your life!”

Rumble…

The collapse of the mountain range spread outward from the center of the battle between the two sides, intensifying as it expanded. In the center, the old beggar remained standing, the mountain trembling but not collapsing, even as the outer perimeter began to crumble.

“Oh no, there are still residents in the mountains! Protect the town!”

Suddenly, the old beggar saw someone in the mountains. His anger flashed and he disappeared in an instant. His whole body surged with spiritual power as he rapidly formed hand-seals to cast a mountain-suppressing technique to stabilize Pozi Mountain.

“Hahahaha… An immortal is indeed an immortal! Let’s see if you can keep yourself in check!”

Tu Siyan laughed wildly. She was at a disadvantage in a direct fight with the old beggar, but when to causing damage, she might be able to surpass him. The suppressed tail was now relaxed, and all eight tails re-emerged simultaneously. Four beams of demonic light shot into the sky and bombarded the mountain peaks, while four more beams of demonic light swept the earth, aimed at the earth veins. With a pair of sharp claws, she was going to turn Pozi Mountain upside down.

Chapter 482: A Nine-Tailed Demonic Fox? Tu Siyan?

“Demon, you’re simply courting death!”

The old beggar was enraged. His entire body ignited with spiritual power, and bursts of light emanated form his body, forming an auspicious halo behind him.

The spiritual light permeated the entire Pozi Mountain range, vaguely forming illusory mountains in the sky, which slowly pressed down. Countless previously shattered rocks from the surrounding peaks actually rose against the heavens and gathered in the illusory shadows of the falling mountains, converging towards the formation of a new mountain.

“Let’s see whether or not I can suppress you. Not only will I suppress the mountain, but I’ll also suppress you, ensuring you can’t rise again for a hundred years! You will never be able to rise again… never again…”

The old beggar rarely spoke such harsh words; his words were like thunder from the heavens, resounding with howling echoes. The flying sand and stones in the sky could not obscure the dazzling light of his spiritual energy, and the pressure brought by the mountain-suppressing technique was growing ever stronger.

Ji Yuan stood in the sky, though he was hidden behind the Great Void Jade Talisman. The old beggar’s methods astonished him.

‘This is simply… like being trapped under the Five Finger Mountain!?’

Ji Yuan had read Journey to the West countless times, and he naturally remembered the episode where the Buddha suppressed Sun Wukong.1 As a child, he had felt anger and pity for the Monkey King; now, he was more interested in the mystery of the laws and techniques hidden within the story.

Seeing the old beggar’s technique and hearing his words, accompanied by the thunderous echoes, Ji Yuan was deeply shocked. Originally, Ji Yuan’s deepest impression of this technique was from the Mountain-Suppressing Demon-Subduing Seal that the Mingwang Buddha’s incarnation had used to deal with Lu Shanjun, but compared to the Mountain-Suppressing Technique that the old beggar was using now, it was like comparing a small minnow to an enormous fish.

In the Pozi Mountain Range, the villagers who had felt the earth shaking rushed out of their homes in panic.

“Run! Run! The earth dragon is turning over!”

“Ah… Father!”

“Mother!”

“Get out of the house quickly!”

“Bring your bow, bring your bow!”

“Waaaaah… Mother, I’m scared…”

…

Besides the surrounding tremors, the mountain village was filled with panicked shouts and cries from the villagers, as adults, children, and the elderly rushed out of their homes.

The hunters also made sure to bring bows, arrows, and spears, as it was getting dark, and without these weapons they might be attacked by wild animals.

“Rumble…”

“Bang…”

Some old mud-brick houses in the village collapsed, but this was nothing compared to the situation before them. After fleeing the village, most of the villagers stared blankly into the depths of the mountains.

There, entire mountains collapsed, and even more dazzling light rose. Amidst the dust filling the sky, a colossal phantom of a mountain range appeared, and the old beggar’s booming voice truly sounded like heavenly thunder.

“You will never rise again, not even after a hundred years…”

An elderly man in the village pointed tremblingly into the distance.

“Mountain God, subdue the demon! Mountain God, subdue the demon! Quickly, bow down! Quickly, bow down!”

“I kneel to the Mountain God!”

“Yes, yes, hurry up and bow!”

Faced with an incomprehensible power, the villagers in the mountain village chose to worship it. The sight of the mountains in front of them easily led them to associate it with the work of a mountain god.

What the villagers didn’t know was that the very being that wanted to connect with the earth’s veins and become a mountain god was currently hiding underground, trembling with fear.

‘Great immortals and demons, quickly finish your battle and leave! Spare this little monster! I haven’t done anything!’

Even though the mountain spirit who wanted to become the mountain god was now terrified, it still spared some of its power to keep an eye on the small village in the Pozi Mountains. Putting aside everything else, it had to protect the villagers who kept calling for the mountain god and bowing in worship.

When the old beggar was truly enraged and attacked with all his might, even Tu Siyan, who was an eight-tailed fox demon, was unable to withstand it. The illusory mountains in the sky grew more and more conspicuous and clear.

“Ah… Immortal Master, is this really how you intend to treat me? I, Tu Siyan, am an eight-tailed spirit fox from the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern. I have never committed any heinous acts. This time, I only intervened because I happened to witness the juniors of my fox clan being slaughtered by humans. I couldn’t bear it and stepped in to help. Could this truly be considered a demon harming humanity?”

Tu Siyan knelt on one knee, her eight tails now back to normal length, tucked behind her and swaying. At the same time, a look of despair crossed on her face as she accused and questioned the heavens.

“Immortal Master! If I promise not to act rashly and harm anyone again, could you please spare me? Even if not for my sake, could you at least consider the reputation of my ancestors?”

“You wretched creature, no matter how eloquent you are, it’s useless. Even if the Nine-Tailed Fox were to intervene right in front of you, this matter cannot be left unresolved!”

If Tu Siyan had spoken these words at the beginning, the old beggar might have hesitated, but since this demon had previously tried to collapse the entire Pozi Mountain Range without any regard for the people and animals living in it, such words were useless.

Moreover, Ji Yuan was watching from the sidelines. He had been tricked by this vixen once before, and then almost made a mistake because of her. If something else went wrong now, and Ji Yuan had to clean up the mess, where would he put his face? Would he even care about his reputation?!

Ji Yuan, watching from the side, nodded repeatedly. The old beggar was indeed an old beggar master, a genuine master of the immortal path. His composure was quite impressive, ignoring his tendency to occasionally argue with Ji Yuan over some trivial matters.

Tu Siyan’s face had already developed some downy hairs, and she even sported short fox whiskers. Her demonic power was gathering all over her body, and her demonic aura was soaring into the sky. However, her energy had been locked down by the old beggar’s mountain-suppressing technique, leaving her feeling powerless.

“Old beggar, you’re ruthless!”

Tu Siyan let out a low cry, frantically chanting in her mind, “Ancestor, save me!”

One of her eight tails swayed in front of her, and a long hair, or rather a white hair, flew out from the depths of the downy fur at the end of the tail.

As soon as the hair appeared, the demonic power on Tu Siyan’s body instantly converged towards it, and almost immediately, a strong white light emanated. In the next moment, the hair swirled around and flew to her tailbone.

With a “whoosh,” her white hair transformed in the light, and a ninth tail slowly sprouted from Tu Siyan’s back.

All these changes occurred in a mere instant. Coupled with the already soaring demonic aura and the dust covering the mountain, the old beggar, who was casting his technique, did not even notice.

“Roar—“

The moment the new fox tail appeared, Tu Siyan’s roar, accompanied by an intense demonic aura, soared into the sky. The old beggar’s mountain-suppressing light fluctuated violently, and the surrounding mountain terrain, which had previously been stable, began to sway again.

“Boom…”

Tu Siyan’s entire face transformed into a fox head with a long nose, claws almost as long as her slender fingers, and her entire body was shrouded in thick white light.

“Old beggar, I’ll kill you! Rooar—”

Coupled with the demon’s intense roar, the demonic light shone brightly, even surpassing the old beggar’s spiritual light. The mountain that had initially been used for the Mountain-Suppressing Technique was also swaying. This kind of technique was similar to Ji Yuan’s Immobilization Technique. It required a higher level of cultivation than the opponent to be used effectively, otherwise it would easily become unstable. Although this Mountain-Suppressing Technique was a fully manifested technique, it was facing precisely that problem at this moment.

“A nine-tailed fox? Impossible! Even under duress, you couldn’t possibly cultivate a ninth tail at this moment. This isn’t your level of cultivation!”

“So what if it’s not? This Pozi Mountain and you, you old beggar, are not going anywhere today. I’m not made of clay!”2

Tu Siyan was forced into a very difficult situation today, and even resorted to her most desperate survival techniques. Of course, she knew that she was not a true nine-tailed fox. Even if she could defeat this unfathomable old beggar at this moment, she would never be able to kill him. But she still had to make a big show of it and at least make him look bad, or even injure him before running away!

“Prepare to die!”

With a loud roar, nine tails rose into the sky and shot out in nine directions, their length continuously extending, seemingly covering the entire land and sky of the Pozi Mountain Range.

Unfortunately, one of the tails grazed Ji Yuan’s hiding spot, knocking him out from behind the Great Void Jade Talisman with a “bang”.

Even though the situation was extremely urgent for both sides in the battle, Ji Yuan’s appearance still attracted their attention. Despite the continuous spiritual light and demonic energy, the scene instantly seemed to fall silent.

“Uh, haha… Mr. Lu, can I make my move now?”

Ji Yuan’s calm, pale eyes swept over Tu Siyan. Compared to the alluring gaze she gave when seducing others, Ji Yuan’s eyes were equally captivating. As he spoke, his right hand was already gripping the hilt of the Green Vine Sword.

“Ji Yuan…!?”

Tu Siyan’s voice was less one of astonishment and more one of screaming terror.

So that’s how it was! No matter how powerful the country of Daxiu, how could it possibly have a celestial being like the old beggar? And the other party’s claim that he just happened to be passing by was such a far-fetched lie.

Now everything made sense. It was indeed a trap, but not one set by the Heavenly Master of Daxiu. It was a trap set by Ji Yuan. She was already struggling so much against the old beggar; now, with an even more terrifying Ji Yuan, it was as if the heavens were trying to cut off the fox’s path to survival.

As soon as this feeling of despair arose, her demonic aura immediately subsided, and a slight conflict arose between her and the ninth and most crucial fox tail.

She wasn’t a true nine-tailed fox to begin with, so how could this momentary incompatibility escape Ji Yuan’s watchful eye, as he was always keeping a close eye on Tu Siyan?

‘An opportunity!’

With a thought, he drew his sword.

“Shiiing—”

As the immortal sword was drawn, the world suddenly brightened. In the dazzling sword light that illuminated the world, the sword intent and sword energy swept past Ji Yuan in an instant.

“Ah……”

Tu Siyan’s scream rang out, and in the very next moment, blood gushed out from her mouth as she collapsed to the ground. A white fox tail floated into the air.

Chapter 483: A New Mountain

With the ninth tail severed by the sword, the pressure the old beggar felt immediately decreased significantly. He felt much more relaxed than when he had been fighting against Tu Siyan with her eight tails before. This was because not only had Tu Siyan’s vital energy been damaged by this sword, but her spirit had been severed as well.

Tu Siyan collapsed to the ground, curled up and trembling. Her remaining eight tails were all tucked away beside her, and she no longer possessed the power to shake the mountains. She raised her head and looked desperately at the mountain in the sky that had begun to materialize. The old beggar’s mountain-suppressing technique was clearly uninterrupted because of Ji Yuan’s intervention.

Ji Yuan slowly sheathed the Green Vine Immortal Sword. The sharp sword intent that filled the sky dissipated as the blade was tucked away.

After sheathing the Green Vine Sword, Ji Yuan held it in his left hand and waved his right sleeve. There was neither a whooshing sound of air nor the pull of spiritual energy; it was as if Ji Yuan’s raised sleeve had merely covered a small part of the sky, and the fox tail in the sky was whisked away by Ji Yuan’s “Universe Within a Sleeve” technique.

When Ji Yuan took the fox tail out of his sleeve again, the white fox tail had already transformed into a long silver hair.

Even though it was just a single hair, Ji Yuan still felt a slight sense of heaviness when he held it in his hand.

Rumble rumble rumble…

The entire Pozi Mountain began to tremble once again, pulling Ji Yuan’s attention away from the hair in his palm. The mountain in the sky had become almost tangible, and the surrounding air felt as heavy as lead.

“No…don’t suppress me! You– you two True Immortal level immortals are ganging up on me, a weak woman. Don’t you feel ashamed?”

“Ji Yuan! Mr. Ji! We are old acquaintances. Back then on the flower boat on the river, if it weren’t for certain circumstances, we could have been intimate. Are you really so heartless?”

Tu Siyan was pale, blood trickling from the corner of her mouth. She looked up at Ji Yuan in the sky and cried with a sorrowful expression. Her beautiful and pitiful expression was truly heartbreaking.

Even though the old beggar knew it was almost certainly a lie, he still instinctively looked at Ji Yuan when he heard these words.

Even with Ji Yuan’s current composure, the old beggar’s gaze made Ji Yuan’s lips twitch. He sent a mental transmission to the old beggar.

“Absolutely not!”

“Yes, yes, the old beggar knows.”

The old beggar nodded, and with a flick of his wrist, the mountain in the sky fully materialized.

“The Mountain Descends to Suppress Demons!”

Amidst the old beggar’s booming voice like rolling thunder, a massive mountain rapidly descended from the sky.

“Whoosh—” The howling sound of the air being torn apart indicated that the mountain had now become a physical entity.

“Ugh—”

“Boom…”

Tu Siyan’s sharp scream echoed throughout the mountains and fields, causing the entire Pozi Mountain to shake violently. The people on the mountain were so frightened that many of them fell to the ground. Even the spirits deep within the mountains were so terrified that they covered their heads tightly and dared not move.

Despite the commotion, no mountain collapsed; at most, the sand and rocks in the mountains trembled.

“Crack-crack-crack-crack-crack…”

Where the mountain fell, the surrounding mountains connected with each other with a series of crackling sounds. The originally isolated mountain became one with the surrounding hillside, as if it had always been a part of the Pozi Mountain Range.

The soaring demonic aura in Pozi Mountain seemed to be suppressed beneath the mountain at this moment, disappearing in an instant without a trace. The spiritual light that filled the sky gradually dimmed, leaving only the seven-colored halo around the old beggar that had not yet faded.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar slowly descended from two different directions. During this process, the old beggar, who originally radiated a certain divinity, returned to his ragged beggar form as the halo disappeared.

The dust in the sky fell at an abnormal speed, and Pozi Mountain gradually regained its clarity. The surrounding mountains and forests were deathly quiet, making it seem like just an ordinary mountain evening.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar slowly ambled over from their respective locations like two mortals wandering in the mountains. Then they walked together to the foot of the mountain where they had landed.

At this moment, on the mountain wall one zhang away from Ji Yuan and the old beggar, there was a narrow crack about the width of a finger. With Ji Yuan and the old beggar’s eyesight and divine vision, they could see dozens of zhang deep through this crack.

Inside the dimly lit interior of the mountain, in a small space of about a few square feet, a woman lay, unconscious. It was Tu Siyan, who had been suppressed beneath the mountain. Even within this space, her entire body was still not exposed.

Apart from her neck and head, only half of Tu Siyan’s shoulders and arms, covered in tattered clothing, were exposed; the rest of her body was hidden under the rocks.

‘How ruthless…’

Ji Yuan couldn’t help but sigh in his heart, comparing it with the situation of the Monkey King in Journey to the West. When Sun Wukong was suppressed under the Five Fingers Mountain, at least half of his body was exposed outside the mountain. Indeed, truly exposed, all the way outside. Although he was inevitably exposed to the wind and sun, at least he could breathe fresh air and even talk to the cowherds who passed by.

As for Tu Siyan, her entire body had been completely suppressed under the mountain. Forget about communicating with people, even ordinary people would not know she existed. The cramped space inside the mountain was just enough to catch a breath, so it made little difference.

Thinking about it this way, the Buddha was truly compassionate…

The old beggar turned his gaze away and chuckled at Ji Yuan, who was still peering inside.

“That vixen has finally been subdued! But Mr. Ji is ruthless and merciless; that sword strike was truly swift and accurate!”

Ji Yuan sensed a hidden meaning in the old beggar’s words and couldn’t help but turn his gaze towards him.

“Mr. Lu, please stop teasing me. Tu Siyan dares to say anything. She can even claim the dead came back to life. I was investigating a strange and mysterious case in Dazhen when I happened to encounter this demon. I was caught off guard and she escaped.”

“Hmm, this old beggar was just saying things offhand, sir, there’s no need to explain it so clearly.”

Having a rare opportunity to tease Ji Yuan, the old beggar pretended to be ignorant, and even added another comment.

“It was just a coincidence this time, I understand!”

It truly was a coincidence. Ji Yuan, seeing the old beggar’s reaction, was both amused and exasperated.

Then, the two of them gradually straightened their expressions, growing more serious. Ji Yuan thought for a moment and spoke.

“Old Master Lu, do you really only intend to suppress her for a hundred years?”

The old beggar shrugged.

“Otherwise, as the saying goes, ‘even if you don’t respect the monk, you should give the Buddha some face.’1 The eight-tailed spirit fox of Jade Fox Sacred Cavern didn’t transgress in a way that was enough justification to kill her. Didn’t Mr. Ji only cut off one of her tails instead of killing her?”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“Indeed, but we should still question her once she wakes up. This demon has been running around everywhere, and we don’t know what trouble she’s been causing. Since we’ve run into her, there’s no reason not to ask her.”

Sensing Tu Siyan’s weakened state, Ji Yuan asked another question.

“Mr. Lu, when will she wake up?”

“Well, that depends on her. Although Mr. Ji’s sword strike severed a fake tail, she was indeed imbued by the aura of the Nine-Tailed Fox Spirit at that time. She was seriously injured by that sword, far more severely than by my Mountain-Suppressing Technique. That’s why I said, sir, you were quite ruthless in destroying her.”

The old beggar stopped teasing Ji Yuan, thought it over carefully, and then calculated some divinations with his fingers.

“It might take her a year or two to wake up.”

…

Outside Pozi Mountain, the soldiers who had previously fled on foot only paused briefly when the earth shook. Then, urged by the military officer, they quickened their pace and fled. Even a fool would know that it must have been caused by the immortal master fighting with the demon.

Now that things had quieted down, the soldiers who had already fled several miles away subconsciously slowed their pace, partly out of curiosity and partly because they were utterly exhausted.

Wearing full armor and weapons, carrying some dead foxes, and hauling essential supplies such as water flasks and dry rations from the saddles of their dead horses, they had been running with frenzied energy for quite some time in their escape. Even though they were skilled in battlefield martial arts, they had been injured by Tu Siyan’s earlier emotional manipulation, and were already on the verge of collapse.

“Haah…haah…haah…”

The military officer used his spear to prop himself up on the ground, panting heavily. The weather had been rather muggy these past few days, and the shock and strenuous exercise had left his face drenched in sweat like rain, and his clothes were already soaked through. As soon as he stopped, fatigue and discomfort overwhelmed him. Meanwhile, his comrades around him collapsed and gasped for breath.

The military officer, leaning on his spear, turned to look at Pozi Mountain, where the terrifying commotion had subsided.

“General, haah…haah…General, it should be…alright now… right?”

“No, I don’t know. Just now, that white light and the sound of the sword… it must have been the swordsmanship of an immortal… But… I hope the immortal has won… Haah…”

Unlike the soldiers who were filled with fear and exhaustion, many inhabitants of the mountain village in Pozi Mountain began to feel slightly excited.

From the villagers’ perspective, they could see the silhouette of the mountain had appeared in the sky before the old beggar. When that giant mountain fell from the sky, the earth did indeed shake, but afterwards everything calmed down.

The unsettling feeling vanished at that moment without a trace, and after the mountain fell, the auspicious light in the sky lasted for quite a while before gradually fading away. In the villagers’ simple understanding, it must have been that the “mountain god” had successfully suppressed the evil spirits.

Some of the bolder villagers had already planned to hurry up and head deeper into the Pozi Mountain Range before nightfall, even if it was just to catch a glimpse from afar.

Even with the earnest advice of the elder generation of villagers, they still couldn’t dissuade some of the young hunters in the village.

Seven young and strong hunters, carrying spears, bows and arrows, ropes, and even torches and tinder, set off in the direction where the mountain had fallen earlier.

About half an hour later, the hunters arrived at a hilltop deep within the mountains with practiced ease. Each hunter stood there, frozen, as soon as they arrived.

The familiar mountain path in the distance was gone, and so was that wide mountain valley…

“Am I seeing things?”

“Quick, pinch me!”

“Then… there’s a new mountain!”

The hunters couldn’t help but think of the mountain silhouette they had seen in the sky earlier. It turned out that there really was a mountain that had fallen. The peak of this mountain was connected to the surrounding mountains and was far taller than the original peaks of the Pozi Mountain Range, making it the most magnificent peak of the Pozi Mountains…

T/n: In case you’re wondering (slight spoiler), yes, Tu Siyan comes back to cause trouble later. I definitely disagree with JY’s decision not to just kill her here, it’s really frustrating, but honestly that’s just the kind of protagonist JY is. He doesn’t make the perfect decisions every single time, and at the very least I can respect that he sticks by his own morals and opinions. As readers, we have a kind of god’s-eye-view of the whole situation and know that Tu Siyan is Bad News, but JY is a good bean and prefers not to kill. In fact, I can only think of very few instances were he struck a mortal blow, and in every single one of them it was in a situation where more people would have died had he not acted (see the snake demon all the way at the beginning of the novel that was actively drowning people, the zombie creatures, the evil dragons that were going against Ying Hong’s wife…). He had never actually struck any other killing blows. So honestly, this restraint is very much in character for him… even if it’s frustrating as heck for me.

In short, even if you don’t respect the person, give some respect to the forces backing them

Chapter 484: The Immortal Commands: Guard This Mountain

While several hunters marveled on the distant mountain ridge, Ji Yuan and the old beggar were still standing at the foot of the mountain where Tu Siyan was being suppressed. The old beggar tried to wake Tu Siyan up using a method to clear her mind, but perhaps due to her self-protective instinct caused by her severe injuries, her consciousness was deeply buried and she did not awaken.

Given that Tu Siyan’s vital energy was currently severely damaged, it was not advisable to use overly drastic methods, otherwise it could easily lead to serious damage to her soul, or even cause her soul to directly leave her body. Therefore, they could only leave her here for the time being and wait for her to wake up on her own.

Although this mountain-suppressing technique had suppressed Tu Siyan, it would not cause her any other damage. A trace of spiritual energy would also gather in the mountain, giving her a chance to recover her vitality. Although this would take a very long time, she wouldn’t recover too slowly.

The hunters in the mountains ultimately dared not approach the newly appeared mountain. After looking at it, seeing that it was really getting dark, they quickly left with excitement, rushing to tell the villagers what they had seen.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar each stopped their techniques. The latter had mainly been using incantations, while the former flicked a drop of ambergris into the incense, but Tu Siyan didn’t react at all.

“Mr. Ji, I think we should just give up and wait for this demon to wake up on her own.”

Ji Yuan could only sigh.

“Very well, that’s all we can do. But Tu Siyan is an eight-tailed fox demon, and she even briefly reached the level of a nine-tailed fox just now. I wonder if she’ll cause any trouble?”1

The old beggar smiled.

“Mr. Ji, please rest assured. My mountain-suppressing technique is still quite effective. Few in the immortal path can compare to it. Under my mountain-suppressing technique, this demon cannot control her demonic power, and can only meet the minimum requirements for survival and cultivation. Unless an external force breaks this mountain, she will have no chance of escaping.”2

Seemingly anticipating that Ji Yuan would ask another question, the old beggar added.

“If external forces were to break this mountain, their skill in cultivation would have to be exceptional, and I would also immediately be alerted!”

If the old beggar was mentioning “exceptional skill” to Ji Yuan, then indeed this would truly require a high level of expertise. He still did not know Ji Yuan’s skill level, but even though destruction was always easier than construction,3 it was likely that Ji Yuan was superior to the old beggar himself.

The old beggar spoke confidently in his first few sentences, but after thinking about it, he then added.

“If we’re really talking about taking shortcuts, there are ways. If there’s a mountain god here who controls the earth’s veins, and that mountain god were to work in concert with this demon, it might be possible to shake the mountain’s force…”

As he spoke, the old beggar frowned, looked around at the mountains, and muttered to himself.

“I wonder if this mountain has a mountain god?”

Mountain gods, land gods, river gods, and water gods—these earthly deities shared a common characteristic: they could hide. Unless they were determined to destroy the entire water and earth veins, it would be very difficult to find a god who was determined to hide.

Previously, the old beggar’s spiritual duel with Tu Siyan was so intense that it almost caused the entire Pozi Mountain Range to collapse. Even if there were gods in these mountains, they would have hidden themselves as deeply as possible long ago. They would never reveal themselves on their own.

This might have been the reason for the existence of the Spirit-Binding Art. However, due to the special nature of such divinities, the Spirit-Binding Art was extremely difficult to learn, let alone master.

Moreover, the Spirit Binding Art was considered one of the most top-tier supernatural secret techniques. Very few cultivators or cultivation forces possessed the complete method, and they would not easily pass it on. Even true disciples would not be taught if their cultivation level was insufficient. Instead, various fragmented pieces would occasionally circulate. The different Spirit-Binding Techniques varied greatly, but they were all still considered precious.

The old beggar had actually contemplated the Spirit-Binding Art, and even practiced a relatively complete method. Unfortunately, the method was only relatively complete, not truly complete. A slight difference could lead to a huge error. To this day, the old beggar still could not fully master the Spirit-Binding Art.

It went without saying that it was difficult to capture stronger deities, but even weaker land spirits were not easy to capture. It wasn’t entirely due to luck, but rather that weaker land and water spirits might have shallower connections to the earth’s veins, making it difficult to summon and bind them with the terrain or water features.

However, considering the current situation, the old beggar thought he should give it a try, so he spoke calmly after finishing his previous words.

“Mr. Ji, have you ever heard of a method that can summon spirits to your presence? Although it might offend some people, it’s quite suitable for the current situation.”

Upon hearing the old beggar’s words, Ji Yuan’s interest was immediately piqued. This old beggar actually knew how to summon spirits?

“Mr. Lu, are you referring to the Spirit-Binding Art?”

In all these years, Ji Yuan had never encountered anyone anyone else besides himself who could summon spirits. Of course, Ji Yuan’s Spirit-Binding Technique was unique, and had evolved through his own unique Edicts.

So when he heard the old beggar clearly preparing to use a Spirit Binding Technique, he was very interested in watching and seeing where the differences lay.

Seeing Ji Yuan’s initial surprise, followed by a look of great interest, the old beggar felt a surge of satisfaction and nodded with a smile in response.

“Mr. Ji is right, this is indeed the Spirit-Binding Technique! This method is widely known, and this old beggar has also done some research on it by chance. I can only say that I have achieved some minor success, not complete mastery, but it is worth a try.”

Ji Yuan had been waiting for him to say that, so he immediately stepped to the side, deliberately making room for him, and waved his hand toward the ground.

“Please, Mr. Lu!”

The old beggar didn’t stand on ceremony. He gently swung his right arm, rotating his index finger upwards, while holding his left palm flat in front of him. He murmured softly, and soon a faint crimson light emanated from his fingertips.

At this moment, the old beggar’s expression turned solemn, and he touched his left palm with his fingers, gently sliding them to form a special talisman.

Throughout the entire process, Ji Yuan on the side was completely focused. Clearly, the old beggar’s Spirit-Binding Technique was almost entirely different from Ji Yuan’s.

The casting process was short, taking only three or four breaths from the initial hand gesture to the final palm swipe. Then, the old beggar’s eyes narrowed, and he swept his right hand across his left palm. The next moment, his left palm gently struck the ground.

“Bang…”

The ground beneath their feet trembled slightly, and a ripple, like a drop hitting the water, slowly spread outwards, a trait almost identical to Ji Yuan’s Spirit-Binding Technique.

“I invite the Mountain God to appear.”

The faint rumbling sound emanating from the old beggar’s mouth also spread as he unleashed his spiritual power.

One breath, two breaths, three breaths… Time slowly passed, but no mountain god appeared before Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

The old beggar waited for a while, without feeling embarrassed. He turned around and looked thoughtfully at the mountain he had created with his previous mountain-suppressing technique.

“It seems that even if this mountain has a mountain god, the connection between the mountain and the earth’s veins is very shallow. In addition, my mountain-suppressing technique has changed the mountain range’s terrain, so the Spirit-Binding Technique was ineffective. Of course, it’s more likely that this mountain does not have a mountain god. After all, the country of Daxiu knows about the divine path, and has extremely strict control over illicit shrines in the countryside. Without the help of faith, ordinary spirits and monsters can hardly become mountain gods.”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“That is indeed possible. In that case, let us leave first. I will leave some tricks here.”

As he spoke, a yellow talisman shaped like a human flew out of Ji Yuan’s sleeve, attracting the attention of the old beggar, who peered over with great curiosity. This talisman had been specifically cut into the shape of a small person, and there were no talismanic symbols or text on it, which was quite peculiar.

Ji Yuan gently tossed the yellow paper talisman forward. As it slowly drifted down in the air, a powdery golden glow appeared. Ji Yuan uttered a soft sentence.

“I summon my Warrior.”

The next moment, the yellow paper talisman transformed into a stream of golden powder, swirling and changing before Ji Yuan and the old beggar’s eyes, before finally solidifying into a burly, golden-armored general.

As soon as the Golden-Armored Warrior appeared, he performed his signature gesture, slowly bowing and meticulously clasping his hands in a salute to Ji Yuan. His voice boomed like bells.

“Your Excellency.”

“Ah, the fox demon Tu Siyan is suppressed beneath this mountain. You must stay here and guard this mountain, protect its terrain, and prevent this demon from escaping.”

The Golden-Armored Warrior maintained his bowing posture and answered in a loud and deep voice.

“I accept the command.”

The old beggar’s eyes lit up, his expression full of surprise. He stared at the Golden-Armored Warrior from head to toe. He had never seen or even heard of such a wondrous technique before. Although there were puppets, clones, and incarnations in the immortal path, and talisman techniques were incredibly diverse, none of them were the same as Ji Yuan’s.

The Golden-Armored Warrior received the command, then straightened his body with his arms hanging down, slowly took two steps back, and his figure gradually faded, appearing and disappearing along the mountainside.

This elusive figure was only visible to Ji Yuan and the old beggar; an ordinary person would certainly not have been able to see it.

“Alright, Mr. Lu, let’s leave now. I have some matters to discuss with the Imperial Preceptor of Daxiu, so I won’t stay in these mountains any longer.”

“Haha, let’s go then!”

Clouds rose from beneath their feet, slowly lifting Ji Yuan and the old beggar into the air. However, after rising several dozen feet, Ji Yuan suddenly looked in a certain direction in the mountains.

There was a faint aura of vitality rising from the mountain, which was most likely the location of the mountain village. However, he also saw a faint aura of divine will rising from the same area. Moreover, this divine will was absorbed into the surrounding mountains after it rose, rather than dissipating directly. Because Ji Yuan had studied the relationship between the Edict technique and the mysterious yellow Qi very deeply, he could definitely not be mistaken.

At that moment, the hunters returned to the mountain village and began to spread the word about what they had seen, embellishing the story of how powerful the mountain god was and how amazing the mountain was. As a result, the villagers worshipped the mountain god even more frequently.

‘The aura of divine faith hasn’t dissipated?’

Ji Yuan frowned and subconsciously glanced at the old beggar. Could there be a mountain god in this mountain?

“What’s wrong, Mr. Ji? If you’re tired, this old beggar can control the clouds too.”

“No, Mr. Lu, please wait a moment. We’ll go down again, and this time let me try.”

As he spoke, the clouds under Ji Yuan’s control flew back.

“Try it? Try what?”

The old beggar was stunned for a moment, then suddenly realized something. As he looked at Ji Yuan in surprise, Ji Yuan, having landed, had already activated his technique.

“I summon the Mountain God.”

As the edict was issued, Ji Yuan lightly stomped his right foot on the ground.

A ripple spread out like water, and the next moment, a wisp of smoke rose from the ground, from which emerged a yellowish-brown monster dressed in cloth with a terrified expression.

As soon as it appeared, the spirit immediately bowed and kowtowed to Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

“The Mountain God of Pozi Mountain greets the two immortal masters!”

Just as expected!

“Mountain God, there’s no need for such formalities. It’s just that you have no temple in these mountains, so I had no choice but to summon you using the Spirit-Binding Art.”

Ji Yuan responded briefly, then addressed the old beggar.

“This mountain seems to have a mountain god, but it is not deeply connected to the earth’s veins.”

The old beggar: “…”

T/n: JY, you are in a xianxia novel with a shameless evil fox demon, of course she’s going to come back and cause trouble
