Chapter 485: Guarding This Mountain Together

The old beggar’s facial muscles twitched noticeably as he looked at the monster that appeared before him. For a moment, he had the impression that what had been summoned before him by Ji Yuan’s Spirit-Binding Technique was not the mountain god, but his own pride.

Ji Yuan couldn’t help but smile at the old beggar’s expression,1 but the latter’s expression was only subtle for a moment before he immediately returned to normal. His thick skin was comparable to the mountain that had been formed by his previous mountain-suppressing technique.

After a quick glance, Ji Yuan turned his attention to the Mountain God, who stood there nervously, his eyes darting between Ji Yuan and the old beggar. However, his main focus was on the mountain before him, which was impossible to ignore.

As someone who aspired to become the true god of Pozi Mountain, the first step was to understand the mountain’s terrain and the earth’s veins. He was very familiar with every blade of grass and every tree on Pozi Mountain, let alone the hills and peaks.

The sudden appearance of this magnificent peak was undoubtedly a result of the previous battle.

‘Oh my god… oh heavens…’

The mountain god originally wanted to imitate the mountain people’s lament, “Oh my god!” but suddenly realized that he was a god,2 so he could only immediately change his thoughts and ask the heavens for help.

Upon seeing Ji Yuan and the old beggar turn their gazes towards him, the Mountain God quickly averted his own.

“My name is Shi Youdao. May I ask why you two immortals have summoned me here?”

Ji Yuan pointed to the side.

“This mountain was created by Master Lu using his mountain-suppressing technique. A female demon is imprisoned beneath it, unable to escape for a hundred years.”

The Mountain God’s mouth dropped slightly open. He looked at the mountain and nodded.

“You are the Mountain God of this mountain range. I hope you can take good care of it and prevent the suppression seal from being damaged due to the destruction of the mountain.”

With such a huge mountain pressing down on it, the Mountain God could hardly imagine what kind of monster was underneath. Ordinary demons would probably be crushed into mincemeat by now, let alone be suppressed for a hundred years. Thinking about this, the Mountain God felt a chill in his heart. What if this demon really escaped? Wouldn’t it come after him, its “jailer”?

But the monster hadn’t yet escaped, and the two immortals who had created a mountain to suppress it were truly standing before him. How could the Mountain God dare to refuse?

“This humble servant obeys, this humble servant understands!”

The Mountain God bowed again, not daring to show the slightest disrespect in his words.

“Ahem!”

The old beggar coughed and spoke up.

“This mountain that suppresses the female demon will also be of great benefit to you, as a Mountain God who has not yet become a true deity. This is not an illusion or a trick, but a real mountain. Moreover, it will combine with the surrounding peaks to form a powerful mountain range. It may not be obvious now, but in five, ten, or even one hundred years, the territory covered by the mountain range of Pozi Mountain will greatly increase because of this mountain. If you, the Mountain God, can obtain the position of a true deity, you should understand what this means.”

This was a real benefit. The mountain god’s eyes lit up. He bowed deeply to the old beggar.

“Thank you for your guidance, Immortal!”

After hesitating for a moment, the Mountain God finally voiced his concerns.

“However, although I am extremely willing to share the burdens of the two immortals, my cultivation is shallow and my spiritual power is extremely limited. I can only do my best and dare not claim that everything will be foolproof… Furthermore, although I call myself a Mountain God, it is merely self-aggrandizement. The country of Daxiu does not recognize my divine status and I cannot openly and freely display my powers in the mountains. Even if I wanted to build a temple, it would be immediately designated as an illicit shrine and destroyed…”

Upon hearing this, Ji Yuan and the old beggar couldn’t help but give the Mountain God a serious look. They were both intelligent people and naturally understood the implied meaning in the Mountain God’s words.

Interesting. They didn’t expect this mountain god to be so clever, knowing how to seize this opportunity, and also to have enough courage to try and take it.

“Mountain God, please rest assured.”

As Ji Yuan spoke, he gestured with his hand, and a golden-armored warrior stepped forward from the side of the mountain behind him. His figure revealed itself only after a few steps, and his muscular and powerful physique startled the Mountain God.

“Your Excellency.”

The Golden-Armored Warrior bowed slightly, then straightened. Although he himself showed no particular emotions, he was not unresponsive to the outside world. So, after his gaze swept over the old beggar, he naturally looked at the Mountain God.

However, the Golden-Armored Warrior usually stood tall and proud, while the Mountain God is rather short. As a result, the warrior stood next to Ji Yuan, looking straight and glancing sideways at the Mountain God. Not to mention the Mountain God, even Ji Yuan and the old beggar could feel a sense of contempt and disdain coming from the warrior.

“Hehe, this is the Golden-Armored Warrior, also known as the Yellow-Robed Warrior. He is a kind of guardian spirit, and will be here to help guard this place and lend a helping hand to the Mountain God.”

The mountain god nodded in realization, quickly stepped forward, and bowed deeply to the Golden-Armored Warrior.

“I am Shi Youdao, the mountain god of Pozi Mountain. I will be working with the divine general for some time!”

The Golden-Armored Warrior stood motionless, without bowing in return. He maintained his stance, his head tilted to the side. When the Mountain God finished bowing and looked up, the Golden-Armored Warrior withdrew his gaze, looking straight ahead and completely ignoring him.

“Uh, haha, the Golden-Armored Warrior didn’t mean it that way. Let’s talk about something else. You said that the country of Daxiu doesn’t recognize your status, and you might not even dare to let the government of Daxiu know that you want to become a god, let alone establish a shrine.”

Ji Yuan thought for a moment and continued.

“This matter is quite simple. Mr. Lu and I will go and talk to the Imperial Preceptor of the country of Daxiu. I think the royal family of Daxiu will give us this much face.”

The Mountain God was overjoyed, and couldn’t suppress the smile on his face. If he could gain the official approval of the current dynasty of Daxiu and build a Mountain God Temple in the mountains or along the roads by the mountain pass, he would gain immense benefits.

‘If I could also obtain an imperial edict from the Emperor of Daxiu, ordering the government to lead the people in offering sacrifices, then… well, never mind…’

At this thought, the Mountain God couldn’t stop smiling. The words of such an immortal were a promise, much more reliable than a mortal’s vow.

Despite his joy, the Mountain God remained composed and quickly bowed again to express his gratitude. This matter concerned his future cultivation and he dared not be careless in the slightest.

“Thank you both for your kindness, sirs. With that, I can rest assured!”

Ji Yuan and the old beggar exchanged a glance and nodded slightly to each other. With the Mountain God present, the protection they received had increased by more than one level.

“Alright, thank you for your trouble, Mountain God. We take our leave now. The female demon will come to you when she awakens. Perhaps you won’t need to guard her for a hundred years.”

Ji Yuan didn’t finish speaking, and the Mountain God didn’t understand, but the old beggar understood his implication: Tu Siyan, this eight-tailed fox demon, was not easy to kill, and even harder to subdue.

Seeing that Ji Yuan and the old beggar were about to leave, the Mountain God hurriedly asked one more question.

“May I ask you two immortals what kind of monster the female demon being suppressed at the foot of the mountain is? I would like to be prepared.”

Clouds and mist rose beneath Ji Yuan and the old beggar’s feet, lifting them into the air. At the same time, Ji Yuan’s words reached the ears of the Mountain God.

“Tu Siyan is an eight-tailed fox demon. She is eloquent and adept at transformation. No matter what she says, do not believe her.”

After leaving these words, Ji Yuan and the old beggar bowed slightly to the Mountain God and ascended into the clouds. The Mountain God, of course, did not dare to be negligent and gave them a grand send-off.

“Phew… Having such powerful figures in front of me is quite stressful, but it’s all good in the end, haha…”

Once the white clouds had dispersed, the mountain god relaxed and laughed more freely. Although the eight-tailed fox demon was indeed frightening, the two immortals who could subdue it were even more remarkable. With such powerful figures protecting him, his future was bright.

The Mountain God turned to face the mountain with a smug smile, only to be startled by the Golden-Armored Warrior.

“Oh!”

The Mountain God shuddered and shrank back, almost forgetting that the other divine general was guarding the mountain. Had everyone seen how he had just acted? He wondered if this divine general would report him…

“Uh, may I ask your esteemed name, General? We may be working together for quite some time now, haha, this humble god greets you!”

Mountain God Shi Youdao bowed to the Golden-Armored Warrior again. The latter glanced over, eyes narrowing as he looked down, making Mountain God uneasy. He assumed that the other party must have seen his slightly arrogant demeanor earlier.

“Divine General, is there anything you desire? This humble god would be honored to extend my hospitality as your host…”

The Golden-Armored Warrior finally reacted to these words. He turned his head slightly to look at the mountain before turning his gaze back to the Mountain God.

“By order of His Excellency, I am to guard this mountain and protect against evil spirits.”

After speaking, the Golden-Armored Warrior slowly retreated towards the mountainside, his figure disappearing from the Mountain God’s sight until he was imperceptible.

The Mountain God stood there stunned for a while, realizing that although the other party looked down on him as a worthless little monster, he was meticulous. He bowed slightly towards the mountain in front of him again, and then turned into a wisp of green smoke and disappeared into the ground.

…

Above the white clouds, Ji Yuan and the old beggar stood silently. After a long while, the old beggar finally couldn’t help but speak.

“Mr. Ji, you also know the Spirit-Binding Art?”

“Yes.”

“Why didn’t you say so earlier?”

Ji Yuan grinned.

“Why didn’t Mr. Lu ask earlier?”

“Sigh…”

The old beggar sighed before speaking to Ji Yuan again.

“Mr. Ji’s Spirit-Binding Technique, using Edicts to transform words into Law, is truly wondrous. It is far superior to this old beggar’s technique.”

“You flatter me. This is the first time I’ve ever seen Mr. Lu’s Spirit-Binding Technique. It is equally wondrous.”

The old beggar smiled.

“The original method was slightly flawed, and this old beggar improved and practiced it. It’s nothing special.”

Ji Yuan opened his mouth, but ultimately remained silent.

After a moment of silence, the old beggar suddenly spoke again.

“Mr. Ji, what other mysterious supernatural abilities do you possess? Could you tell this old beggar about them? Like your ‘Universe Within a Sleeve’ technique?”

This was something he couldn’t actually demonstrate, as it was just for show. As for the other supernatural abilities, they didn’t seem suitable for casual display. Just talking about them would seem like bragging, so Ji Yuan could only shake his head.

“We cultivators naturally have some hidden skills, but I am merely a hermit from the mountains. I cannot compare to your profound knowledge and foundation, Elder Lu, so I will not mention them.”

The old beggar looked incredulous, but didn’t press the matter. He had only mentioned it casually, and he was genuinely afraid that Ji Yuan might reveal some earth-shattering techniques.

Chapter 486: The Cunning Fox Girl

The old beggar thought that Tu Siyan wouldn’t wake up for at least a year or two, while Ji Yuan thought that the fox demon Tu Siyan couldn’t be judged by common sense. However, given her injuries and the fact that she was being suppressed under the mountain, she would definitely be fine in the short term.

However, on the afternoon of the third day after the two left, some commotion had already begun at the foot of the Fox-Suppressing Mountain, deep in the Pozi Mountain Range.

“Drip… drip… drip…”

In the dark little space inside the mountain, a drop of water would fall from the ceiling every so often. Although only a little over two days had passed, the mountain had been filled with clouds and mist for several days, and dew had begun to seep into the space inside the mountain where the fox demon Tu Siyan was located.

The dew continued to drip, but no matter how heavy the rain was outside, the flow of water remained constant.

A mountain spring formed from drops of dew dripped in front of Tu Siyan, splashing water onto her forehead. At that moment, Tu Siyan’s eyelashes trembled. Her consciousness returned.

Tu Siyan felt her eyelids were incredibly heavy. She struggled to get up, but felt the force of a mountain pressing down on her body, making it difficult to even move.

Although one shoulder and one of her hands were exposed within the small space inside the mountain, they were actually also under the same pressure from the mountain’s sealing force, making it very difficult to even lift the hand.

After struggling for a long time, Tu Siyan finally opened her eyes. All she could see was darkness, with only a sliver of light in the distance.

“Where am I? Where am I? I… hiss…”

A sharp pain shot through Tu Siyan’s head, and the scenes from a few days ago flooded her mind. She recalled the battle with the old beggar and Ji Yuan.

“I’m…I’m being suppressed under this mountain? What mountain is this?”

Trying to look up only intensified the pressure. The oppressive atmosphere of the surrounding environment had increased exponentially in the short time since she awoke, sending chills down the spine of this fox demon who had very rarely ever known fear.

‘One hundred years…one hundred years! No, no, I can’t, I don’t want to!’

“I don’t want to stay here for a hundred years!”

Tu Siyan tried everything to escape, but her demonic power was slow to react, the surrounding spiritual energy was sparse, and there was no sunlight or moonlight entering the space. It would have been better had she not used her spiritual energy at all, as the instant she did, the Mountain-Suppressing Technique immediately activated, and a huge pressure squeezed down from all directions.

“Ugh…”

Tu Siyan cried out in pain and dared not recklessly use her demonic power anymore. However, the pain in her body grew stronger and stronger. The Mountain-Suppressing Spell did not stop, but instead seemed to be punishing her by constantly intensifying.

“Crackle…crackle…”

Rocks made popping sounds as they pressed against the creaking bones of her body.

“Ah…stop, stop…I won’t do it again, I won’t do it again, ah…”

About half an hour later, the excruciating pressure gradually subsided. Tu Siyan was soaked in sweat and her body trembled uncontrollably.

“Ha… old, old beggar… you’re so cruel…”

In this painful and desperate situation, Tu Siyan suddenly felt a slight sweetness in her throat, followed by a slightly spicy yet mellow aroma that wafted out, sliding down her throat into her abdomen, and waves of heat spreading into her limbs and bones.

The intense pain from before made the comfort brought by this heat even more pronounced. It even alleviated the injuries and helped restore her vitality.

This was the ambergris that Ji Yuan had previously spiked into the mountainside, which was only now taking effect.

“Huff…huff…huff…This is…wine? Ji Yuan’s?”

Tu Siyan wasn’t quite sure who the old beggar was, but she was very wary of Ji Yuan, so she had tried to learn more about him. She knew that Ji Yuan loved wine, and appreciated it regardless of its provenance, whether it was from a mortal or a spiritual background. He loved any wine that had a unique flavor, making him a true connoisseur, though not a drunkard.

Such a person who loves wine, though he wouldn’t be picky when drinking, he must have some extraordinary wines on hand. This must have belonged to Ji Yuan.

Although she hated it, Tu Siyan lacked the courage to spit out the wine at this moment. Instead, she needed to guide the wine’s effects to all parts of her body to relieve the pain and replenish her energy.

After a long while, Tu Siyan finally let out a long sigh of relief.

‘I never imagined this wine was so miraculous; I definitely wouldn’t share it with outsiders…’

While physical comfort may have improved somewhat, it didn’t alleviate her fear of this confined environment. Although some people spent decades in seclusion for spiritual practice, the psychological experience was different.

In just a short while, Tu Siyan had calmed down. Sweat dripped from her face, and her expression was thoughtful, yet somewhat cold and aloof.

She was unwilling and unable to be suppressed at the foot of this mountain for a hundred years. She had to find a way out. Perhaps her ancestor already knew of her state, or perhaps not.

If the ancestor didn’t know, Tu Siyan would have to find a way to escape her predicament by herself.

Moreover, even if her ancestor came, the old beggar might be no threat, but he might not fare well against Ji Yuan.

After a period of silence, Tu Siyan shouted loudly again.

“Old beggar, old beggar, let me out! Ji Yuan! Ji Yuan… come out quickly…!”

“Old beggar! Old beggar! Ji Yuan!”

The fox demon’s screams continued to echo in the confined space, with only a faint sound reaching out. However, she knew that if Ji Yuan and the old beggar were nearby, they would definitely hear it. So, after confirming that some sound could indeed be heard, she gathered some of her demonic power, focused her vocal cords, and continued to shout in the direction in which the sound would travel.

The woman’s shrill screams carried faintly through the air. They might have been inaudible to ordinary people, but some animals with sensitive hearing could hear them. Bats and other creatures in some of the mountain caves scurried about restlessly, some even flying out of their caves while the sun was still high in the sky.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar were already far away from this place, so of course they wouldn’t hear it, but it did attract the Mountain God of the region.

A wisp of smoke rose from the mountainside in front of the place where Tu Siyan was suppressed, and transformed into a spirit dressed in a cloth robe.

Shi Youdao listened intently and confirmed that a woman’s voice was coming from the mountain. Who else could be under this mountain but the suppressed eight-tailed fox demon? So the mountain god was on high alert. He looked around, hoping to spot the Golden-Armored Warrior who didn’t even bother to tell him his name, but the other party did not appear.

After much hesitation, the Mountain God decided it was best to ignore the demon, and was about to disappear into the mountain when Tu Siyan, inside the mountain, somehow seemed to know that someone was outside. She cried out in a joyful voice.

“Who’s outside? Old beggar? Ji Yuan? Is it you? Are you outside? Answer me! Answer me! I’m going crazy, answer me!”

Even though she had calmed down, Tu Siyan deliberately shouted in a voice filled with pain, madness, and despair, almost like a plea.

The Mountain God stopped in his tracks again and looked back at the mountainside. He had never actually seen what the female demon looked like, or just how exaggerated her eight tails were.

As the Mountain God, he touched the mountain wall and carefully felt around it. He quickly found the crevice, peered inside, and couldn’t see anything at all, only pitch blackness.

“Who are you? You are neither Ji Yuan nor the old beggar! May I ask which esteemed being is outside?”

Tu Siyan’s voice, filled with anticipation and trepidation, carried a tone that conveyed a deep sense of unease to anyone who heard it.

Since the other party was so afraid, Shi Youdao’s courage grew a bit.

“Please, speak to me! Just talk to me, I’m going crazy…”

The demon’s pleading voice rang out, her melodious voice trembling with the hint of a sob.

Shi Youdao originally didn’t want to respond, but at this moment, he was imagining the future expansion of the Pozi Mountain Range. Someday, it would transform from a small mountain range to a massive one. Since he was to be a Mountain God, perhaps he could experience the majesty the position a little earlier. In addition, he figured he was smart enough that just talking to her would not let this demon escape. Otherwise, that would make this immortal’s seal seem too frivolous.

“Ahem! I am the true god of this mountain, hereby ordered by the immortal to guard you, you fox demon. Do not play any tricks, or I will report to the immortal and you will suffer the consequences!”

The spirit Shi Youdao tried to make his voice sound as majestic as possible, and then let it travel through the cracks in the mountain to the interior.

Hearing this, Tu Siyan immediately smiled.

Play petty tricks and get reported to the immortal? So all you’re good at is tattling…

‘No! Ji Yuan would never leave such a minor character to guard the place!’

“So it’s the Esteemed Mountain God! This humble servant greets you, but I am trapped under the mountain and cannot give you my respects in person. Please, Mountain God, don’t blame me, please don’t punish me…”

Shi Youdao smiled, thinking to himself, ‘With such a huge mountain pressing down on you, it’s no wonder you can’t perform the proper etiquette!’ At the same time, he was very satisfied with Tu Siyan’s attitude. The phrase “Esteemed Mountain God” was exactly what he wanted to hear, and besides, it was an eight-tailed fox demon who had uttered it!

“Well, this Mountain God knows that you cannot perform proper etiquette. Just stay put and the mountain spring water will continue to flow, ensuring the smooth flow of spiritual energy.”

No sooner had the words left his mouth than a surprised and grateful voice came from inside.

“Thank you, Mountain God! Thank you, Mountain God. I kowtow to you… thud… thud…”

The sound of someone kowtowing actually came from inside the crevice.

“However, can the Mountain God alone decide on the matter of guarding me? I don’t ask for anything, I only ask that the spring water not be cut off, so that I can drink and wash from time to time.”

The woman’s hesitant and apprehensive voice rang out, and the mountain god was immediately speechless. He subconsciously looked around, but still did not see the Golden-Armored Warrior appear.

He figured that, since there was a mountain spring inside the space, it must have been a spring originating from the mountain itself. Thus, it was a method left behind by those immortals, so the divine general would not speak against it.

“Ahem, you don’t need to worry. This is a trivial matter, and the Divine General will not have any objections.”

Deep inside the mountain, Tu Siyan’s eyes flashed. Sure enough!

After thinking for a moment, she spoke in a mournful voice.

“Hmm…that’s good, that’s good enough…Although I don’t think I was wrong, it’s too late now, I can only silently bear it. Maybe I should have listened to Ji Yuan in the first place…”

After saying this ambiguous sentence, Tu Siyan fell silent. She knew that she couldn’t say everything at once. Anyway, she had already gotten a preliminary feel for the Mountain God’s abilities, it seemed he wouldn’t be too difficult to deal with.

The Mountain God outside frowned, a sense of curiosity rising in his heart, but he restrained himself from asking. After waiting for a long time without hearing any more sounds, he lingered for a while before disappearing into the mountains.

T/n: Tu Siyan lives up to her mythological fox nature by being crafty as heck. Meanwhile, I think we can all say we saw this idiocy coming from a mile away with the ambitious, overeager, coward of a Mountain God

Chapter 487: Do Not Approach

In Bianrong Prefecture of the country of Daxiu, the strange changes at Pozi Mountain within the prefecture’s borders had caused a great uproar.

Not only did the two hundred or so elite soldiers who had escaped death return to the prefectural capital to report the situation, but many ordinary people within the prefecture also learned of these happenings.

In fact, Pozi Mountain Range was not just home to one small mountain village; many people lived near the mountains. Rumors of the changes that had occurred in the mountain spread when the villagers went out to buy goods, and then the news rippled out to the surrounding areas.

Such bizarre and fantastical stories spread very easily, especially since they were substantiated. It could be said that the news travelled as fast as the horse-drawn carriages and oxcarts.

By the fourth day, people in the capital of Bianrong Prefecture were already talking about Pozi Mountain.

A Taoist priest, dressed in plain clothes but wearing a gold-threaded hairpin crown, was returning from the market when he passed a pheasant stall and overheard the vendor talking to an old man selling vegetables next door. The topic of their conversation was Pozi Mountain.

“Hey, have you heard about what happened at Pozi Mountain?”

“What happened?”

Upon hearing that the old man next door didn’t know, the pheasant vendor immediately perked up.

“Tsk tsk tsk, you don’t know? Something big has happened at Pozi Mountain!”

“What big event has occurred? Has a giant, man-eating tiger appeared?”

The vegetable vendor shook his head and clicked his tongue.

“That’s nothing. The situation at Pozi Mountain is much more serious. You know I buy wild game from the mountain villagers there. Look at these pheasants, I just bought them yesterday. The hunters around Pozi Mountain have been doing very well these past two days, they’ve hunted a lot of good stuff!”

The old man selling vegetables nodded.

“Oh, is that what the big deal is?”

“No, no, no, that’s not it!”

The pheasant vendor was acting mysterious.

“Do you know why there are so many wild animals? It’s because they all ran out from deep in the mountains, terrified! There was a monster haunting Pozi Mountain before!”

“Ah!?”

The old man selling vegetables was startled. In the ordinary streets of Daxiu, the existence of monsters was still just a rumor and legend, but people here knew more about them than in places like Dazhen.

Besides the stories passed down from their elders, it was also because the government had issued some special decrees.

For example, during the Spring Festival two years ago, some minor officials like the village heads posted notices everywhere in their jurisdiction, and even went door-to-door, warning people about certain supernatural things, even asking them to report any strangers who looked eerie and went out at night.

There was also a period when everyone was ordered that if they heard someone calling their name from behind at night, regardless of whether the voice was familiar or not, they should hurry away and immediately report to the authorities.

Such things didn’t happen often, maybe once every year or two, but each time they left a deep impression. The villagers secretly claimed that the government was investigating evil spirits.

Although the existence of the Heavenly Master’s Department of Daiu was not publicly announced, over time secrets spread. The people of Daxiu had heard of it and spread rumors and exaggerated stories, making it extremely mysterious.

Therefore, when the old man selling vegetables heard that it might be a monster, he didn’t think it was absurd at first, but was instead afraid.

Seeing that the old man’s reaction was as he had expected, the vendor selling wild game smiled.

“Haha, you don’t need to be afraid. I heard from people on Pozi Mountain that a few nights ago, there was a huge commotion in the mountains, as if the earth were turning over. You should remember that, right? I felt the ground shaking for a while when I was eating at home.”

“Yes, yes, I remember, but it came and went quickly.”

“Yes! It was at that moment, in just a short amount of time, that another giant peak appeared deep in the Pozi Mountains! It was a truly towering peak, even taller than the original peaks in the mountain range. It appeared in the sky in an instant, and then crashed heavily down into the mountain range!”

“Huh? Is that so? How could this happen?”

The vendor nodded, then subconsciously glanced in the direction of Pozi Mountain.

“It’s said that the mountain god subdued the demon by moving an entire mountain over it and trapping the monster beneath it!”

“Really?”

“Of course! People from the mountain village have already gone to see it. The mountain is magnificent. In the last couple of days, a lot of thorny vines have sprouted all around it, preventing people from getting close!”

Although the mountain villagers had not witnessed the monster being trapped under the mountain, they somehow guessed correctly, relying half on what they had seen and half on their imagination.

The cultivator from the Heavenly Master’s office only slowed down as he passed by, without stopping to inquire further. On his way back, he overheard people discussing the matter in two or three other places.

‘I never expected to hear news from ordinary people first. I wonder how the people guarding Pozi Mountain are doing now?’

With a hint of worry, the cultivator returned to the Heavenly Master’s residence, hoping that his superiors would send a highly skilled expert to assist him.

When the soldiers returned to report the situation, the Heavenly Master’s Office of Bianrong Prefecture reacted immediately. Instead of making a big show of it, they secretly sent people to Pozi Mountain to investigate, while also requesting assistance from the capital’s Heavenly Master Department. The cultivation level of the few cultivators in Bianrong Prefecture was simply insufficient to handle the situation.

It took two days for the exhausted soldiers to flee back to Bianrong Prefecture to report to the Heavenly Master’s Office. This was after requisitioning mules, horses, and carts found along the way. The Heavenly Master spent half a day discussing and researching the matter before immediately dispatching people to the capital.

Although Ji Yuan was amazed by the various miraculous techniques of cultivators, some of the supernatural abilities he took granted were still considered unattainable for some mid-to-lower-level cultivators.

For example, the art of flight.

To fly high, to come and go with the clouds and mist, to ride the wind, to walk on water… What Ji Yuan considered “basic skills for immortals” were supernatural abilities that only very few highly skilled individuals of the various Heavenly Master Departments of Daxiu possessed.

So much so that when the people from the Heavenly Master’s Office of Bianrong Prefecture went to investigate Pozi Mountain, they still traveled on horseback.

Pozi Mountain was not actually a small mountain range. At least, it was the largest mountain range in Bianrong Prefecture, with dozens of peaks of varying sizes. It even boasted what was considered a deep, ancient forest.

At this moment, a dozen martial artists and three immortal masters dressed in gray robes were trekking through the mountains. They were all agile and nimble, and although the mountain path was difficult, they were able to get through it with the help of their qinggong.

Many of the immortal masters at the Heavenly Master’s Office were actually practitioners of both spiritual and martial arts. In addition to spiritual techniques, they also knew some footwork and agility skills. Thanks to the nourishment of spiritual energy, they learned quickly.

However, as they grew closer to their destination, the mountain path became increasingly difficult to traverse. Just as the common people had described, it was covered with thorns and vines, and often required martial artists to cut a path with their swords.

“Huff… huff… huff… Fellow immortal masters, look, that mountain ahead should be it, right?”

A martial artist, slightly out of breath, pointed his hundred-fold-refined steel saber1 at the magnificent mountain peak behind the thin clouds in the distance. Whether they were immortal masters or other martial artists, they were all slightly astonished as they looked at the distant mountain.

“Pozi Mountain didn’t have this peak before, did it?”

“Replying to the Immortal Master, I have been to Pozi Mountain before, and I never saw such a majestic and precipitous peak here previously.”

“Hmm!”

Two of the immortal masters gazed at the mountain in contemplation for a moment, then took out a tortoise shell from a yellow cloth bag beside them. They then dropped copper coins into it and began to shake it, making a jangling sound. After a long while, they tilted the tortoise shell over, and several special copper coins floated in the air.

“How is it?”

A bystander asked with concern, but the diviner shook his head.

“It can’t predict anything. We can only keep going and see! We must be careful the rest of the way!”

“Alright! Let’s stop resting and set off!”

After another half hour of arduous travel, the group finally approached the mountain peak. They had asked the local villagers beforehand, who said that the mountain path in this direction used to be relatively easy to walk, but in the past few days even experienced hikers had found it difficult to traverse. They finally understood what they meant by “difficult.”

“This…this mountain was really pressed down with a spiritual technique?”

“It’s like moving mountains and filling seas!”

Not to mention the martial artists, even the immortal masters were extremely shocked. Before, through the clouds, they thought it was similar to other mountains, but when they got closer, they realized that the surrounding mountains could only be considered low peaks.

The group slowly approached the mountain. The mountain god Shi Youdao had actually discovered them long ago, but he recognized these people. Or rather, he recognized the clothes of the three cultivators. These were immortal masters from the Heavenly Master Department of Daxiu.

The Mountain God had not yet gained the country’s approval, so he was somewhat hesitant to show himself. However, of them were cultivators, not ordinary mortals; who knew if this would cause any changes in the mountain? This concerned the matter the immortal had entrusted him, so wouldn’t be appropriate to allow these people to approach.

Gritting his teeth, Shi Youdao decided to reveal himself.

When the group from the Heavenly Master’s Office had walked a certain distance, smoke suddenly rose from the mountain path ahead. Shi Youdao, clearly still in the form of a spirit, appeared before them.

“Monster!”

“Protect the Immortal Masters!”

“Shiiing—”

“Shiiing—”

“Shiiing—”

…

The martial artists immediately drew their swords, and even the immortal masters immediately went on guard.

“Don’t be afraid, don’t be afraid! I am not a demon, but a mountain spirit born of heaven and earth. I have appeared here to warn you that the road ahead is extremely dangerous. Please stop here!”

Shi Youdao bowed while quickly urging them to remain calm.

“A spirit? Do you know what’s happening up ahead?”

One of the immortal masters inquired. Shi Youdao nodded.

“A few days ago, two heavenly immortals arrived here and fought a powerful demon. One of the immortals used his spiritual power to create a mountain with his palm, and suppressed the demon beneath it. Yes, that’s the one.”

Shi Youdao pointed to the mountain behind him, and seeing the shocked expressions on the faces of the people in front of him. He continued.

“The demon beneath this mountain is quite formidable. If she escapes, she will surely bring devastation to the land. The two immortals, seeing that I am a natural spirit of this mountain, ordered me to advise those who enter the mountain not to get too close to the mountain-seal.”

Several immortal masters looked at Shi Youdao, who appeared to be a monster. One of them leaned close to the leader, Immortal Master Zhao, and whispered.

“The words of spirits and monsters should not be entirely believed.”

“Hmm!”

Immortal Master Zhao nodded and spoke to Shi Youdao.

“In that case, take us there to take a look. We need to investigate around this mountain, and we also need to climb up to see what’s going on. Don’t worry, we will not disturb the mountain at all!”

To be honest, Shi Youdao didn’t believe these people had the ability to break the seal on the mountain. What he was afraid of was the Golden-Armored Warrior thinking he hadn’t protected it well enough.

“Uh, please don’t make things difficult for me, everyone. The immortal ordered me to send people back, not to lead the way…”

“Stop talking nonsense! You, a mere spirit, dare to guard this mountain? You’re already spouting empty words. If you keep beating around the bush, we’ll capture you and send you to the Heavenly Master! Lead the way!”

“Yes, yes, yes!”

Shi Youdao was genuinely frightened. He had no choice but to lead these people forward, but deliberately avoided the area with the crevice.

When they grew close enough, one of the immortal masters looked up at the mountain and spoke to the martial artists behind him.

“Come with me up the mountain to observe the changes in the terrain! Let’s go!”

The group gathered their strength and leaped four or five zhang into the air, using their footwork skills to jump up the mountain. They intended to go directly up.

At that very moment, a sudden howling wind reached their ears.

“Whoosh…”

“Bang bang bang bang…”

It was as if they had been hit head-on by a carriage. The group was sent flying back through the air. At the same time, a ripple spread from in front of the mountain, golden light glowed, and a red-faced giant wearing golden armor appeared before them.

The Golden-Armored Warrior was now ten zhang tall, his body built like a small mountain. He stood before the mountain and gave the newcomers a sidelong glance.

“By order of the Honored One, I am to guard this mountain and protect against demons! Those who possess spiritual energy are not allowed to approach.”

The Golden-Armored Warrior’s voice rang out like the strike of an ancient bell, its booming sound echoing through the mountains and fields with a deafening ring, making people’s eardrums ache and causing the flowers, grass, and trees in the surrounding mountains and fields to tremble.

In order to make metal stronger, you hammer it, fold it over, hammer it again, fold it over, etc. This aligns all the metal grains in one direction, making it extremely strong against any perpendicular force. Hundred-fold doesn’t mean that it was folded 100 times (katanas are folded 8 to 16 times), but rather that it makes around 100 folds, which means it was folded around 6-7 times. A pretty good sword.

Chapter 488: This One Is a Bit Tricky

Because of their exceptional skills, the immortal master and several martial artists did not fall to the ground when they landed, but they still could not stop their momentum. They were pushed quite a ways away, falling back until they hit a tree or a companion.

The Golden-Armored Warrior had actually swept these people away in a single palm strike. Of course, the Golden-Armored Warrior didn’t use much force, otherwise he probably would have killed them.

The warrior’s voice not only shook the mountains and fields, but his physical size also brought a tremendous sense of oppression. Even a fool would know that the golden-armored giant in front of them was definitely not someone to be trifled with.

At this moment, Shi Youdao quickly came over and stood between the Golden Armored Warrior and the people from the Heavenly Master’s Office. He was really afraid that the Golden Armored Warrior would kill the Heavenly Master’s Office group. If these people died, it would be troublesome for this little monster.

“Divine General! Divine General! Please calm down! These people are all officials from the country of Daxiu. They are only here to investigate the situation and do not intend to threaten the seal!”

The Golden-Armored Warrior simply stood there, glancing sideways at the nervous Mountain God. The latter carefully turned to face the group from the Heavenly Master’s Office.

“Gentlemen, esteemed immortals, please quickly explain to the Divine General what you just told me. If you can’t explain it clearly, you might not be able to leave the mountain!”

“Why didn’t you tell us earlier that there was such a… such a Divine General here?”

An immortal master from the Heavenly Master’s Office angrily questioned the spirit in front of him in a low voice. Shi Youdao had no choice but to give a wry smile.

“How dare I reveal this matter so easily? You are all immortal masters from the Heavenly Master’s Office, but the Divine General only obeys the orders of the Supreme Immortals. If I say too much, retribution would be as easy as crushing an ant! Oh, now is not the time to hold me accountable. Quickly explain to the Divine General!”

The group from the Heavenly Master’s Office also realized what was happening. It was Immortal Master Zhao who stepped forward and led everyone in bowing respectfully.

“We, the cultivators of the Heavenly Master’s Office of Bianrong Prefecture, greet the Divine General!”

After bowing, the group cautiously looked up and saw that the Golden-Armored Warrior remained motionless, looking down at them with his eyes lowered.

“Leave.”

The rumbling sound, like rolling thunder, rang out again. The people from the Heavenly Master’s Office said “yes” repeatedly and hurriedly left. Shi Youdao did not dare be negligent either. He cupped his hands towards the Golden-Armored Warrior and followed the group from the Heavenly Master’s Office.

The immortal master and his party moved swiftly, not daring to stop even when thorns blocked their path. However, this time, the road was surprisingly easy to travel.

It wasn’t until over a quarter of an hour later, when they felt they were far enough away from the sealed mountain, that the group from the Heavenly Master’s Office dared to stop and rest. They all sat down on a pile of boulders.

Shi Youdao appeared out of nowhere, carrying a stone slab in his hand, atop which were some wooden cups filled with spring water.

“Fellow immortals, fellow officials, come, come, have some water. This is water from the mountain spring, it’s sweet and will quench your thirst.”

If Shi Youdao were to become the mountain god of Pozi Mountain in the future, a new minor god like him would definitely have to report to the Heavenly Master’s Department. It was impossible for him to abase himself to these people, but it was very necessary to cultivate good relations now.

“Thank you!”

“Thank you!”

“Thanks!”

This time, the group’s attitude was much improved. They took the wooden cups and thanked Shi Youdao. Only after the immortal master checked with his own methods and found no problem did he nod to let everyone drink with peace of mind.

After gulping down a cup of spring water, everyone felt much better.

“May I ask who you might be, sir…?”

“Oh, my name is Shi Youdao. I once aspired to become the mountain god of Pozi Mountain. Of course, without Daxiu’s permission, I do not dare establish a shrine. however, I have some knowledge of the mountain’s terrain and earth veins.”

“Oh, Fellow Taoist Shi, could you elaborate on the Golden-Armored Warrior and the demon suppressed at the foot of the mountain?”

Shi Youdao smiled and shook his head.

“It’s not that I am unwilling to go into details, but rather that I don’t know much. I only know that the suppressed demon is extraordinary and must not be allowed to escape. The Golden-Armored Warrior is one of its guards.”

‘One its the guards?’

The group from the Heavenly Master’s Office was startled. They exchanged bewildered glances, wondering if there were some other powerful guards as well. Clearly, no one had considered the monster before them to be one such guard.

“Thank you for informing us, Fellow Taoist Shi. Please keep in touch with our Heavenly Master’s Office and inform us of any news about this mountain. There is currently no temple or mountain god on this Pozi Mountain Range. If you are interested in the future, our Heavenly Master’s Office in Bianrong Prefecture is willing to vouch for you!”

Although he had received promises from Ji Yuan and the old beggar, Shi Youdao still showed surprise and quickly bowed to the people from the Heavenly Master’s office to express his gratitude.

“Thank you all, thank you all, esteemed immortal masters. I will certainly inform you if there are any developments! By the way, I would also like to trouble you all to post notices in your official capacity to warn visitors not to approach the sealed mountain.”

“As is our duty!”

“That’s right, that is indeed our duty!”

…

After the group from the Heavenly Master’s Office left, the golden-armored warrior did not disappear. Instead, he slowly turned around and faced the Fox-Suppressing Mountain, from which some faint sounds could be heard.

“Ouch…ouch…I’m in so much pain…”

Tu Siyan’s trembling voice drifted out softly, her head bowed, her eyes gleaming.

‘This must be the divine general!’

This was the first time Tu Siyan had ever seen the Golden-Armored Warrior. Even through the suppressing mountain seal, she could still sense that this divine general was extraordinary. She wanted to make some contact with him.

Sure enough, Tu Siyan’s voice caught the attention of the Golden-Armored Warrior, who slowly walked to the crack that led straight into the belly of the mountain.

“Hmm…is that the Divine General outside?”

Tu Siyan’s mournful voice rang out, as if she were enduring immense pain, before she slowly raised her head.

However, what greeted Tu Siyan was not the pity, curiosity, or vigilance she had imagined, but a pair of eyes devoid of emotion or expression, showing utter contempt.

Even through the slightest crack, the fox demon could imagine the towering warrior outside staring at her with utter indifference.

“May I ask your esteemed name, General?”

Tu Siyan waited for a while, but there was no response.

“My Lord General… I know my sins are grave, and I deserve this severe punishment. You must be following Mr. Ji… I…”

Tu Siyan couldn’t continue speaking at this point, because the general’s eyes remained unchanged from beginning to end, without the slightest ripple, just like… just like Ji Yuan’s eyes, as if he could see through everything.

Under the contemptuous gaze of this unknown divine general, it was as if all her calculations and verbal skills had been seen through. It was as if the other party were looking at a clown. There was no laughter, but the silence was more painful than mockery. Rather, it was a silent mockery in itself.1

‘Don’t be stupid, save your energy… I’m not like that foolish mountain god… A wicked creature is a wicked creature, what use are these sweet words… How pathetic…’

Such thoughts flashed through her mind as Tu Siyan gritted her teeth, trying to imagine what the divine general was thinking.

‘This one’s more troublesome!’

The saying “nothing can be swayed by emotion; nothing can escape through change”2 seemed to perfectly describe the current situation.

Ji Yuan knew that Tu Siyan was a formidable fox demon, and he was also well aware that she was not only highly skilled in demonic arts but also adept at manipulating people’s hearts. Therefore, he left a guard who would obey his orders to the death. No matter how skilled she was at manipulating people’s hearts, the Golden-Armored Warrior would not react to her.

…

As for Ji Yuan and the old beggar, they had already been in the capital of the country of Daxiu for two days.

Instead of going directly to the Imperial Preceptor of Daxiu, the two of them learned a little about the affairs of the Heavenly Master’s Department and the current situation of the Daxiu dynasty.

The old beggar probably thought Ji Yuan was afraid of getting into trouble by dealing with secular dynasties, while Ji Yuan had his own ideas. He was curious about Daxiu, this great country with a flourishing human society.

The main purpose of the journey was to observe the Heavenly Masters and Immortals they encountered. That evening, Ji Yuan planned to visit someone with the old beggar.

The sun was about to set. Qiao Yong stood up from the corner of the market, dusted himself off, and picked up the stool he had been sitting on. He put it in a large basket next to him. Then he took the carrying pole leaning against the wall, put it on his shoulder, hooked the two baskets on either end, steeled himself, and then lifted the carrying pole with all his might.

This was Qiao Yong’s stall. Apart from a scale,3 the baskets were mostly filled with vegetables such as cabbage and radishes. Only a bit of produce was left at the bottom.

Qiao Yong carried the shoulder pole steadily and walked slowly towards his home.

A young man in a blue robe and an old beggar in tattered clothes walked towards him. This pair was quite strange, but the people passing by seemed to ignore them obliviously, making Qiao Yong frown.

Qiao Yong kept walking, his eyes constantly glancing at the two people. He vaguely felt that the gentleman in the blue robe looked somewhat familiar, but to avoid offending anyone, he refrained from staring.

Just when Qiao Yong assumed they would pass each other, the two people stepped directly in front of him and stopped.

“Uh, are you two buying vegetables? I have some left in this basket. If you want them, I’ll sell them to you at a discount?”

The old beggar looked Qiao Yong up and down, then glanced at Ji Yuan, who also frowned slightly.

“Commander Qiao, how did things come to this?”

The person before him was none other than Qiao Yong, the Grand Commander of the fleet that had sailed for years in search of Xianxia Island.

Back then, Qiao Yong had led over two hundred ships4 of all sizes with more than 30,000 men on a grand voyage to search for Xianxia Island. He was no small figure, but now he was selling vegetables in the market.

Hearing the other man’s term of address, Qiao Yong looked at him with slight surprise.

“Sir, you are…?”

Ji Yuan smiled.

“What? Commander Qiao, don’t you recognize me? Back in the Eastern Sea, it was I who advised you to lead your people back home.”

Hearing this, Qiao Yong was startled. His eyes widened, his chin trembled slightly, and then his face lit up with joy.

“You, you are the Immortal Master? You are Immortal Master Ji from the Eastern Sea!”

“At least Grand Commander Qiao still remembers me.”

Qiao Yong was so excited that he shook his head and nodded at the same time.

“How could I forget, how could I forget? Immortal Master Ji has finally arrived! Would you like me to take you two to see the Imperial Preceptor? Oh, by the way, it’s getting late. Why don’t you two come to my house first, and I’ll entertain you properly? We can make further plans tomorrow, how about that?”

“Alright, let’s do as Grand Commander Qiao suggests.”

“Oh, oh, Immortal Master, you flatter me! I am just a commoner now, please do not address me with official titles! Come, come, please, Immortal Master. Although I sell vegetables, don’t let my appearance fool you. I have some resources, and will certainly be able to provide you with a proper meal!”

Overjoyed, Qiao Yong found it much easier to walk, his load much lighter as he lead Ji Yuan and the old beggar home.

Necessary to weigh silver and gold

The specific word used is 宝船 (bǎochuán), known as “Chinese treasure ships,” are a specific kind of historical ship from the fleet of Admiral Zheng He during the Ming dynasty, who conducted “treasure voyages” (read: colonialism and trade expansion) in the Indian Ocean. For the fellow boat enthusiasts, they’re a very particular kind of wooden ship called a junk ship, characterized by a central rudder, overhanging transom (end of the stern), watertight bulkheads, and a flat bottom. For non-boat-enthusiasts, just know the author was very particular about the style of ship for some reason.

Chapter 489: Qiao Yong, Unemployed

“Let’s go, let’s go, Immortal Master, it’s just ahead!”

“Alright.”

At each intersection, Qiao Yong, who was leading the way, would stop and point in a direction, afraid that Ji Yuan and the old beggar would get lost. Even though he knew the two were immortals, he couldn’t neglect this courtesy.

Although Qiao Yong was no longer a young man, he was clearly very healthy. He carried his haul with great speed, leading Ji Yuan and the old beggar onwards.

The old beggar watched Qiao Yong leading the way, while he and Ji Yuan walked behind, and spoke in a hushed tone.

“Mr. Ji, didn’t you say he was a high-ranking official who could take an old beggar like me out for a decent meal? We haven’t eaten or drunk anything for the past two days, we’re completely starving. In this situation, is there any way we can still afford a good meal?”

Ji Yuan smiled helplessly.

“If Mr. Lu is not satisfied, I will personally pay for a good meal and wine to entertain you at a restaurant, how about that?”

“Alright, you said it yourself, Mr. Ji, you can’t go back on your word, haha…”

“Ah, you…”

Ji Yuan’s amused smile was not forced at all. He was neither rich nor poor. He wasn’t exactly rolling in money, but he wasn’t short of money for a meal either. After all, the little bit of gold that Hu Yun had found was still almost untouched.

Having said this, the old beggar looked at Qiao Yong again.

“However, since Mr. Ji sent him back to deliver the message, even if you didn’t leave any strong evidence, those few words alone are worth considering. Surely it shouldn’t have led to Qiao Yong having to sell vegetables on the street?”

“That’s true. However, I observed that Qiao Yong’s complexion was rosy and he shows no signs of shortness of breath or weakness. Although his official aura is faint, it has not completely dissipated. So it can’t be said that he’s in a terrible state. Let’s wait and see.”

As the two were talking, they turned down an alley and came to a main street. Across the street was a rather grand-looking mansion. Qiao Yong pointed to it.

“Immortal masters, this is my home. Please come with me to my home and have a seat!”

As he spoke, Qiao Yong quickened his pace considerably.

The old beggar looked into the distance. He knew that Ji Yuan’s eyesight was not very good. Although the characters on the plaque were large, he still needed to get close to see them. So he kindly spoke:

“Mr. Ji, it says ‘Qiao Residence’ on it.”

Ji Yuan laughed.

“Even if I can’t see it clearly, I can still guess!”

The Qiao family mansion had barely maintained its former glory. There were no servants at the gate, and even the fallen leaves in front of the entrance had not been swept away.

Qiao Yong carried his shoulder pole and hurried to the door, knocking on the brass ring on the gate.

“Bang bang bang… bang bang bang…”

“A’De, A’De, open the door quickly, I’m back!”

“Coming! Coming!”

Footsteps approached, and a moment later, a side door next to the main gate opened from the inside, revealing an old man with gray hair.

“Master? Over here. What are you doing at the main entrance? And who are these two?”

Qiao Yong slapped his forehead and quickly said.

“Oh dear, A’De, quickly open the main gate! Open the main gate! We must welcome our distinguished guests! Open the main gate to greet our two immortal masters!”

“Huh? Oh, oh, oh, open the main gate, open the main gate…”

Qiao De was now the only servant in the manor. He had been with the Qiao family for decades, and even though the Qiao family had long since declined, he remained loyal to them.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar did not stop the Qiao family’s actions. Although it was a bit of a hassle, Qiao Yong valued this kind of etiquette, so they let him do it.

“Creak…”

The long-unused main gate slowly opened, and Qiao Yong, along with the other members of the Qiao family who had rushed over upon hearing the news, welcomed Ji Yuan and the old beggar inside.

In addition, Qiao Yong also instructed his wife and children to prepare a sumptuous dinner, emphasizing several times that it must be grand, before leading Ji Yuan and the old beggar to the main hall.

This mansion was originally the Qiao family mansion. Although it was no longer considered a prestigious residence in the capital, it was no small mansion either. Nowadays, there weren’t many people in the Qiao family mansion, and many of the wings were left vacant, since they could not be kept clean.

The Qiao family was naturally very curious about the arrival of Ji Yuan and the old beggar. While they were working in the kitchen, the family members continued to gossip.

“Is that an immortal? Why is there a beggar too?”

“Don’t talk nonsense, that’s just an illusion!”

“They seem quite different from the immortal masters at the Heavenly Master’s Office. Aren’t the latter all immortals, too?”

“Of course there are differences between immortals. You and I look completely different.”

“Stop joking around, start the fire, cook the rice, and I’ll go kill two chickens.”

“We’re getting chicken for dinner?”

“Great!”

…

The atmosphere in the kitchen was cheerful, while in the living room, after pouring tea for Ji Yuan and the old beggar, Qiao Yong told them about his experiences after returning home.

Although he had been wandering at sea for many years, it only took him a year to actually return to the harbor of Daxiu’s capital. Strangely enough, the journey back was smooth sailing. He did not encounter any storms, nor did he lose his way. He returned to Daxiu without incident.

When the emperor heard that they had returned, the entire palace was overjoyed, thinking that the fleet had brought back elixirs of immortality. He immediately summoned Qiao Yong and even dispatched the imperial guards and personal bodyguards to receive them.

The Emperor of Daxiu had dispatched three expeditions to seek the elixir of immortality, each to one of the three legendary secluded immortal abodes. The treasure ship fleet was sent to the Eastern Sea, which should have been the slowest to arrive and had the least hope of success. Unexpectedly, the fleet was the first to return.

“Haha, the emperor must have been overjoyed!”

The old beggar couldn’t help but tease him upon hearing Qiao Yong’s retelling, and mentioned that he had once tried to have an emperor as a disciple and had been beheaded. This Emperor of Daxiu was quite similar to the former Yang Zong.

Hearing the old beggar’s comment, Qiao Yong gave a wry smile.

“Who could deny it? In fact, even before my fleet entered the port, the Observation Bureau had already observed the fleet’s return. So when the fleet entered the port, there were already armored soldiers to greet it.”

Qiao Yong paused for a moment to recall before continuing.

“When I told the officials who came to greet us that we had not obtained the elixir of immortality, but only received a promise from Mr. Ji, the officials who came to greet us did not dare to report directly to the capital, and insisted on taking the officials of our fleet with them.”

“And then?”

Ji Yuan asked, and Qiao Yong continued recounting his memories.

“Upon arriving in the capital, he learned that we had not found the elixir of immortality, and that Master Ji’s words were merely a verbal promise. The Emperor believed the word of an immortal, but not necessarily mine… Furthermore, some officials impeached me, claiming that I had fabricated the story of the immortal’s message just to return from the sea. The Emperor was furious and dismissed me from my post and imprisoned me. If it weren’t for the Imperial Preceptor’s intervention, I might not have been able to keep my head…”

“It was my oversight, please forgive me, Mr. Qiao!”

Ji Yuan cupped his hands and bowed in apology. Qiao Yong startled so much that he stood up and repeated, “I dare not.”

Although Ji Yuan had admitted his oversight, he actually didn’t have any token to leave behind. Who was Ji Yuan anyway? People had definitely never heard of him. What good would leaving a token do? The news of the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly was far more convincing.

Ji Yuan’s mistake was that he initially thought the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly was a very secretive, high-level event that only people of certain status would know about. However, when he arrived at the Ruan Mountain Crossing, he realized that, although not everyone in the world knew about the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly, there were still quite a few. Just because someone knew about the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly didn’t mean their word was reliable.

“Did the Imperial Preceptor believe you?”

Ji Yuan asked again. Qiao Yong shook his head and answered honestly.

“Although the Imperial Preceptor left me enough room to maneuver, I cannot say he completely trusted me. He questioned me extensively about Immortal Master Ji and also about your relationship with Xianxia Island. My knowledge was limited, and I could only answer as best I could. Fortunately, the Imperial Preceptor is skilled in divination and calculated that I was not lying. He also said that when he tried to divine Immortal Master Ji, no matter how he calculated, it was all blank.”

Qiao Yong smiled sheepishly as he mentioned this.

“When I heard the Imperial Preceptor say those words to the Emperor in the Golden Hall, I was terrified. I thought that the Imperial Preceptor’s words meant something terrible was about to happen, and even the Emperor’s anger seemed to intensify. Fortunately, the Imperial Preceptor later explained that this could only be because I had encountered a truly powerful master. Barring the option of a supreme treasure, this master’s cultivation must have been far superior to his own to cause such a situation. That’s how I was saved…”

“After being imprisoned for half a year, news came of another expedition seeking immortality. However, Lord Li from that team escaped with his supplies and dared not return to Daxiu. The Emperor was naturally furious, but some of my former friends in the court took the opportunity to speak up for me, saying that at least I, Qiao Yong, had not forgotten the Emperor’s grace and knew that I must return to report the news. Even if I had no merit, I had still worked hard…”

Qiao Yong said with relief.

“Ah, His Majesty originally intended to imprison me until next year. If there were to be no news of the immortal I spoke of, I would be executed next autumn. But because of this matter, I was released half a month later and have been living in seclusion at home until today.”

“Actually, His Majesty has treated me quite well. Although he stripped me of my official position, he did not confiscate my property. However… those brothers in the fleet back then are not doing well. Those who are healthy are fine, but those who were injured at sea are having a hard time. Because of His Majesty’s anger, they did not receive full compensation. How can I feel at ease? I can only do my best to help them if I can.”

The old beggar nodded slightly to Ji Yuan. The two of them basically understood the whole story of the changes in the Qiao family. Even without any divination or verification, it was clear to them that Qiao Yong was telling the truth.

“Master Qiao is indeed kind-hearted.”

The old beggar commented with a smile, but Qiao Yong simply shook his head.

“I wouldn’t call myself kind-hearted, but I made a promise to my brothers that I failed to keep, and now I feel uneasy, right here!”

Qiao Yong said, patting his chest.

T/n: Man, emperors are all the same, huh?

Chapter 490: There’s a Faster Method

Qiao Yong had successfully led the fleet home, which, strictly speaking, did not break his promise. However, while he did bear the consequences as he had promised, he couldn’t shore up the damage.

Although his brothers weren’t imprisoned, they didn’t receive the rewards they deserved. Regardless of whether they were injured, they all lost their jobs. As for Qiao Yong, although he had been imprisoned, he was later released, and his family was safe and his wealth intact. But he always felt like he had a thorn stuck in his heart, making him restless and unable to eat or sleep.

Qiao Yong saying he was “doing his best to help them if he could” was not just empty talk. Ji Yuan and the old beggar could see this from the current situation of the Qiao family. Not only had they given away their gold and silver, but a large portion of the Qiao family’s once substantial land holdings had also been distributed. Although the Qiao family was not currently starving, the luxurious life they used to have was gone, and they had to rely on their own labor.

Getting to know Qiao Yong also meant getting to know Daxiu. In general, although the Emperor of Daxiu longed for the elixir of immortality, he was not so stubborn that he would be unwilling to listen to advice.

As for seeking elixirs of immortality, if it had been the previous Emperor of Dazhen, this would have been considered a neglect of his duties, as the path to immortality is elusive. However, Daxiu had its own Heavenly Masters. While most of the common people were ignorant, the upper-class had some understanding of the path to immortality. At least, they knew that there were truly powerful immortals in this world, so seeking an elixir of immortality was not purely a pipe dream.

At this moment, Qiao De, the old servant of the Qiao family, came from outside into the main hall and spoke.

“Sir, Madam says dinner is ready, and asks if you wish to start eating?”

Hearing this, Qiao Yong looked at Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

“Immortal Ji, Immortal Lu, shall we have dinner now?”

The old beggar smiled.

“Haha, this old beggar has been waiting for you to say that. Mr. Ji and I haven’t had a drop of water or a bite to eat for days, we’re starving!”

“Oh, oh, oh, A’De, hurry up and serve the food, hurry up and serve the food! Is the dining hall ready yet?”

“Yes, yes, it has been tidied up already. I’ll go to the kitchen first. Master, you can take the two immortals over now!”

With that, the old servant Qiao De bowed to Ji Yuan and to the old beggar in the main hall, took a few steps back, and turned to leave.

Ji Yuan watched the only servant of the Qiao family leave with a thoughtful expression. Through his blurry vision, he could see that Qiao De was full of vital energy. He turned his gaze to the old beggar and found that the latter was also looking at the departing old man.

Then the old beggar turned his gaze back and met Ji Yuan’s eyes. The two smiled at each other and said nothing.

“Please, esteemed immortals, come with me to the dining hall!”

Qiao Yong had already stood up and gestured for them to enter from the doorway.

A moment later, in the Qiao family’s dining hall, the entire Qiao family, along with the two guests, Ji Yuan and the old beggar, were seated at a large round table. The only one standing was the old servant, Qiao De.

The old beggar’s gaze swept across the dining hall, noticing some remnants of cobwebs and other debris. Ji Yuan sniffed. In addition to the aroma of the food, he could also smell a damp, dusty odor, the kind of smell that usually came after cleaning.

It seemed the dining hall hadn’t been used for a long time; it had been specifically cleaned for this event.

There were ten dishes on the round table. There were plenty of fresh vegetables, but the most eye-catching dish was a large braised fish and two large platters of poached chicken. There were also peanuts, dried radish, and other side dishes, plus a jar of wine. This was the whole of the Qiao family’s dinner.

Everyone at the table was watching Ji Yuan and the old beggar. No one touched their chopsticks. Madam Qiao and the eldest son of the Qiao family, who was already an adult, managed to keep their gaze from being fixed on the food, but the two young children had their eyes glued to the two plates of chicken.

Qiao Yong personally filled Ji Yuan and the old beggar’s wine cups, and said politely:

“Immortal masters, please eat! It is an honor for the Qiao family to have you here in our home!”

Ji Yuan looked at the old beggar.

“Well, Mr. Lu, weren’t you starving? Please have some.”

“Hahaha, then this old beggar won’t stand on ceremony! Tsk tsk tsk, these two plates of chicken are this old beggar’s favorite!”

The old beggar was naturally unreserved. Under the expectant gazes of the two young children of the Qiao family, he snatched a chicken thigh, then snatched second one and presented it to Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, this is quite a good dish. It’s commonly known in the market as ‘phoenix tail meat,’ this is for you.”

Ji Yuan waved his hand repeatedly.

“No need, no need. A gentleman does not take what others like. It’s fine if Mr. Lu eats it. I don’t like this kind of food.”

As he spoke, Ji Yuan picked up a few pieces of green vegetables from the soup bowl in the middle with his chopsticks and put them in his mouth. The delicious flavor of the vegetables filled his mouth as he chewed. It was clear that the soup had been made with chicken broth, and it was very good.

“Everyone, eat! It’s not right for the host to only watch and entertain the guests without eating.”

As Ji Yuan spoke, he reached out with his chopsticks and picked up two more chicken legs, but this time he put them in the two children’s bowls. The two children immediately looked at Qiao Yong, and when they saw the latter nod, they smiled and grabbed the chicken legs with their hands, dipped them in soy sauce, and started eating.

“Yes, yes, we’ll eat too! We’ll eat too.”

As soon as Qiao Yong spoke, the Qiao family members started eating. The atmosphere in the dining hall quickly became lively, with the children’s laughter adding even more vitality.

…

In the middle of the night, after the Qiao family had all gone to bed, Ji Yuan and the old beggar sat alone at the stone table in the courtyard of the guest wing. One looked up at the moon, while the other closed his eyes to rest.

Even a starved camel was bigger than a horse; although the Qiao family had declined, this house was still quite nice, and it could easily accommodate a couple of guest rooms.

At some point in the dead of night, Ji Yuan withdrew his gaze from the moon. The old beggar opened his eyes, and the two exchanged a slight smile.

A gentle breeze blew by. When it passed, the two immortals were no longer standing in front of the stone table.

Behind the Qiao family mansion was a small alley. At this moment, Qiao De, like a night owl, lightly leaped over the courtyard wall and landed in the alley outside the courtyard. He landed on his toes, his body slightly bent. The whole process was silent.

“Impressive qinggong!”

A calm and composed voice rang out, startling Qiao De, who thought he had gotten away unnoticed. He turned around and found Ji Yuan and the old beggar standing against the Qiao family’s courtyard wall.

The moonlight shone on the two of them, allowing Qiao De to clearly see the smiles on Ji Yuan’s and the old beggar’s faces.

“I am very curious, if neither Mr. Lu nor I had attended the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly, what would have happened to the Qiao family? And how would you, Qiao De, have conducted yourself?”

The old man’s expression shifted several times before he finally looked up and gritted his teeth.

“I know nothing about this Cultivators’ Grand Assembly. I only know that if no immortals were to have visited before next autumn, I, Qiao De, would risk my life to escort the master’s family out of the capital. They would find a place to stay wherever they went!”

The old beggar laughed heartily.

“Unexpectedly, he’s a double agent, but he does have some sense of loyalty!”

Qiao De shook his head.

“You flatter me, Immortal Master. Although I believe I have never done anything to harm the Qiao family, I am ultimately disloyal to my master.”

“Hmph, you’re not even a member of the Qiao family, so how could you be disloyal? Where are you going? To the Heavenly Master’s Office? You think you can hide such petty tricks from me and Mr. Ji?”

The old beggar asked with a sneer, and with a flick of his finger, a sachet flew out of Qiao De’s arms. He felt around it with his fingers; there was likely a talisman inside.

Qiao De didn’t hide anything.

“You two are truly masters. I will not lie. I am indeed going to the Heavenly Master’s Office. The Heavenly Master is idle in his residence and seems to be doing nothing, but how could he possibly be indifferent? It’s just that he has gone from overt moves to covert intelligence. But he has not done anything to harm us.”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“That’s true, but if we didn’t come before next autumn, who knows what would have happened…”

With a sigh, Ji Yuan turned to Qiao De.

“Go ahead and do what you need to do.”

Qiao De was taken aback.

“Uh, immortal masters…”

The old beggar said irritably.

“Of course we’re going back to rest. We haven’t slept or eaten for days and nights. After a good, filling meal, we’re exhausted. If it weren’t for you, you little rascal, this old beggar would have gone to bed long ago!”

“Haha, I’ve figured it out now. A good meal for Mr. Lu consists of two chicken thighs and a chicken head, plus half a bowl of soup and a few dishes. Next time I invite you to a meal, I’ll have a standard for what to order.”

“You, you, you! Ji Yuan, how could you say such a thing?!”

“Hahahaha…”

Amidst the laughter around him, Qiao De noticed that Ji Yuan and the old beggar were fading away from his sight, until they fully disappeared. He rubbed his eyes hard and looked around again, but there was indeed no one there.

After hesitating for a while, Qiao De quickly left, heading towards the direction of the Heavenly Master’s Office in the capital.

…

The next morning, Qiao Yong, who got up very early, naturally didn’t go to sell vegetables. Instead, he took Ji Yuan and the old beggar straight to the Heavenly Master’s Office.

As a crucial institution within the country of Daxiu, the Heavenly Master’s Office was situated within the incredibly valuable grounds of the Imperial City, right next to the Imperial Palace.

Unlike the relatively secluded nature of the Heavenly Master’s Offices of other regions, the one in the capital was opulent and grand.

On the one hand, it was partly because there were basically no common people who could enter the area around the palace; on the other hand, a location the emperor visited frequently couldn’t possibly be too modest, right?

Several guards stood watch outside the Heavenly Master’s Office. When they saw Qiao Yong and the two people behind him approaching with a very purposeful attitude, they all grew wary.

“Halt! What are you doing here? Don’t you know that civilians are not allowed to approach this place? Get out of here immediately!”

Qiao Yong quickly stopped and bowed to them.

“My lords, I was originally Qiao Yong, the Admiral of the Western Garrison Navy of our dynasty, and concurrently the Grand Commander of the Immortal-Seeking Fleet of the Eastern Sea. I once said that I had received guidance from an immortal to lead the fleet back to the court. Now that the immortal has arrived as promised, I have brought the two immortals to the Heavenly Master’s Office to see the Imperial Preceptor!”

“Qiao Yong!?”

“Admiral of the Western Garrison Navy?”

“It seems such a man did indeed exist!”

“Are those two immortals behind you?”

The Heavenly Master’s Office was filled with extraordinary people, but few were qualified to be called “immortals.” The term “immortal master” was merely a polite official honorific. For many, it would actually be more appropriate to address them as “master.”

“However, the Imperial Preceptor is not at the Heavenly Master’s Office. Why don’t you all come again tomorrow?”

Ji Yuan smiled.

“I know, the Imperial Preceptor and the Emperor of Daxiu are at the City God Temple in the capital.”

“Hmm!?”

The guards became wary, but before they could ask anything, Ji Yuan turned to the old beggar and asked him a question.

“Mr. Lu, what do you think we should do?”

“Haha, going back and forth is too troublesome, let’s use a faster method.”

Ji Yuan and the old beggar had decided before they arrived that they could afford to blow this matter a little out of proportion.

As the old beggar spoke, his spiritual power circulated throughout his body. He pointed to the sky and the earth, twisting his hand subtly. He then wrote spiritual characters on his left palm. The next moment, he gently patted the ground and spoke with utmost concentration.

“City God of Daxiu’s Capital, I invite you!”

“Bang…”

Ripples spread out like water, and a wisp of green smoke, accompanied by a slight whirlwind, appeared before the old beggar as it spun.

The figure was dressed in a black robe and wearing a black gauze hat, with divine light shining behind him, yet his face was filled with horror…

The City God of the capital of Daxiu stared wide-eyed in disbelief, his mouth slightly agape, at Ji Yuan and the old beggar.
