Chapter 491: Before He Could Even Say a Word

Almost everyone in the capital city had visited the City God Temple and offered incense there. Even if not everyone could remember the City God’s face, they could still recognize his appearance. Moreover, the City God was now radiating divine light.

“Lord City God!”

“Is this the City God?”

“It really is the City God!”

“I went to the City God Temple the day before yesterday, and the City God’s statue was dressed just like this!”

“What? Why is the City God here all of a sudden?!”

“It seems they were invited by that immortal master…”

“Invited? Invited??”

The guards could see it clearly; it wasn’t just an invitation, it was practically a summons.

Not to mention the guards, even Qiao Yong, who had come with Ji Yuan and the old beggar, was gaping in shock.

The country of Daxiu was not a small country. Its capital city had a large population. The City God of the capital of Daxiu had a close relationship with the dynasty and undoubtedly received their full support. Therefore, the City God’s spiritual power was naturally profound, incomparable to that of ordinary local deities.

Because he himself was well aware of this, the City God of the capital was even more astonished at this moment. He experienced momentary confusion and dizziness, almost like teleportation, when he was forced to appear here. In addition, by the sensations he had experienced in his inner world before appearing, the City God knew he had been directly summoned by a powerful being using the Spirit-Binding Technique.

Even though he was accustomed to his high and mighty status and filled with disbelief, the City God, upon realizing what had happened, did not dare neglect his manners and quickly bowed to the old beggar and Ji Yuan.

“Chu Ning, the City God of the Capital, greets the two immortal masters!”

Although the City God tried his best to suppress his shock, there was still a slight excitement lingering in his voice, which did not escape Ji Yuan’s notice. This was exactly the effect that he and the old beggar wanted to achieve.

“Well, please don’t be offended, City God Chu. Mr. Ji and I don’t have much free time. These guards said that since the Imperial Preceptor of the country of Daxiu is with you, we should come back another day. This old beggar thought that if he still wouldn’t be back soon enough another day, it would be best to see him today. We don’t know the City God Temple, so we wanted to find someone suitable to pass on our message.”

The old beggar spoke quite seriously, without his usual playful smile, but his words almost made Ji Yuan burst out laughing. Even a highly skilled immortal could be quite sarcastic.

The City God naturally understood the meaning behind the old beggar’s words and quickly responded.

“I will return to the City God Temple immediately to inform the Imperial Preceptor. By the way, would the two immortals be willing to see His Majesty?”

“Please ask the City God to invite the Imperial Preceptor over. Tell him that Ji Yuan has come to visit. I told Envoy Qiao about this matter on the Eastern Sea years ago. I hope the Imperial Preceptor hasn’t forgotten.”

Ji Yuan’s answer did not specify whether the emperor should come along; he only said to quickly invite the Imperial Preceptor.

The City God quickly responded to Ji Yuan.

“I will go immediately. The Imperial Preceptor has not forgotten this matter; he just mentioned it to me. I will go right away!”

After finishing his words, the City God bowed to the two of them again.

“I shall take my leave now!”

With that, the City God transformed into a silhouette and sped away towards the outskirts of the imperial city.

After the City God left, the old beggar smiled at Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, this old beggar’s action wasn’t too much, was it?”

“It was just right.”

Ji Yuan nodded in response.

The old beggar smiled and looked at the guards at the gate of the Heavenly Master’s Office. The guards reacted quickly and bowed.

“Immortal masters, we were blind to your true nature. We hope you will forgive us!”

As the guards bowed, voices sounded from inside the Heavenly Master’s Office, accompanied by footsteps. Three cultivators dressed in Taoist robes emerged.

Because of the commotion caused by the old beggar’s Spirit-Binding technique, several cultivators who had been practicing at the Heavenly Master’s Office were awakened. They rushed to the door as fast as they could and saw the guards bowing to Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

“Who are these two?”

When these cultivators looked at Ji Yuan and the old beggar, they couldn’t sense any power or divine light emanating from them. However, they didn’t even consider the possibility that they could be mortals — or at least, not ordinary mortals.

“Immortal masters, these two immortal masters just summoned the City God, and now the City God is going to see the Imperial Preceptor…”

The guard gave a brief account of the events leading up to this moment, but the three cultivators were more interested in the process of summoning the deity.

“They summoned the City God? Here?”

The three looked slightly puzzled. Then, one of the older ones suddenly paused, as if remembering something, and quickly bowed to Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

“Seniors, please come inside and rest, have some tea and snacks, and wait for the Imperial Preceptor to return!”

“Please come in, both of you!”

Ji Yuan glanced at Qiao Yong.

“Lord Qiao, let’s go in together.”

“Uh— ah, hey!”

…

Chaos had broken out in the City God Temple of the capital of Daxiu. Previously, the City God had been with the Emperor of Daxiu and the Imperial Preceptor in the main hall of the temple.

The scene had originally been one of drinking tea and chatting, and the conversation was about the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly. The old emperor was very concerned about whether the envoy sent by Daxiu could actually enter the venue of the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly.

The Imperial Preceptor and the City God of the capital both knew that the old emperor was overthinking things, and that there was absolutely no possibility that they would be allowed entry. The former had already advised the emperor, but he was still not convinced, so he came to the City God Temple to ask the City God again.

City God Chu Ning was also helpless, and continued to explain.

“Your Majesty, I have been in charge of the underworld in the capital for many years, and I am considered a deity in some way. I have certain channels of information. This Cultivators’ Grand Assembly is a grand gathering of immortals, and it is very difficult for a mortal court to…”

Before he finished speaking, the City God’s physical form suddenly blurred for a moment, transformed into a whirlwind of smoke, and vanished from the main hall in a flash.

“What!?”

The Imperial Preceptor Daxiu suddenly stood up.

“Protect His Majesty!”

“Guards, protect His Majesty!”

“Protect the Emperor! Protect the Emperor!”

The emperor’s guards grew agitated, and in an instant, several top imperial guards leaped into the hall, while the guards outside tensed up.

“Imperial Preceptor, where is the City God? Has he been captured by demons?”

The old emperor also looked surprised, glancing at the equally astonished Imperial Preceptor, Men Yutong.

If the Grand Preceptor had seen the old beggar perform the Spirit-Binding Technique, he would have recognized it. However, recognizing the steps to perform a technique did not mean that he could recognize what a deity looked like when it was being bound. Therefore, he was somewhat bewildered and a little nervous.

“Your Majesty, do not panic. I am here and will ensure your safety. Everyone, remain vigilant! I beseech the gods of the underworld to appear and protect His Majesty together!”

One by one, the chief officials of the underworld appeared, accompanied by yin messengers.

The tense atmosphere in the City God Temple lasted for a while, before being broke when City God Chu Ning returned. He hurriedly fled back, reappearing at the temple entrance and quickly walking back to the main hall of his temple.

“Your Majesty, Imperial Preceptor!”

“City God Chu, what just happened? Why did you suddenly disappear?”

Hearing the Imperial Preceptor’s question, and seeing both his and the old emperor’s expressions, Chu Ning shook his head.

“No, no, it’s not that I disappeared, but that I was bound!”

“Bound? Could it be that such a powerful demon has entered my capital?”

The old emperor’s words were tinged with surprise, but the Imperial Advisor’s reaction was even greater.

“Could it be… Spirit-Binding!?”

The City God nodded.

“Indeed, it was the Spirit-Binding Technique that captured me, the City God of this great capital, and brought me before them to serve.”

“What is ‘Spirit-Binding’? Oh, Imperial Preceptor, and City God Chu, stop playing games and tell me!”

Seeing that the old emperor was anxious, the Imperial Preceptor quickly apologized and explained.

“Your Majesty, please forgive me, but this news is truly astonishing. The art of Spirit-Binding is a mysterious and extraordinary technique, and very few people in the world possess it. Only those with great supernatural powers can achieve it. Moreover, City God Chu is the rightful City God of the capital of our Daxiu, and his divine position is one of immense power. To bind him would mean that the opponent’s cultivation level is immeasurable!”

“By the way, City God Chu, where is the immortal who cast the Spirit-Binding Technique on you, and what were their instructions? Why did you return so quickly?”

City God Chu looked at the Imperial Preceptor, then at the Emperor.

“There were two immortal masters in total. The one who bound me was an immortal who resembled an old beggar. The other looked like a refined scholar and wore a blue robe. The one in the blue robe was Immortal Master Ji, who left the message on the Eastern Sea. The two of them were at the Heavenly Master’s Office, and came to see you, Imperial Preceptor. Oh, and Qiao Yong was also there.”

“They came for me? Why would they need to use the Spirit-Binding technique and be so serious about looking for me?”

The Imperial Preceptor was clearly taken aback. It was like having a band of musicians beating drums as loudly as possible by his bedside when a single shake could wake him.

“Your Majesty, a scout did indeed report last night that the Qiao family received two distinguished guests, a scholar in a blue robe and an old beggar. The Qiao family also killed two chickens and bought a fish.”

“What? You’re only telling me now?!”

The old emperor turned and glared at his personal guard, who immediately knelt down on one knee.

“Your Majesty, I deserve death! I thought it was just the Qiao family entertaining guests, and there was no need to alarm Your Majesty!”

Clearly, although Qiao De claimed he was going to report to the Heavenly Master, he was actually contacting someone from the royal family.

The Imperial Preceptor shook his head. He seemed to understand why the other party had used such an unusual technique as Spirit-Binding. He bowed to the Emperor and the City God.

“Your Majesty, City God Chu, since a master has come to see me, I shall take my leave now.”

“Um, Imperial Preceptor, may I accompany you?”

The City God quickly interjected.

“I just asked the two immortals if they were willing to see His Majesty. Immortal Ji told the Imperial Preceptor to come quickly, but he didn’t say he wouldn’t see His Majesty.”

“Is that so!”

The emperor’s face lit up with joy.

“Then I’ll go too!”1

The Imperial Preceptor thought for a moment and nodded to the Emperor, but then solemnly gave him a word of advice.

“Your Majesty, have you thought this through? This journey may not be auspicious!”

The old emperor was taken aback, narrowing his eyes thoughtfully, but after hesitating for a moment, he still gritted his teeth and spoke.

“But I must go!”

…………

“His Majesty has arrived!”

“The Imperial Preceptor has arrived!”

Inside the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office, Ji Yuan and the old beggar were drinking tea when a loud voice came from outside, indicating that the main figure had arrived.

The Imperial Preceptor, Men Yutong, had arrived with the old emperor. When they arrived in the main hall, he had already dismissed his attendants.

Qiao Yong quickly rose from his seat, walked briskly to the center of the hall, and bowed deeply in the direction of the emperor.

“This humble servant Qiao Yong pays his respects to His Majesty and the Imperial Preceptor!”

“Dear Minister Qiao, please rise!”

The old emperor left the Imperial Preceptor behind and quickly entered the hall. He personally went to Qiao Yong and helped him straighten up. He looked at Qiao Yong with concern, nodded, and then slowly turned to face Ji Yuan and the old beggar, bowing respectfully.

“You must be the two immortal masters. I pay my respects!”

The Imperial Preceptor stepped forward and bowed deeply to Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

“This junior, Men Yutong, pays his respects to the two seniors!”

Ji Yuan and the old beggar did not take advantage of the other party, so they also got up and returned the greeting with a slight bow. Then the former addressed the old emperor.

“Although Your Majesty is the ruler of a nation, you have no business here, so we will not keep you here to listen.”

As soon as he finished speaking, Ji Yuan flicked his sleeve, and the old emperor and Qiao Yong felt their bodies sway and become unsteady. The surrounding scenery blurred and changed. When they came to their senses, they were already outside the courtyard, surrounded by the male and female attendants and guards who had spread out around the main hall.

“I… couldn’t even say a word?”

T/n: well I’m certainly not going to stop him from graciously delivering his own head to the chopping block

Chapter 492: A Terrifying Demonic Aura

“Your Majesty, it seems we were sent out by the immortals inside?”

The guard approached the old emperor and cautiously asked.

“I can tell.”

The old emperor looked around and found that none of the immortal masters from the Heavenly Master’s Office had come out. Apart from the Imperial Preceptor, the three people who were originally inside with Ji Yuan and the old beggar were still in the main hall. He then turned his gaze and noticed Qiao Yong standing to the side with a nervous expression.

The old emperor’s face softened, showing a kind expression.

“Good Minister Qiao, tell me how you met these two immortals. Did they say anything special? Guards, prepare a table, chairs, tea, and refreshments. Minister Qiao and I want to have a long talk here.”

Qiao Yong was flattered and repeatedly said “I shall obey, Your Majesty.” After the servants set out the tables and chairs, he sat down with the emperor and began to recount in detail his encounter with Ji Yuan and the old beggar.

As soon as he started talking about how he had been closing up his stall after he had finished selling vegetables for the day, the old emperor interrupted him with a look of astonishment.

“What? Minister Qiao has fallen to the point of having to sell vegetables to make a living? Is there a treacherous official in the court persecuting you? I was only showing compassion for your years of hard work overseas, asllowing you to rest at home for a while, hoping that you would continue to serve the country in the future. How could you be in such dire straits?”

Regardless of whether the emperor was truly unaware or feigning ignorance, Qiao Yong, as a mere commoner, did not dare to act as though he had a right to use his connection to an immortal for personal gain. He remained cautious and deeply grateful for the emperor’s concern.

“Your Majesty’s concern is truly overwhelming! No one has harmed me. It is only because I felt guilty towards my former brothers. Knowing that they were struggling, I gave them all my wealth and land, and lent them a small helping hand. Of course, everything is fine at home; I have enough to eat and wear. Selling vegetables is also a way to cultivate my character, indeed it cultivates my character…”

The old emperor did not show any pleasure upon hearing this; instead, his expression turned serious.

“I have failed my brothers, Good Minister Qiao. Tell me in detail how I have failed you. I remember that our dynasty provides a considerable amount of pension funds. Could it be that someone has embezzled them?”

A fierce glint appeared on his face as his gaze swept over the guard beside him. The guard felt a chill run down his spine and immediately understood the emperor’s meaning, bowing respectfully with his fists clasped.

“Your Majesty and Minister Qiao, please wait a moment. This humble servant will go and investigate immediately!”

After saying this, the guard slowly retreated, then used his agility skill to quickly leave. He didn’t know if someone had truly embezzled funds, or if it was the emperor who had given this order from the beginning, but regardless, from this moment on, someone must have embezzled funds to cause Qiao Yong’s old subordinates to suffer. No matter who held the fault, the emperor could not be wrong.

Seeing the guard disappear, the old emperor sighed and looked at Qiao Yong with a kind expression again.

“Oh, Good Minister Qiao, you’ve worked hard. Come on, continue, speak slowly!”

“Yes, yes, Your Majesty, as you command…”

Qiao Yong dared not refuse. He sat up straight to explain in detail.

On a tree in the courtyard, a paper crane kept its eyes fixed on the emperor and Qiao Yong, and glanced in the direction the guard had left.

…

Inside the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office, the Imperial Preceptor and the other Heavenly Master’s Office cultivators saw Ji Yuan send the Emperor and his retinue away with a wave of his sleeve, but did not dare say anything more, let alone mention any crime of disrespect.

For powerful figures like the man in the blue robe and the old beggar, there was virtually nothing forbidden to them. Whether one was a worldly noble or an emperor, a demon or monster, no status had any effect on them.

“I heard from Lord Qiao years ago that Mr. Ji had something to ask me. I wonder what brings you two esteemed immortals here today?”

Men Yutong didn’t mention Ji Yuan’s promise to contact Xianxia Island for them. He only spoke of Ji Yuan and the old beggar’s requests, even though he knew very well that the old emperor would likely be more concerned about the immortal’s promises.

“Haha, Imperial Preceptor, there’s no need to be so formal. To tell you the truth, the reason Mr. Lu and I went to such lengths was simply to seek a small amount of justice for Qiao Yong, as I owe him. As for why I came to you…”

As Ji Yuan spoke, the old beggar perked up his ears. He had been asking Ji Yuan about this matter before, but the latter was always so secretive. Surely he had to tell him now at last; he couldn’t just kick him out, could he?

Ji Yuan thought for a moment before speaking.

“The Imperial Preceptor gave Qiao Yong a jade pendant that could distinguish between good and evil. I wonder if the Imperial Preceptor still remembers it?”

Men Yutong nodded.

“Of course I remember. It was a Xiezhi pendant. Its main function was to provide early warnings, so that the treasure ships and the fleet could be alerted in advance, prepare for danger, and proceed quickly if they encountered good fortune.”

“Hmm.”

Ji Yuan nodded, lost in thought.

In truth, this world was full of all kinds of animals, monsters, and spirits. There were all sorts of bizarre creatures, from dragons, to phoenixes, to deities. It was normal for other mythical beasts and strange creatures to exist. But so far, apart from dragons and phoenixes, Ji Yuan had not been able to accurately learn about any other mythical beasts.1 In fact, there were even more dragons than phoenixes.

The Xiezhi was a highly representative mythical beast. Ji Yuan had clearly heard someone else explain that had almost the same definition as the mythical beast from his memories of his previous life.

“Then, how did the Imperial Preceptor obtain such a supernatural artifact? Or rather, does the Imperial Preceptor know anything about the Xiezhi beast?”

“Xiezhi?”

The old beggar muttered to himself. It was the first time he had ever heard of such a beast’s name. It seemed to be a monster with a very powerful background, otherwise Ji Yuan wouldn’t have been so interested.

The Imperial Preceptor first bowed to Ji Yuan and the old beggar, then reached into his right sleeve and took out a scroll from the dimensional storage artifact within. It appeared to be a container for either a writing scroll or a painting.

“Immortal Master, please take a look!”

As he spoke, Men Yutong approached Ji Yuan and the old beggar, slowly unfurling a foot-long scroll in his hand, revealing a painting.

The painting depicted a majestic and powerful mythical beast covered in thick, dark fur. It had bright, piercing eyes, a large horn on its forehead, strong limbs, sharpened hooked claws, a short tail, a thick body, a large mouth, and long teeth.

“Roar…”

The image of the mythical beast was still while it was being unrolled, but it came to life after it was fully unfurled. It roared towards the world outside the painting and swayed its body from side to side, as if it wanted to break out of the frame. It even brought an undeniable pressure.

“Roar…”

The old beggar and Ji Yuan stood up again, the latter because he was slightly excited, and the former because he was awestruck by the imposing aura of the strange beast in the portrait.

“What kind of monster is this? Why has this old beggar never seen one before? What is it? Does Mr. Ji know?”

At this moment, the Imperial Preceptor gripped the scroll tightly with both arms, his body radiating spiritual power. It seemed as though this painting was not something that could unfurled so easily. Ji Yuan had already taken a few steps closer and stood in front of the painting, squinting as he answered.

“This is the Xiezhi, also known as the One-Horned Beast.2 It understands human speech and human nature, and is an ancient divine beast that can distinguish good from evil.”

Men Yutong initially thought that Ji Yuan was just curious about the jade pendant he had seen at sea. Hearing the old beggar’s question, he wanted to answer for Ji Yuan. However, upon hearing Ji Yuan’s words, he immediately realized that this immortal knew far more about the beast in the painting than he had imagined. Not even Men Yutong knew the alternative name “One-Horned Beast”.

“An ancient divine beast? A Xiexhi?”

The old beggar’s face was solemn.

“Mr. Ji, could you elaborate?”

Ji Yuan shook his head.

“I only know a little bit about it. Let’s ask the Imperial Preceptor first. Is this the only painting you have?”

Men Yutong nodded.

“This is the only painting, but it changes at different times, and will also react after being infused with spiritual energy and power.”

As he spoke, Men Yutong channeled his spiritual energy into the painting. The next moment, the colors of the portrait seemed to become more vibrant, and the beast in the painting became more lifelike.

“Roar… I am the Xiezhi, who dares to disturb me here? I am the Xiezhi, who dares to disturb me here? Roar…”

“Well, in most cases, it only utters these two phrases.”

Men Yutong explained to Ji Yuan and the old beggar. Both of them seemed to be deep in thought. Ji Yuan first narrowed his eyes, then opened his supernatural eyes and focused his spiritual power in his gaze. He then stretched out his right hand, and a golden-red feather appeared in his palm.

As the feather appeared, Ji Yuan began to subtly infuse it with a wisp of spiritual energy. A terrifying demonic aura rose up. However, this demonic aura only existed at the level of intent, and ordinary people could not perceive it at all.

But the old beggar frowned and looked at the feather. He could see nothing wrong with it, but why had he felt a faint palpitation just now?

Ji Yuan couldn’t help but glance at the old beggar again. Judging from his reaction, the old beggar’s skills were indeed a bit stronger than Ju Yuanzi’s.

“Xiezhi, do you recognize this?”

Ji Yuan spoke calmly, holding the feather in his hand before the painting. However, the divine beast in the portrait still spoke the same sentences as before.

Ji Yuan originally intended to put the feather away, but suddenly thought of something and spoke to Men Yutong.

“I would like to trouble the Imperial Preceptor to infuse some more spiritual power and spiritual energy into the painting.”

“Yes!”

Men Yutong was, after all, a cultivator at the level of a Master. With a thought, his whole body shimmered with divine light, and a continuous stream of spiritual power and energy flowed into the painting.

A moment later, black smoke began to rise from the ferocious-looking xiezhi depicted on the scroll.

“Roar… I am the Xiezhi, who dares to…”

The words suddenly stopped. The divine beast in the painting made a movement that had never been seen before, its gaze fixed purposefully on the feather in Ji Yuan’s hand.

“Heh…”

A hoarse and terrifying roar emanated from the scroll. The Imperial Preceptor felt the painting become much heavier and hotter, with wisps of black smoke almost overflowing from it.

“Heh…”

The voice grew even deeper, but the sense of oppression it brought was even greater.

“Sizzle sizzle…”

Smoke rose from Men Yutong’s hands. The scroll in his hands resembled a branding iron. He could hold a real branding iron, but he could barely hold onto this scroll, and his arms began to tremble slightly.

‘As expected, there’s a reaction!’

Ji Yuan turned to the old beggar.

“I would greatly appreciate it, Mr. Lu, if you would take over for the Imperial Preceptor and imbue the painting with spiritual energy.”

“Hmm!”

Without further ado, the old beggar took the painting from Men Yutong’s hands. Anyone with eyes could see that the Imperial Preceptor was about to collapse, but once it was out of his hands, the painting immediately stabilized.

“Let me see what’s going on!”

The old beggar and Ji Yuan exchanged nods. The old beggar then increased the amount of spiritual energy poured into the painting, almost entirely filling it with spiritual light. Ji Yuan did the same, pouring even more spiritual energy into the feather.

Whoosh…

A wisp of smoke shot out from the painting, condensing into a ball of ethereal black flame, while the feather in Ji Yuan’s hand ignited into a wisp of ethereal golden-red fire. The two flames collided, stirring up a powerful demonic wind.

“Whoosh… whoosh…”

A fierce wind howled inside the main hall, and several immortal masters almost lost their footing, not because the strong wind, but rather because of overwhelming fear.

Demonic energy! A terrifying demonic energy!

The old beggar stared in horror at the scroll in his hand and Ji Yuan’s feather, then at Ji Yuan’s solemn expression.

T/n: tfw you realize your buddy has been carrying around the equivalent of a nuke made of demonic energy in his pocket and you may have just helped him set it off

To clarify: JY has not come across any other beasts from classical Chinese mythology in this world, just your generic dragons and miscellaneous spirits. None of the named ones… (except, if you’ll recall, the “Sky-Devouring” Peng, which I’m sure will come up later :D)

獬 (xiè): part of a compound word that roughly translates to mythical beast, 廌 (zhì): unicorn

Chapter 493: A Clash of Power

Even with his current shock, the old beggar knew that now was not the time to ask questions. Since Ji Yuan had let him participate in this experiment, he would definitely explain the whole story to him, or at least a good part of it.

Ji Yuan knew very well that although he called the Xiezhi a divine beast, this classification was only based on his memories from his previous life. The divine beast he had such memories of, to cultivators, exuded a terrifying monstrous energy. After all, even a True Dragon was considered a divine beast, yet it was still a monster.

Of course, there were a few exceptions, such as the legendary phoenix and other such beasts, which possessed a sacred aura. Of course, immortal beasts practicing orthodox immortal arts also counted among that number.

At this moment, to the senses of the old beggar and the cultivators at the Heavenly Master’s Office, the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office was filled with monstrous energy, a terrifying monstrous energy unlike anything they had ever seen before.

The cultivators at the Heavenly Master’s Office could comfort themselves, thinking that their terror was due to their limited knowledge. After all, they knew very well that there were always mountains beyond mountains. At the Heavenly Master’s Office, they were revered as “Immortal Masters,” but in reality, they were not considered significant figures in the cultivation world.

But the old beggar was different. He was a highly skilled immortal with profound cultivation. He had not only seen many demons, but also subdued many monsters, including powerful monsters like True Dragons. Yet, none of them could match the monstrous aura currently emanating from in front of him.

The demonic energy in the hall wasn’t particularly strong, since it was only released by the vessel in his hand, and couldn’t possibly blot out the sky like a great demon would. But even this small amount of demonic energy had a chilling quality.

“Whoosh…whoosh…”

Ji Yuan continued to increase the input of spiritual energy and power, because he had not yet seen the change he wanted to see. At the very least, the clash between the two monstrous energies was not intense enough yet.

Since Ji Yuan didn’t stop, the old beggar naturally wouldn’t stop either. Without Ji Yuan’s instruction, he tacitly increased his own input of spiritual energy and power as well.

The actions of these two powerful immortals caused considerable hardship for the cultivators of the Heavenly Master’s Office in the palace. Whether it was the Imperial Preceptor Men Yutong or the other cultivators, they had all long lost their usual calm and aloof demeanor. They were all forced into a corner, and even resorting to casting techniques on each other to try to resist this terrifying monstrous energy.

Even though the monstrous energy didn’t cause any real harm, it still made the Heavenly Masters sweat profusely, mobilizing all their spiritual power to support the array they had set up in order to increase their psychological comfort.

A while later, the feather in Ji Yuan’s hand remained unchanged, but the scroll in the old beggar’s hand began to tremble slightly. It wasn’t that the old beggar could no longer hold onto it anymore, bur rather that the scroll itself was trembling rhythmically. The old beggar did not try to counteract this change.

“Sizzle… sizzle sizzle…”

Smoke began to rise from the old beggar’s palm. The scroll began to shake more and more, to the point that the mythical beast in the scroll even began to blur.

“Roar…roar…roar… Get lost!”

The ethereal black monstrous energy on the scroll suddenly solidified, transforming into a sharp claw formed from black flames. It seemed to leap out of the scroll, viciously grabbing at the golden-red feather.

Ji Yuan grasped the feather with his right hand and tucked it behind his back. He rotated his wrist with his left hand, making a three-finger array with his index finger, little finger, and thumb, forming the Mountain-Shaking Seal from his own original Wondrous Technique of Heaven and Earth. As lightning flickered at his fingertips, he struck out with lightning speed to meet the black claw.

The moment they made contact, Ji Yuan felt an intense, suffocating sense of oppression. This was only a strong sensory stimulation; the actual power was not exaggerated, at least not to the point that Ji Yuan would have trouble withstanding it.

“Boom…”

With a loud bang, a ripple exploded from the point of contact, like a boulder crashing into the once calm water, stirring up the water.

“Crack-crack-crack-crack-crack…”

“Sizzle… sizzle… hiss hiss hiss…”

Many of the curtains in the main hall were torn apart. The floor tiles crumbled, and even the pillars were cracked. The impact swept directly against the four walls.

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

“Clatter clatter clatter clatter…”

Some of the windows were directly slammed open by the impact, and the original doors were all swept shut. The wooden shutters all made a loud banging sound as they clattered and broke open one after another. Tiles flew off the roof of the main hall.

“Whoooosh… whoosh whoosh whoosh…”

A blast of air rushed out of the main hall and swept outside, raising a cloud of dust like a raging storm in front of the main hall.

Outside the courtyard, the Emperor of Daxiu and Qiao Yong were startled by the commotion, and those who were sitting nearby almost immediately stood up.

“Protect the Emperor!”

“Guards, protect the emperor!”

“Protect the Emperor!”

“Shiiiiiing…”

“Shiiiiiing…”

“Shiiiiiing…”

The guards, whose nerves were already on edge, tensed up once again. This time, they drew their swords and protected the emperor and Qiao Yong in layers.

“What happened?”

“It’s still unclear!”

“It sounds like the commotion is coming from the Imperial Preceptor’s side?”

The old emperor glanced at Qiao Yong, then at the equally bewildered guards, and commanded the latter.

“Go and see if the Imperial Preceptor and his men are in trouble, and whether they need assistance.”

“Yes, Your Majesty!”

The two left quickly, and another guard gave a suggestion.

“Your Majesty, shall we escort you away first?”

The old emperor looked across the courtyard wall at the buildings in the direction of the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office. He shook his head.

“No rush. If it’s not safe here, we can hide in the palace.”

Although the commotion came suddenly, it subsided even faster, completely calming down two breaths after it began.

Separated only by two courtyards, the two guards used their agility skills to leap to the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office. The first things they saw were the fallen wooden window shutters and several door panels.

Looking further ahead, they saw that the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office had undergone a complete transformation.

With the keen eyesight of a martial artist, they could see the two immortals standing in the middle of the hall, while the Imperial Preceptor and others were positioned against the wall behind them.

Almost all the floor tiles were shattered in a circle centered on Ji Yuan and the old beggar. The closer they were to the center, the more severe the damage. Apart from the few tiles upon which the old beggar and Ji Yuan were standing, all the other tiles in the very center had been reduced to dust. The shattering of the outer tiles was also very regular, radiating out in the shape of tree branches and lightning bolts, extending all the way to the walls, causing cracks to appear on all four walls of the main hall.

The curtains that were originally inside the hall, whether they had been drawn down or rolled up, were now mostly shredded, some yellow scraps of cloth still falling like snowflakes.

As for the doors and windows, the paper windows were all torn, many of the wooden shutters had been blown out, and even the doors were all crooked.

“Imperial Preceptor, fellow immortal masters, are you all alright? His Majesty is very worried about this place and sent us here to investigate. Is there anything we can do to help?”

One of the guards asked very cautiously in a suitable tone. Men Yutong immediately responded from outside.

“Please go back and tell His Majesty that it was nothing serious. Do not alarm him for the time being. You may leave now.”

The guards looked at each other in bewilderment. The main hall was almost demolished, and yet he was saying nothing serious had happened?

But they dared not disobey the Imperial Preceptor, so they could only clasp their hands in agreement and slowly retreat away.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar had already withdrawn their spiritual energy and power after their initial clash. Now, the two of them were looking at the scroll and the feather with solemn expressions, focusing particularly on the scroll in the old beggar’s hand.

The old beggar turned his gaze back to Ji Yuan, focusing particularly on Ji Yuan’s left hand.

“Mr. Ji, are you alright?”

Ji Yuan looked at his left hand. It was clean, without even a scratch. Although it was a little numb, he reassured himself that his hand had been tempered by the Heavenly Tribulation Lightning, and should be fine. He nodded.

“It’s alright.”

Their gazes returned to the painting. The mythical beast within had calmed down. It now appeared as though it were an ordinary street vendor’s item, but no one here would mistake it for one.

At this moment, Imperial Preceptor of Daxiu and several Immortal Masters were regulating their breathing, seemingly having just stabilized. Ji Yuan looked at them and asked.

“Imperial Preceptor, where did you obtain this painting?”

Men Yutong suppressed the shock in his heart, stepped forward, and answered with clasped hands.

“In response to Immortal Master Ji, I found this painting in the royal treasury of the country of Daxiu. At first, I only thought it was extraordinary. Later, based on the characteristics of the painting, I used the spirit transfer and copying technique to refine the Xiezhi pendant, which has the divine effect of bringing good fortune and warding off evil. However, I did not know that it contained such profound mysteries.”

The method of spirit transfer and copying was a commonly used and wondrous technique in the immortal path. There were many schools of thought that studied it. Generally speaking, it involved imitating a certain divine object or a certain creature, absorbing its essence face-to-face, then sealing it and refining it using specific methods. To a certain extent, one could obtain some spiritual abilities that were quite similar to the copied object.

“Does the Imperial Preceptor think that the royal family knows the origin of this painting?”

Ji Yuan pressed on, unwilling to give up, while simultaneously calculating a divination in his sleeve.

After thinking it over carefully, Men Yutong shook his head.

“It seems that the source is unclear. It is unknown where it was confiscated from or which emperor left it here. When I first obtained it, I also wanted to investigate its origin and even asked His Majesty, but I found nothing.”

As soon as Men Yutong finished speaking, Ji Yuan also stopped his divinations, finding nothing. The old beggar opposite him also shook his head.

“I can’t calculate anything.”

After saying that, the old beggar added another sentence.

“Mr. Ji, shall we still give it a try?”

Ji Yuan slowly let out a breath, raised his right hand to look at the feather in his palm, and then put it into his sleeve.

“No, I’m not going to try.”

Although he didn’t get any clear information from the Xiexhi painting, the intense clash of the two monstrous energies convinced Ji Yuan even more that the feather in his hand was extraordinary, at least a being of the same level as the Xiezhi.

“Hmm.”

The old beggar nodded, slowly closing the scroll in his hands. There were slight scorch marks on his palms, but they faded quickly and disappeared after a few breaths.

“The beast in this painting is a Xiezhi, so where does the feather in Mr. Ji’s hand come from? Could you tell this old beggar about it?”

The old beggar couldn’t resist now; he was simply itching to ask.

Ji Yuan didn’t hide anything and frankly stated his guesses.

“I have two guesses: one is the Golden Crow, and the other is the Bifang.1 The former may be closer in appearance, but although the latter’s feather color is a little different, it’s not impossible.”

The old beggar subconsciously glanced at the scroll in his hand. The two names that Ji Yuan had mentioned were ones he had never heard of before.

The Bifang is a mythological one-legged bird that resembles a crane, said to be an omen of fire

Chapter 494: Golden Thread

In fact, there were many powerful mythical creatures or ferocious beasts with feathers. In his previous life, Ji Yuan was just an ordinary office worker, and a science and engineering major at that; he was not some great expert in history and mythology. The first two that came to mind and had made the greatest impression on him were the Golden Crow and the Bifang.

Of course, the old beggar was unaware of any of this, and naturally could not point out whether Ji Yuan had missed anything. He was currently thinking about those three names, the Xiezhi, the Golden Crow, and the Bifang, trying to find a place in his memory where he might recognize them, but it turned out to be in vain. The old beggar was certain that this was the first time he had ever heard of these three creatures.

While Ji Yuan and the old beggar were whispering to each other, the Imperial Preceptor Men Yutong and several other cultivators from the Heavenly Master’s Office also cautiously approached.

“Immortal masters, are you alright?”

The old beggar smiled at them.

“It’s nothing, it’s nothing. Mr. Ji and I made a little commotion, which startled you all.”

After looking around, Ji Yuan bowed slightly to the Imperial Preceptor and the group of cultivators, offering his apologies.

“I couldn’t stop myself for a moment and damaged the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office. Please forgive me.”

“No problem, no problem! You two immortals need not worry about it. These are just worldly possessions. The imperial court will send people to repair them.”

Even if anyone truly cared about the main hall, Men Yutong and the others would never dare to blame Ji Yuan and the old beggar. Their curiosity, however, had been piqued.

Men Yutong couldn’t help but ask a question similar to the one the old beggar had asked earlier.

“Immortal masters, that demonic aura just now was truly terrifying. What exactly did that commotion represent? And what kind of strange beast does the feather in Mr. Ji’s hand belong to?”

The old beggar looked at him and shook his head.

“There are some things that are better left unknown.”

Although he had never heard of the Xiezhi, Bifang, or Golden Crow,1 the old beggar had no doubt that these creatures truly existed or had existed in the past. And these things now seemed to be some kind of secret, something that ordinary people should know little about.

Indeed, in the old beggar’s eyes, these cultivators from the Heavenly Master’s Office were pretty much just “ordinary people”.

Upon hearing the old beggar’s words, the Imperial Preceptor and the others quickly agreed without suspicion. Ji Yuan, standing to the side, remained silent, thus minimizing his explanations.

Remembering what the Imperial Preceptor had said about the painting having come from the treasury, Ji Yuan’s eyes flashed, and he had an idea.

“May I ask, Imperial Preceptor Men, if I wish to visit the treasure vault, would you be so kind as to grant me permission?”

Ji Yuan knew that neither the emperor nor his ministers would object to his request; this was merely a formality.

As expected, Men Yutong immediately assured him.

“Rest assured, Master Ji, His Majesty will definitely agree.”

Meanwhile, on the other side, two guards hurriedly left the main hall and returned to the courtyard where the emperor and his entourage were waiting. There, they found the emperor under heavy protection.

Although the emperor had previously said there was no need to return to the palace, believing that neither place would be much safer than the other, his actions betrayed his true thoughts.

In the short time it took for the two guards to come and go, the area had already been surrounded by imperial guards and experts, and they even set up talismans specifically crafted to protect the royal family, looking ready for imminent battle.

Upon seeing the two guards return, everyone breathed a slight sigh of relief. The old emperor asked with concern.

“How is it? What exactly happened over there? Are the two immortal masters still alive? Is the Imperial Preceptor alright?”

The two guards exchanged a glance, and one of them stepped forward to answer.

“Your Majesty, the Imperial Preceptor and the other immortals are all unharmed. However, the main hall of the Heavenly Master’s Office was almost completely destroyed, with everything inside and outside shattered. It seems to have been caused by some kind of supernatural force. The Imperial Preceptor told us that they were not seriously injured, and asked us to return first.”

“Do you know what kind of immortal technique it was?”

When the old emperor pressed for an answer, the guard could only shake his head.

“The Imperial Preceptor had given the order, and we did not dare to linger. We only know that it must have been caused by the arrival of those to immortals today.”

“Is there anything I can do to help?”

“The Imperial Preceptor said there was no need for help.”

The old emperor glanced in the direction of the main hall, sighed slightly, and turned to a eunuch beside him.

“Pass on my decree: instruct the Ministry of Works to begin preparations for the repair of the Heavenly Master’s Office.”

“Yes, Your Majesty!”

The eunuch bowed and slowly withdrew, then quickly departed. Even if the emperor didn’t specify a deadline, one could never assume there was no rush. On the contrary, that would likely be the most urgent matter to settle.

“Minister Qiao, surely Master Ji and Master Lu won’t leave immediately?”

Hearing the emperor’s question, Qiao Yong had no choice but to answer with a forced smile.

“This… this humble subject does not know either. How can we mortals presume to speculate on the actions of immortals?”

Just then, someone saw the Imperial Preceptor Men Yutong hurrying in from outside towards the courtyard, which invigorated the old emperor and his entourage. When he approached, Men Yutong asked directly.

“Your Majesty! The two immortals have a request: they hope Your Majesty will open the treasure vault so that the immortals may have a look inside. What is Your Majesty’s opinion?”

Upon hearing this, the old emperor’s face lit up with joy, and he quickly replied.

“Granted, this Emperor grants his permission. Not only is a visit permitted, but whatever the Immortal Masters desire, they may take from the treasury as much as they please!”

For the old emperor, wealth was naturally no obstacle, but it still depended on the circumstance. In this situation, it was better to forge a good connection with the immortals than to have mountains of gold and silver.

As he spoke, the old emperor leaned closer to Men Yutong and lowered his voice.

“Imperial Preceptor, give me a definite answer. These two immortals, just how powerful…?”

The old emperor didn’t finish his sentence, but Men Yutong understood his meaning and nodded slightly in response.

“These are the most powerful individuals I have ever seen in my life. Even at a grand gathering of immortals, they would surely be seated in a high position.”

Hearing this, the old emperor’s smile deepened. He immediately inquired about when the two immortals would arrive at the treasury, hoping that they could take as much gold and silver as possible.

…

An hour later, most of the imperial guards and palace experts who had been protecting the Imperial Palace’s highest-tier treasury were withdrawn. The old emperor arrived alongside the Imperial Preceptor, Ji Yuan, and the old beggar.

This treasure vault was enormous, with thick walls and sturdy doors, and talismans drawn throughout.

Two skilled guards led the way, reaching the bronze gate first. They used keys to open two enormous and unusual bronze locks. As the keys were inserted into the locks, Ji Yuan could hear a series of “click-clack” sounds from inside before the gate finally opened.

“Creeeak… Creeeak…”

The slightly harsh sound of metallic hinges squealing filled the air as the two guards pushed the door open.

The old emperor gestured forward.

“Please come in, Immortal Masters. You are welcome to anything in the treasure vault that you fancy; there is no need to report it, and there is no limit to the quantity!”

Ji Yuan and the old beggar simply nodded to the emperor and stepped into the treasure vault together, while the Imperial Preceptor followed a step behind.

Upon entering the treasure vault, even though Ji Yuan could not see clearly what lay around him in his blurry vision, he still felt that it was magnificent and resplendent, all due to the light reflected from the gleaming gold.

Yes, as the imperial palace’s highest-tier treasury, even silver was utterly unworthy of being stored here. Besides various precious jewels and other treasures, the most abundant item here was gold, and this treasury is probably half the size of a football field.2

Some of the treasures here were neatly categorized and stored away, while others were simply piled together, gleaming a dazzling gold.

Even with Ji Yuan’s composure, he couldn’t help but gasp slightly. He had never seen so much money in either of his lives. It was too extravagant. Thinking about the gold ingot in his hand, which he originally considered a fortune, he felt utterly shamed in comparison.

However, this feeling was similar to that of an ordinary person watching a joke. After a brief laugh, Ji Yuan focused his attention back on more important matters at hand. The Imperial Preceptor spoke up at this moment.

“Immortal masters, I found the Xiezhi painting on that shelf over there.”

As he spoke, the Imperial Preceptor led the two to a calligraphy and painting bookshelf slightly further inside, where scrolls of calligraphy and paintings were neatly arranged, with various labels on the shelves.

The labels listed the names of various famous calligraphers and painters, some even specifying the period, while others are marked with phrases like “miscellaneous artist, details unknown.”

Ji Yuan opened his supernatural eyes wide, scanning up and down, but saw nothing unusual. The old beggar also activated his divine eyes to observe the surroundings.

After a long while, the two looked at each other and shook their heads slightly.

“Let’s look around.”

“Yes.”

Thus, the two of them wandered through the palace’s treasury, truly searching for treasures.

Outside the treasury, the old emperor was also somewhat anxious. He wasn’t afraid that the items would be taken away; rather, he was afraid that nothing would be of interest to them. Based on his experience, the latter was more likely.

Sure enough, half an hour later, the three people who had entered the treasure vault emerged. Before the old emperor could ask anything, the Imperial Preceptor, who was a step behind, had already winked at him and shook his head slightly, indicating that the two immortals had not taken anything.

“Thank you, Your Majesty, for opening the treasury to us. I have truly broadened my horizons!”

“This old beggar is also amazed. This is the first time in my life I’m seeing so much money. Heh, it seems that all the wealth in the world has been gathered here.”

The old emperor could only force a smile and respond with, “you flatter me,” although he was quite anxious inside.

He hadn’t considered letting Ji Yuan and the old beggar look at the other treasure vaults, because only the Imperial Treasury contained all kinds of treasures, among which were the most likely items to be noticed by immortals: antiques and curios. The other treasure vaults were all gold and silver, and even those had been recast into ingots, offering nothing of interest.

“Why don’t you two immortals stay for a meal? You must not have had a good meal at Minister Qiao’s house yesterday. The imperial cuisine in the palace is excellent. Why don’t you join us?”

“Haha, no need. The food at Qiao Yong’s house was quite plentiful. This old beggar ate until his belly was round and full, enough to keep me full for eight to ten years.”

Ji Yuan just smiled.

“I have no need to either.”

What to do? What to do? These two were clearly leaving. The old emperor appeared calm, but his mind was racing.

An old eunuch cautiously approached the emperor and whispered in his ear.

“Your Majesty… you’ve forgotten that only a part of the treasures are in the Imperial Treasury. The really good ones are all in the imperial study and the various palaces…”

The old emperor’s eyes lit up.

“Yes, yes, yes! Immortals, the treasures here are only a part of the collection. Many precious items are actually in the palace. There are quite a few in my imperial study. Shall we go and take another look?”

Ji Yuan thought for a moment and replied.

“Very well, let’s go take a look!”

The old emperor then personally led them to the imperial study. This time, not only did the Imperial Advisor accompany them, but the old emperor himself also walked inside to introduce Ji Yuan and the old beggar to what he considered to be the most noteworthy items.

The Imperial Study was neither too large nor too small, and contained many precious items. Ji Yuan’s discerning eye swept over them, finally settling on a scroll, either of calligraphy or of painting, resting on a bookshelf. Or rather, his gaze rested not on the scroll, but on the thin gold thread binding it.

Ji Yuan informed the old emperor of his intent, walked to the shelf, and took down the scroll. He untied the thread and pulled it out, ignoring the scroll itself. Then he held it in his hand to examine it closely. The old beggar also came over.

This golden thread did not actually display any spiritual or divine light, but it had a very unique characteristic, which the old beggar named in a whisper.

“Interesting! This golden thread doesn’t even belong to any of the five elements!”
