Chapter 499: Her Escape Is Certain

Soon, Ji Yuan and the old beggar flew to Pozi Mountain and landed on the top of the mountain where the fox demon was suppressed. The Golden-Armored Warrior appeared at the foot of the mountain, bowed respectfully towards the summit, then disappeared again.

Unlike Ji Yuan and the old beggar, Tu Siyan was unaware that the two had returned to this place. At this moment, she was still racking her brains for a plan to escape.

Tu Siyan had to consider that if no fox in the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern knew she was being suppressed by an immortal, and no other external force came to her rescue, she refused to be suppressed for a hundred years. A hundred years was longer than a lifetime for mortals, and it was no shorter for a fox demon.

The mere fact of being completely unable to move, and for even the slightest movement to cause pain, would be unbearable for anyone. After only a few days, Tu Siyan could no longer endure this oppressive predicament, but her rationality kept her calm.

‘There will definitely be a way, there will definitely be a way. The Mountain God is easy to deal with, but that general is too difficult to handle. He never speaks to me, nor does he ever show any expression. I can’t guess what he’s thinking at all. And the Mountain God is so afraid of him. He’s impervious to reason and ruthless. This kind of person is the hardest to deal with…’

‘Or should I beg Ji Yuan and that old beggar for mercy? They’ll definitely come back; they can’t possibly let me stay here for a hundred years. But I don’t know when they’ll return. It’s a pity Ji Yuan took away my ancestor’s hair. If it were still there, things wouldn’t be like this…’

Tu Siyan was somewhat annoyed. She regretted that she had been so stubborn and fought with the old beggar. If only she had been more patient. She could only blame herself for underestimating the old beggar who had suddenly appeared.

‘No! What do you mean by underestimating the old beggar? This was all a trap set by Ji Yuan. What use is it if I underestimated him?’

On the mountaintop, Ji Yuan and the old beggar looked down, as if they could see Tu Siyan through the mountain.

“She’s awake after all. No wonder she’s an eight-tailed fox demon. We can’t judge her by ordinary standards. Mr. Ji, let’s hurry down and meet her. Then we need to find a quiet place on Nine Peaks Mountain to continue our discussion. We also need to find suitable Earth-element items and refine the five elements first. There’s a lot to do!”

Ji Yuan looked at the old beggar, somewhat confused as to whether it was him or the old beggar who wanted to refine a spiritual weapon, as the old beggar seemed even more excited than him.

“Let’s go down and take a look.”

With that, Ji Yuan took the lead and slowly drifted down from the mountaintop like a falling leaf, eventually landing at the foot of the Fox-Suppressing Mountain, right in front of the crevice.

The Mountain God Shi Youdao had been keeping an eye on the mountain and naturally noticed Ji Yuan and the old beggar’s arrival. After appearing and bowing, he retreated again. He saw that the divine general had also bowed and left, so he gave up the idea of ​​trying to ingratiate himself, fearing that he might displease the master.

“Is Miss Tu awake?”

Ji Yuan’s voice echoed through the mountains, startling Tu Siyan. After a moment of panic, she quickly calmed down and responded in her uniquely alluring voice.

“Yes, with such a huge mountain pressing down on me, I was almost crushed into mincemeat. How could I not wake up? Sir, you are truly ruthless and cruel. That sword strike caused Siyan excruciating pain…”

Tu Siyan’s voice grew increasingly sorrowful, but Ji Yuan and the old beggar simply smiled and watched from the mountainside without any further reaction.

Ji Yuan had already explained Tu Siyan’s situation to the old beggar, as well as their encounter in Dazhen and the other fox demon that seemed to be from the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern.

The old beggar understood the whole story. Their relationship was definitely not one of friendship, but they didn’t also have much of a right to be considered Ji Yuan’s enemies. For now, he could only beat them verbally.

“Hmph, so you’re supposed to be true immortals, you two heartless people! Speak up, what do you want to ask? As long as you promise to let me out, this lady will tell you everything I know.”

Before Ji Yuan could speak, the old beggar answered with a smile.

“The thing is, it’s Mr. Ji who wants to ask questions, but this mountain was left by me, this old beggar. He can ask his questions, and I can seal it off. Even if he gets his answers, I can’t just release you directly. How about this, if Mr. Ji agrees, I won’t suppress you for a hundred years, but reduce it to sixty years, how about that?”

“What?! You old beggar, don’t go too far!”

Tu Siyan said angrily.

“Alright, Tu Siyan, let me ask you this: why did you give the young master of the Xiao family such a vicious talisman in Dazhen? Did you know that if it were allowed to develop, it might disrupt the dynasty and shake the force of humanity of Dazhen?”

Tu Siyan did not answer immediately, but spoke in a lazy tone after a few breaths.

“Oh, Mr. Ji, you’re talking about that young couple? I had to think hard to remember. I was deeply moved by their sincere love, so I helped them out. As for the talisman, sir, you’re exaggerating. It’s not that powerful… Even if it did have some effect, I can only say that I meant well but did something wrong. My intentions were good; I even volunteered to serve those men on the brothel ship for that woman, however…”

Tu Siyan smiled as she said this.

“However, if it weren’t for that, I wouldn’t have met Mr. Ji on that pleasure boat. Although we spent only one night together, my memory of it is still vivid.”

Hearing this, the old beggar glanced at Ji Yuan. Knowing full well that the old beggar was pretending not to understand, Ji Yuan could only shake his head helplessly in response.

“And that day, they were the ones who injured my fox clan first. Well, at least that’s what I saw. I was angry, so my methods were a bit too extreme. If you two immortals are dissatisfied, I apologize here.”

Ji Yuan smiled and said calmly.

“Miss Tu is all silver tongue. Stop trying to twist the facts. You impersonated a deity to bewitch the young master of the Xiao family, leaving behind a vicious blood curse. Ordinary mortals don’t have strong wills, how could they resist using the blood curse? That blood talisman of yours was taken by the True God of the Tongtian River. If it were an ordinary person, they would have been drained of their life essence long ago. You also disguised yourself as a courtesan to absorb people’s vital energy, and even tried to steal my Green Vine Sword. Furthermore, I also saw a fox in the country of Zuyue. That fox recognized me upon meeting me. It must have been you who sent the message. Jade Fox Sacred Cavern is far away in the Lan Province of the Western Regions, yet we encountered two foxes from that sacred cavern in a corner like Dazhen. Do you, or rather, does the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern have any plans?”

“I’m innocent, Mr. Ji! You’re putting a huge accusation on us. We were just traveling around. The world of swords is mysterious and wondrous, and I’ve been fascinated by swords since childhood. I just wanted to see it for myself. That’s why I fled to Heng Province in the Northern Territories after offending you. The matter with the Xiao family was also an accident. I’ve lived in a secluded paradise for a long time and am ignorant of worldly affairs. How could I know that mortals are so weak… As for what happened on the pleasure boat…”

Tu Siyan’s voice became soft and charming.

“Mr. Ji, do you really think that it’s not worth it for a mortal to sacrifice a little bit of his yang energy to be intimate with me?”

The old beggar couldn’t help but snort coldly.

“Hmph, you vixen!

“Mr. Ji, I don’t think she’ll say anything now. There’s no need to waste time with her. Let’s talk about it again in a hundred years.”

As he spoke, the old beggar also gave Tu Siyan a cold look.

“You sly fox, you don’t need to play any tricks. Let me tell you the truth, even if the Nine-Tailed Celestial Fox were to come in person, you wouldn’t be able to escape!”

Tu Siyan, who had originally intended to deal with the situation gently and evasively, immediately flew into a rage when she heard the old beggar mention her ancestor.

“You filthy beggar! A single hair from my ancestor’s head was enough to overwhelm you. Stop spouting nonsense!”

The old beggar simply closed his eyes to rest and stopped arguing with Tu Siyan.

“Mr. Ji, you should return my ancestor’s hair to me. Otherwise, my ancestor will definitely be unhappy. It’s a small matter if I am punished, but it’s a big deal if my ancestor misunderstands that you are hostile to my Jade Fox Sacred Cavern. The previous events were all misunderstandings, and I am willing to patiently sort out my thoughts…”

Tu Siyan didn’t say a single honest word. Ji Yuan and the old beggar exchanged a glance, and the latter transmitted his voice mentally, “Let’s deflate her arrogance.”

“It’s good to see you so energetic beneath the mountain. Let’s talk about it after the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly.”

As Ji Yuan finished speaking, a gentle breeze blew around them, and he and the old beggar slowly rose into the air.

Only then did the Golden-Armored Warrior reappear, bowing towards the direction where Ji Yuan had ascended into the sky. The Mountain God Shi Youdao naturally followed suit.

Tu Siyan frowned slightly, knowing that Ji Yuan was playing hard to get. However, after a short while she suddenly realized that she was talking to someone on the level of a True Immortal with a clear and natural state of mind. Such people were carefree and unrestrained, and their actions were in harmony with their knowledge. If they said they were leaving, they might really have left.

“Ji Yuan? Old beggar? Ji Yuan? Come back! Come back!”

Tu Siyan shouted several times but received no response. She angrily slammed her fist on the ground.

“Ugh! I’m so angry!”

The duration of the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly was completely unpredictable; it might end a few months after it officially began, or it might continue for several years due to the popularity of the Taoist discussions.

As soon as Ji Yuan left, Tu Siyan, still feeling annoyed, saw a pair of indifferent yet contemptuous eyes appear through the crevice in the mountain. This made her grit her teeth in hatred, and she muttered resentfully, “Like master, like slave.”

Just as she was growing angry, Tu Siyan suddenly felt a stirring in her heart. She could vaguely smell a slightly familiar scent, and she was immediately delighted. It was neither demonic nor spiritual energy; it was just a very faint scent.

It was the scent of the gray fox from before. Before Tu Siyan and the old beggar fought here, she had already used a technique to send the fox away. She thought the little beast had run away on its own, but she didn’t expect it to have the guts to come back.

As the sun gradually set and the sky darkened, faint singing could be heard from the depths of the Fox-Suppressing Mountain in the Pozi Mountain Range.

“Moonlight reflected in the water~ I comb my hair before the bright moon~ My hand caresses the moon’s reflection in the water, the ripples mirror my heart, lonely, sorrowful, heartbroken…”

The Mountain God listened quietly underground, finding the song both beautiful and melancholic.

However, before she could sing for long, the Golden-Armored Warrior appeared and threw a punch at the mountainside.

“Bang……”

“Crackle…”

The mountain seal pressed down heavily.

“Ugh…you dog servant, you just wait, aaah…”

The pain of the seal was temporary. Looking at the shallow crack, a cold smile appeared in the darkness; the fox’s scent had vanished.

Now Tu Siyan no longer worried that no one would know about her suppression; she was certain she could escape no matter what.

Chapter 500: Mountain God Jade, Wish Coin

After riding on the white clouds for some time, carried by the gentle breeze, the old beggar looked back at the direction of the Pozi Mountain Range and shook his head, saying:

“This fox didn’t tell a single truth from beginning to end.”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“However, sometimes you can still get information even if everything is a lie. At least Tu Siyan seems a little unsure whether the Nine-Tailed Fox in the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern knows about her suppression.”

The old beggar thought for a moment and agreed.

“If Tu Siyan were really plotting something, and it was at the behest of the Nine-Tailed Fox, then the Nine-Tailed Fox would definitely be keeping a close eye on her. The Nine-Tailed Fox would definitely have sensed it when Tu Siyan was suppressed.”

At this point, the old beggar withdrew his gaze from Pozi Mountain and turned to Ji Yuan again.

“So, it’s more likely that the fox is causing trouble on her own?”

Ji Yuan shook his head.

“It is not yet certain.”

He had encountered quite a few strange things over the years. Of course, this was a world with gods and monsters, and the world was so vast that anything could happen. Ji Yuan cared deeply about the world, making him prone to overthinking.

“It’s a pity that she’s ultimately an eight-tailed fox demon from the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern. It’s not good to deal with her too harshly. Otherwise, if we stripped away her three vital energies and prevented her from attaining enlightenment in this life, she would understand how insignificant she is!”

The old beggar’s words stirred Ji Yuan’s heart once again. He was very curious about what the old beggar meant by “stripping away her three vital energies” and how it could be accomplished. However, now was not the right time to ask, so he could only make a note of it and find an opportunity to discuss it later.

At the same time, Ji Yuan was also pondering the old beggar’s words.

The world was vast, and information was abundant. Ji Yuan could only enrich his experience and knowledge little by little through contact. He now had a certain understanding of the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern.

Just as the True Dragons ruled over the world’s water and four seas, the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern was also a place of considerable importance in the hearts of some demonic races. Although they had no formal endorsement, it was a kind of conventional balance.

There was no absolute fairness in this world. The old beggar dared to suppress Tu Siyan for a hundred years, but he still had to consider the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern, and would not kill Tu Siyan directly. He had never been to the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern, but the Nine-Tailed Celestial Fox held that kind of importance.

As for Ji Yuan, he was even more cautious than the old beggar. Besides his concerns, he had countless thoughts running through his mind, and he had even begun to consider whether to make a move with Lu Shanjun.

“Mr. Ji, what are you thinking about?”

Seeing that Ji Yuan remained silent for a long time, the old beggar couldn’t help but ask a question curiously.

‘Doesn’t Ji Yuan want to know how this old beggar can cut off someone’s Three-Flower Qi? Such a wondrous method is almost never known outside! Or perhaps he’s pondering the meaning behind my words?’

The “Three Flowers Qi” was just the old beggar’s own terminology. In fact, the appearance of the Three Flowers1 after attaining enlightenment was a rather abstract concept, the so-called “first glimpse of the wonders of profound mystery”.

There were many ways to sever someone’s foundation for cultivation, such as breaking their meridians, destroying their energy points, or damaging their primordial spirit. However, no immortal or Taoist text ever mentioned cutting off the three vital energies. Such supernatural powers or strange arts were inherently extraordinary.

“Oh, nothing, nothing. I was just thinking about playing chess.”

The old beggar curled his lip, realizing he had been overthinking things; Ji Yuan wasn’t really considering the matter of cutting off the Three Flowers.

“Let’s discuss the matter of refining spiritual artifacts, Mr. Ji. Ruan Mountain Ferry is bustling with activity right now. Let’s go take a look; perhaps we can find some earth-element objects that are qualified to be refined into a spiritual weapon.”

“Alright, let’s go!”

The two increased their speed on the clouds and flew towards Nine Peaks Mountain.

The Ruan Mountain Ferry Crossing market, which was under the jurisdiction of Nine Peaks Mountain, was still bustling with activity. Or rather, this market was at its most lively during the Cultivators’ Grand Assembly, and was a place where humans, demons, spirits, gods, and immortals coexisted to a certain extent.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar wandered around, looking at all the market stalls and searching all the “brand” buildings, but they couldn’t find anything suitable. Or rather, they couldn’t find anything that the old beggar thought was suitable.

Inside a treasure pavilion called “Lingbao Pavilion”,2 a cultivator wearing a short robe with a hanging token enthusiastically led Ji Yuan and the old beggar to the second floor.

“Fellow Taoists, please be careful of the barriers in the building. Please take your guest tokens. My Lingbao Pavilion happens to have a rare earth-element treasure. You’ve come to the right place!”

Although the old beggar was dressed in rags and looked like a beggar from head to toe, a treasure pavilion run by immortal cultivators would certainly not discriminate against people by appearance, or at least not when it came to Ji Yuan and the old beggar, as these two were clearly quite powerful.

As for the old beggar’s tattered clothes, there was nothing to be surprised about. There were plenty of eccentric people among cultivators, and some even studied the art of transformation, turning themselves into flowers, trees, mountains, rocks, and clouds. It was quite normal for them to be dressed in rags.

Ji Yuan and the old beggar had already seen many places. Unlike when they first arrived, Ji Yuan still harbored a hint of curiosity, while the old beggar had lost interest.

“Come, come, you two, please follow me and sit down here to rest. I’m going to the third floor to retrieve the earth-element item.”

There was clearly an array on the path upstairs. The scenery changed instantly as soon as they arrived on the second floor, only to find that it was just a reception room-like place with no hidden treasures.

The hall was not large, and it certainly didn’t occupy the entire second floor, based on the size of the building. This was clearly another element of the array’s manipulation; perhaps there were many more rooms on the second floor.

The cultivator from Lingbao Pavilion went up to the third floor and came down shortly afterward, carrying a wooden box in his hand. He quickly walked to Ji Yuan and the old beggar, placed the wooden box on the table, and opened it.

“Please take a look, fellow Taoists.”

Ji Yuan knew what it was at a glance; he had seen it before. But the old beggar spoke faster than he could.

“A Mountain God Stone? Sigh…”

There were two items in the wooden box. The most conspicuous one was a fist-sized blackish-yellow stone, and underneath the stone was a layer of yellow soil.

As he spoke, the old beggar simply shook his head. These were just ordinary items. Although the Mountain God Stone was rare, could it compare to things of the same caliber as the True Fire of Samadhi, the Glazed Water Golden Scales, or Heavenly Tribulation Lightning?

Ji Yuan was somewhat amused and exasperated. In truth, he thought the Mountain God Stone was already good enough, but considering that he wanted to refine a powerful spiritual artifact, he was determined to be as meticulous as possible, and the old beggar had made him a bit picky.

“Alas, fellow Taoist, you are mistaken! This object is not the Mountain God Stone, but the Mountain God Jade!”

“Mountain God Jade?”

Ji Yuan asked curiously, as he had never heard of the term before. The old beggar next to him was also slightly taken aback and turned his attention back to the wooden box.

The assistant nodded and answered with his actions. He took out a the fist-sized blackish-yellow stone from the box, chanting incantations as he channeled the spiritual energy of the room into the stone, and even infused it with some of his own spiritual power.

As the steward moved, the soil in the wooden box began to tremble, and soon grains of soil flew into the steward’s palm. About three breaths later, all the soil in the wooden box flew up and hovered around his right hand, forming a hollow powder sphere made of yellow earth.

“Watch closely, you two!”

Suddenly, the soil in the air began to change shape, sometimes gathering into strips, sometimes coalescing in one place, sometimes transforming into the form of grass and trees, and sometimes spinning endlessly.

“Legend has it that the Mountain God Jade was the heart of a highly cultivated earth spirit. When it passed away, all its cultivation returned to the earth, leaving only its heart! Fellow Taoists, is this item worthy of your attention?”

The cultivator just now didn’t use any earth-manipulation techniques; he controlled the soil’s changes entirely with the stone in his hand. This was beyond the capabilities of even a Mountain God Stone. It was likely that what the assistant held was truly a rare and precious Mountain God Jade.

The old beggar looked at Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, the Mountain God Jade should be barely enough to supplement the Earth spirit of the Five Elements, right?”

Ji Yuan also thought it was very suitable, so he quickly replied, “it’s feasible.”

“By the way, I wonder whether Lingbao Pavilion would be willing to relinquish this Mountain God Jade?”

“Haha, although we are cultivators, since we have opened this Lingbao Pavilion, we are naturally bound to be influenced by the ways of commerce. We certainly don’t want to lose money in this business. You two are both highly skilled, so I’m sure you won’t make things difficult for me…”

After exchanging a lot of pleasantries, the manager cultivator finally got to the point.

“Well then, if you two fellow Taoists have any treasures, please feel free to bring them out so that I may take a look. Even if I am not good at judging, there are other fellow Taoists in the pavilion who can appraise them. If you do not have any treasures or are unwilling to part with them, you can also use a certain weight of spirit stones of the five elements.”

Ji Yuan nodded, without moving his mouth, but he had already sent a telepathic message to the old beggar.

“Mr. Lu, could you perhaps lend me some of the five elements spirit stones, or do you have anything suitable for bartering?”

The old beggar stared at Ji Yuan and replied telepathically.

“Mr. Ji, I’m a beggar. You want to borrow from me? You don’t actually lack money, do you?”

The old beggar believed that Ji Yuan didn’t have much mortal gold and silver, but surely he could produce some hard currency from the cultivation world?

‘Does this mean I need to use the spiritual coins again? Or should I go back to the Nine Peaks Sacred Cavern and borrow from the people of Yuhuai Mountain?’

It was fine to exchange spiritual coins for some insignificant things, but the Mountain God Jade was extremely precious now. Although refining spiritual coins was troublesome, it was a completely cost-free business for Ji Yuan. Wasn’t that a bit inappropriate?

However, Ji Yuan still tentatively took out the spiritual coins. Business was about mutual consent. If something was useful to others, it was valuable. There was no need to feel too guilty.

“I refined this item myself. Its spiritual power is quite pure, and it can be incorporated into any technique at will. Fellow Taoist, would you like to accept it?”

The coins looked excellent, and as soon as Ji Yuan took it out, it attracted the attention of the old beggar and the cultivator from Lingbao Pavilion.

“Fellow Taoist, may I examine this more closely?”

Ji Yuan generously handed out a dozen, while the Lingbao Pavilion cultivator carefully accepted them and examined them closely in his hand.

“Fellow Taoist, there’s no need to be nervous. You can try using one.”

“Use one? Well… then I’ll gladly accept your offer!”

The Lingbao Pavilion cultivator first returned the excess spiritual coins to Ji Yuan, keeping only one in his hand. Then, without relying on anything else, he held the Mountain God Jade in one hand and the spiritual coin in the other. This time, he directly used the Mountain God Jade to perform the control the earth, but did not use his own spiritual energy. Instead, he used the spiritual coin with a thought.

Swish swish swish…

The surrounding yellow soil rapidly gathered and condensed in front of the Mountain God Jade, shrinking to its maximum limit in a short period of time.

The cultivator cut off his spiritual power, and the spiritual coin in his hand dimmed slightly. He then flicked his hand at the floating soil.

With a crisp “ding,” a piece of metal was revealed as the soil crumbled.

“Condensing earth to produce gold! I actually pulled it off! I finally succeeded? No, it’s not me who succeeded, it’s this!”

The cultivator looked at the spiritual coin in his hand. After only a brief attempt, he understood its wonderful uses. Indeed, it could transform into all kinds of energy at will.

“Fellow Taoist, how many Wish Coins3 do you have? If…”

The cultivator gritted his teeth and looked at the dozen or so in Ji Yuan’s hand.

“If you have a hundred of these, I will sell the Mountain God Jade to you, fellow Taoist!”

Ji Yuan breathed a sigh of relief. These things were for emergencies. Although he couldn’t refine too many, he had several hundred on hand.

“Alright, a hundred it is! The name ‘Wish Coin’ sounds better than ‘spiritual coins’.”

Seeing Ji Yuan’s smile, the assistant knew he had underestimated the amount. He felt slightly annoyed but not too guilty, since in terms of value, he felt the deal was worthwhile.

T/n: FRIENDS!!!! IT’S CHAPTER 500!!!!!!!! WOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!! To celebrate this momentous occasion, here’s a picture of my domesticated pom pom:

“Three Flowers” comes from “Three Flowers Gather at the Crown,” which is a very common concept in cultivation novels upon gaining enlightenment, where three illusory flowers manifest above the individual’s head. Each flower represents a different vital energy – Essence, Qi, and Spirit.

灵 (líng): spirit, 宝 (bǎo): treaure, 轩 (xuān): pavilion

如意 (rúyì): according to one’s wishes, 钱 (qián): coin.
